本周具体代码任务
Stage 1：文档分块

python
# 安装依赖
pip install nltk sentence-transformers keybert

from nltk.tokenize import TextTilingTokenizer
import re

class AdaptiveChunker:
    
    def __init__(self, max_tokens=800):
        self.max_tokens = max_tokens
        self.tokenizer = TextTilingTokenizer()
    
    def chunk_document(self, text):
        """
        用TextTiling算法分块
        保留语义边界，不强制固定长度
        """
        # TextTiling按语义主题分块
        try:
            tiles = self.tokenizer.tokenize(text)
        except Exception:
            # 如果TextTiling失败，退回到段落分块
            tiles = text.split('\n\n')
        
        # 确保每块不超过800 tokens
        chunks = []
        for tile in tiles:
            words = tile.split()
            if len(words) <= self.max_tokens:
                chunks.append(tile)
            else:
                # 超长则按句子切分
                sentences = tile.split('. ')
                current_chunk = []
                current_len = 0
                
                for sent in sentences:
                    sent_len = len(sent.split())
                    if current_len + sent_len > self.max_tokens:
                        if current_chunk:
                            chunks.append('. '.join(current_chunk))
                        current_chunk = [sent]
                        current_len = sent_len
                    else:
                        current_chunk.append(sent)
                        current_len += sent_len
                
                if current_chunk:
                    chunks.append('. '.join(current_chunk))
        
        return chunks

# 测试
chunker = AdaptiveChunker(max_tokens=800)
with open("ml_slides.pdf", "rb") as f:
    text = extract_text_from_pdf(f)  # 用pdfplumber
    
chunks = chunker.chunk_document(text)
print(f"生成了 {len(chunks)} 个语义块")
Stage 2：实体关系提取

python
from openai import OpenAI
from keybert import KeyBERT
from sentence_transformers import SentenceTransformer
import numpy as np

client = OpenAI()
kw_model = KeyBERT()
sbert = SentenceTransformer('paraphrase-multilingual-MiniLM-L12-v2')

class KGBuilder:
    
    def extract_topics(self, chunk):
        """Stage 2a: GPT-4o提取Topic和Sub-Topic"""
        
        prompt = f"""
        从以下课程材料中提取知识结构。
        
        材料内容：
        {chunk}
        
        提取规则：
        - Topic：抽象概念，一节课的大方向（如"梯度下降"）
        - Sub-Topic：具体内容（如"学习率的选择"）
        - 每个Topic包含1-N个Sub-Topic
        
        输出JSON格式：
        {{
          "topics": [
            {{
              "name": "...",
              "description": "...",
              "sub_topics": [
                {{"name": "...", "description": "..."}}
              ]
            }}
          ]
        }}
        """
        
        response = client.chat.completions.create(
            model="gpt-4o",
            messages=[{"role": "user", "content": prompt}],
            response_format={"type": "json_object"}
        )
        
        return json.loads(response.choices[0].message.content)
    
    def extract_prerequisites(self, topics):
        """Stage 2b: GPT-4o推断先修关系"""
        
        topic_names = [t['name'] for t in topics]
        
        prompt = f"""
        以下是课程的知识点列表：
        {topic_names}
        
        请判断哪些知识点之间存在先修关系。
        先修关系定义：学习B之前必须先掌握A。
        
        只输出强依赖关系，不要输出弱相关。
        
        输出JSON：
        {{
          "prerequisites": [
            {{
              "from": "知识点A",
              "to": "知识点B",
              "reason": "原因说明"
            }}
          ]
        }}
        """
        
        response = client.chat.completions.create(
            model="gpt-4o",
            messages=[{"role": "user", "content": prompt}],
            response_format={"type": "json_object"}
        )
        
        return json.loads(response.choices[0].message.content)
    
    def compute_similarity_edges(self, topics, threshold=0.75):
        """Stage 2c: Sentence-BERT计算相似关系"""
        
        # 向量化所有Topic
        embeddings = {}
        for topic in topics:
            text = f"{topic['name']}: {topic['description']}"
            embeddings[topic['name']] = sbert.encode(text)
        
        # 计算余弦相似度
        similarity_edges = []
        topic_names = list(embeddings.keys())
        
        for i in range(len(topic_names)):
            for j in range(i+1, len(topic_names)):
                a, b = topic_names[i], topic_names[j]
                
                cos_sim = np.dot(embeddings[a], embeddings[b]) / (
                    np.linalg.norm(embeddings[a]) * 
                    np.linalg.norm(embeddings[b])
                )
                
                if cos_sim > threshold:
                    similarity_edges.append({
                        "from": a,
                        "to": b,
                        "similarity": float(cos_sim)
                    })
        
        return similarity_edges
Stage 3：节点摘要生成

python
def generate_node_summary(self, topic):
    """Stage 3: 为每个节点生成结构化摘要"""
    
    prompt = f"""
    为以下知识概念生成结构化摘要：
    
    知识点名称：{topic['name']}
    知识点描述：{topic['description']}
    
    输出JSON格式：
    {{
      "difficulty_level": 3,        // 1-5分
      "target_audience": "本科生",
      "estimated_learning_time": "2小时",
      "prerequisites_summary": "需要了解...",
      "key_sub_concepts": ["概念1", "概念2"],
      "common_misconceptions": ["误解1", "误解2"],
      "practical_applications": ["应用1", "应用2"]
    }}
    """
    
    response = client.chat.completions.create(
        model="gpt-4o",
        messages=[{"role": "user", "content": prompt}],
        response_format={"type": "json_object"}
    )
    
    return json.loads(response.choices[0].message.content)
完整Pipeline运行

python
def build_knowledge_graph(pdf_path):
    """
    完整的KG构建Pipeline
    输入：PDF文件路径
    输出：知识图谱（节点+边）
    """
    
    # Step 1: 提取文本
    import pdfplumber
    with pdfplumber.open(pdf_path) as pdf:
        text = "\n\n".join([
            page.extract_text() 
            for page in pdf.pages 
            if page.extract_text()
        ])
    
    # Step 2: 分块
    chunker = AdaptiveChunker(max_tokens=800)
    chunks = chunker.chunk_document(text)
    print(f"✅ Step 1 完成：{len(chunks)} 个语义块")
    
    # Step 3: 提取实体
    builder = KGBuilder()
    all_topics = []
    
    for i, chunk in enumerate(chunks):
        result = builder.extract_topics(chunk)
        all_topics.extend(result.get('topics', []))
        print(f"  处理块 {i+1}/{len(chunks)}...")
    
    # 去重
    seen = set()
    unique_topics = []
    for t in all_topics:
        if t['name'] not in seen:
            seen.add(t['name'])
            unique_topics.append(t)
    
    print(f"✅ Step 2a 完成：{len(unique_topics)} 个知识点")
    
    # Step 4: 提取先修关系
    prereq_result = builder.extract_prerequisites(unique_topics)
    prerequisites = prereq_result.get('prerequisites', [])
    print(f"✅ Step 2b 完成：{len(prerequisites)} 条先修关系")
    
    # Step 5: 计算相似关系
    similarity_edges = builder.compute_similarity_edges(unique_topics)
    print(f"✅ Step 2c 完成：{len(similarity_edges)} 条相似关系")
    
    # Step 6: 生成节点摘要
    for topic in unique_topics:
        topic['summary'] = builder.generate_node_summary(topic)
    print(f"✅ Step 3 完成：节点摘要生成完毕")
    
    # Step 7: 存储为NetworkX图
    import networkx as nx
    G = nx.DiGraph()
    
    for topic in unique_topics:
        G.add_node(topic['name'], 
                   description=topic['description'],
                   summary=topic['summary'])
    
    for rel in prerequisites:
        G.add_edge(rel['from'], rel['to'],
                   relation='prerequisite',
                   reason=rel['reason'])
    
    for edge in similarity_edges:
        G.add_edge(edge['from'], edge['to'],
                   relation='similarity',
                   score=edge['similarity'])
    
    print(f"\n🎉 知识图谱构建完成！")
    print(f"   节点数：{G.number_of_nodes()}")
    print(f"   边数：{G.number_of_edges()}")
    
    return G, unique_topics

# 运行！
G, topics = build_knowledge_graph("ml_slides.pdf")
✅ 本周验收标准
本周结束时，你应该能展示：

□ 输入：一份ML相关PDF（导师slides或教材）
□ 输出1：N个语义块（TextTiling分块）
□ 输出2：知识点列表（Topic + Sub-Topic）
□ 输出3：先修关系图（可视化）
□ 输出4：相似关系图（可视化）
□ 输出5：每个节点的结构化摘要

验证方式：
  看生成的先修关系是否合理
  例："线性代数"应该是"梯度下降"的先修
  例："梯度下降"和"坐标下降"应该有相似关系
💰 LLM成本估算
导师提到的预算：约£100-200

GPT-4o的价格（2025年）：
  输入：$2.50 / 1M tokens
  输出：$10.00 / 1M tokens

一本ML教材（约500页）的处理成本估算：
  文档分块：免费（TextTiling是本地算法）
  实体提取：约500个块 × 1000 tokens = 50万tokens
            → 约$1.25（输入）+ $0.50（输出）≈ $2
  节点摘要：约100个节点 × 500 tokens = 5万tokens
            → 约$0.50
  
  总计：约$3-5 ≈ £2-4 处理一本书

结论：
  £100的预算可以处理约25-50本书
  完全够用！
  
  建议：先用GPT-4o-mini测试（更便宜）
        确认输出质量后再切换到GPT-4o

第二个问题：TextTiling的原理是什么？
先理解问题：为什么不直接按字数切？
错误的切法（固定800字）：

"...梯度下降是一种优化算法，它通过计算
损失函数的梯度来更新参数。学习率控制每
次更新的步长。如果学习率太大——"
                    ↑ 在这里强制切断！
"——会导致震荡，如果太小会收敛很慢。
反向传播算法用于计算梯度..."

问题：
  ❌ 一个完整的概念被切成两半
  ❌ 每个块的语义不完整
  ❌ 后续提取知识点时会出错
正确的切法（TextTiling）：

块1：
"梯度下降是一种优化算法，它通过计算
损失函数的梯度来更新参数。学习率控制
每次更新的步长。如果学习率太大会导致
震荡，如果太小会收敛很慢。"
        ↑ 在话题切换处自然断开

块2：
"反向传播算法用于计算梯度。它从输出层
开始，利用链式法则逐层向后传播误差..."

优点：
  ✅ 每个块是一个完整的语义单元
  ✅ 话题在块内部保持连贯
TextTiling的工作原理（三步）
第一步：把文本变成词汇向量
原始文本（按句子分组，每组叫一个"伪句子"）：

伪句子1：梯度下降 优化 损失函数 参数 更新
伪句子2：学习率 步长 收敛 震荡
伪句子3：反向传播 梯度 链式法则 误差
伪句子4：神经网络 层 权重 激活函数

每个伪句子变成一个词袋向量：
  伪句子1 = {梯度:1, 下降:1, 优化:1, 损失:1, ...}
  伪句子2 = {学习率:1, 步长:1, 收敛:1, ...}
  伪句子3 = {反向:1, 传播:1, 梯度:1, 链式:1, ...}
第二步：用滑动窗口计算相邻区域的相似度
窗口大小 = k个伪句子（比如k=2）

在每个"间隙"处，比较左边k个和右边k个的相似度：

间隙1（伪句子1和2之间）：
  左窗口 = {梯度, 下降, 优化, 损失, 参数}
  右窗口 = {学习率, 步长, 收敛, 震荡}
  相似度 = 0.6（有一些共同词汇：梯度相关）

间隙2（伪句子2和3之间）：
  左窗口 = {学习率, 步长, 收敛, 震荡}
  右窗口 = {反向, 传播, 梯度, 链式, 误差}
  相似度 = 0.1（几乎没有共同词汇！）
                ↑
                这里相似度骤降 = 话题切换了！

间隙3（伪句子3和4之间）：
  左窗口 = {反向, 传播, 梯度, 链式}
  右窗口 = {神经网络, 层, 权重, 激活}
  相似度 = 0.4（有关联但不完全一样）
第三步：找相似度的"谷底"= 切割点
相似度曲线：

1.0 │
0.8 │    ╭──╮
0.6 │   ╭╯  ╰╮        ╭──╮
0.4 │  ╭╯    ╰╮      ╭╯  ╰╮
0.2 │ ╭╯      ╰╮    ╭╯
0.0 │╭╯        ╰────╯
    └──────────────────────→ 间隙位置
         间隙1  间隙2  间隙3
                  ↑
               谷底！这里切割
               = 话题从"梯度下降"
                 切换到"反向传播"

最终分块：
  块1：[伪句子1 + 伪句子2]（梯度下降相关）
  块2：[伪句子3 + 伪句子4]（反向传播+神经网络）
用一个超简单的比喻理解
想象你在读一本书，用荧光笔标记关键词：

第1段：梯度、下降、优化、损失、参数（黄色词）
第2段：学习率、步长、收敛（黄色词，和第1段有重叠）
第3段：反向传播、链式法则、误差（完全不同的词！）
第4段：神经网络、权重、激活（又是新词）

TextTiling就是在问：
"相邻两段的荧光笔颜色（词汇）
 重叠度突然变低的地方在哪里？"
→ 那里就是话题切换的地方
→ 那里就是切割点
为什么还要加800 token的上限？
TextTiling的问题：
  有时候一个"话题"太长
  比如整本书都在讲神经网络
  TextTiling可能只切出2-3个大块
  → 每块太大，GPT-4o处理不了

解决方案：双重保险

TextTiling先按语义切
      ↓
如果某块 > 800 tokens
      ↓
再按句子边界二次切分
（保证不超过GPT-4o的上下文限制）

结果：
  既保留了语义完整性（TextTiling）
  又控制了块的大小（800 token上限）
完整的可运行代码（整合版）
把这个存成 build_kg.py，直接运行：

python
import os
import json
import nltk
import numpy as np
import pdfplumber
import networkx as nx
from openai import OpenAI
from keybert import KeyBERT
from sentence_transformers import SentenceTransformer
from nltk.tokenize import TextTilingTokenizer

# 下载NLTK数据
nltk.download('stopwords', quiet=True)
nltk.download('punkt', quiet=True)

# 初始化
client = OpenAI(api_key=os.environ.get("OPENAI_API_KEY"))
kw_model = KeyBERT()
sbert = SentenceTransformer('paraphrase-multilingual-MiniLM-L12-v2')

# ══════════════════════════════════════════
# STAGE 1: 文档分块
# ══════════════════════════════════════════

def extract_text_from_pdf(pdf_path):
    """从PDF提取文本"""
    text = ""
    with pdfplumber.open(pdf_path) as pdf:
        for page in pdf.pages:
            page_text = page.extract_text()
            if page_text:
                text += page_text + "\n\n"
    return text

def chunk_document(text, max_tokens=800):
    """TextTiling分块 + 800 token上限"""
    
    # TextTiling按语义分块
    tiler = TextTilingTokenizer(w=20, k=10)
    try:
        tiles = tiler.tokenize(text)
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"TextTiling失败，使用段落分块: {e}")
        tiles = [p for p in text.split('\n\n') if p.strip()]
    
    # 检查每块是否超过800 tokens
    final_chunks = []
    for tile in tiles:
        words = tile.split()
        if len(words) <= max_tokens:
            final_chunks.append(tile.strip())
        else:
            # 超长则按句子二次切分
            sentences = tile.replace('\n', ' ').split('. ')
            current = []
            current_len = 0
            
            for sent in sentences:
                sent_len = len(sent.split())
                if current_len + sent_len > max_tokens and current:
                    final_chunks.append('. '.join(current).strip())
                    current = [sent]
                    current_len = sent_len
                else:
                    current.append(sent)
                    current_len += sent_len
            
            if current:
                final_chunks.append('. '.join(current).strip())
    
    # 过滤太短的块（少于50词）
    final_chunks = [c for c in final_chunks if len(c.split()) > 50]
    return final_chunks

# ══════════════════════════════════════════
# STAGE 2: 实体和关系提取
# ══════════════════════════════════════════

def extract_topics_from_chunk(chunk):
    """GPT-4o从一个块中提取Topic和Sub-Topic"""
    
    prompt = f"""
    从以下课程材料中提取知识结构。
    
    材料内容：
    {chunk[:2000]}  # 限制长度避免超出context
    
    提取规则：
    - Topic：抽象概念，一节课的大方向（如"梯度下降"）
    - Sub-Topic：具体内容（如"学习率的选择"）
    - 每个Topic包含1-N个Sub-Topic
    - 如果材料中没有明确的知识点，返回空列表
    
    输出JSON格式：
    {{
      "topics": [
        {{
          "name": "知识点名称",
          "description": "一句话描述",
          "sub_topics": [
            {{"name": "子概念名称", "description": "描述"}}
          ]
        }}
      ]
    }}
    """
    
    try:
        response = client.chat.completions.create(
            model="gpt-4o-mini",  # 先用mini省钱
            messages=[{"role": "user", "content": prompt}],
            response_format={"type": "json_object"},
            temperature=0
        )
        return json.loads(response.choices[0].message.content)
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"提取失败: {e}")
        return {"topics": []}

def extract_prerequisites(topics):
    """GPT-4o推断先修关系"""
    
    if len(topics) < 2:
        return {"prerequisites": []}
    
    topic_list = [
        f"- {t['name']}: {t['description']}" 
        for t in topics
    ]
    
    prompt = f"""
    以下是课程的知识点列表：
    {chr(10).join(topic_list)}
    
    请判断哪些知识点之间存在先修关系。
    先修关系定义：学习B之前必须先掌握A。
    只输出强依赖关系（必须先学），不要输出弱相关。
    
    输出JSON：
    {{
      "prerequisites": [
        {{
          "from": "知识点A（先学）",
          "to": "知识点B（后学）",
          "reason": "原因"
        }}
      ]
    }}
    """
    
    try:
        response = client.chat.completions.create(
            model="gpt-4o-mini",
            messages=[{"role": "user", "content": prompt}],
            response_format={"type": "json_object"},
            temperature=0
        )
        return json.loads(response.choices[0].message.content)
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"先修关系提取失败: {e}")
        return {"prerequisites": []}

def compute_similarity_edges(topics, threshold=0.75):
    """Sentence-BERT计算相似关系"""
    
    if len(topics) < 2:
        return []
    
    # 向量化
    embeddings = {}
    for topic in topics:
        text = f"{topic['name']}: {topic['description']}"
        embeddings[topic['name']] = sbert.encode(text)
    
    # 计算余弦相似度
    edges = []
    names = list(embeddings.keys())
    
    for i in range(len(names)):
        for j in range(i+1, len(names)):
            a, b = names[i], names[j]
            vec_a, vec_b = embeddings[a], embeddings[b]
            
            cos_sim = np.dot(vec_a, vec_b) / (
                np.linalg.norm(vec_a) * np.linalg.norm(vec_b)
            )
            
            if cos_sim > threshold:
                edges.append({
                    "from": a, "to": b,
                    "similarity": float(cos_sim)
                })
    
    return edges

# ══════════════════════════════════════════
# STAGE 3: 节点摘要生成
# ══════════════════════════════════════════

def generate_node_summary(topic):
    """为每个节点生成结构化摘要"""
    
    prompt = f"""
    为以下知识概念生成结构化学习摘要：
    
    知识点：{topic['name']}
    描述：{topic['description']}
    
    输出JSON：
    {{
      "difficulty_level": 3,
      "target_audience": "本科生",
      "estimated_learning_time": "2小时",
      "prerequisites_summary": "需要了解...",
      "key_sub_concepts": ["概念1", "概念2"],
      "common_misconceptions": ["误解1"],
      "practical_applications": ["应用1"]
    }}
    """
    
    try:
        response = client.chat.completions.create(
            model="gpt-4o-mini",
            messages=[{"role": "user", "content": prompt}],
            response_format={"type": "json_object"},
            temperature=0
        )
        return json.loads(response.choices[0].message.content)
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"摘要生成失败: {e}")
        return {}

# ══════════════════════════════════════════
# 主函数：完整Pipeline
# ══════════════════════════════════════════

def build_knowledge_graph(pdf_path):
    
    print("=" * 50)
    print("开始构建知识图谱")
    print("=" * 50)
    
    # Stage 1
    print("\n📄 Stage 1: 文档分块...")
    text = extract_text_from_pdf(pdf_path)
    chunks = chunk_document(text, max_tokens=800)
    print(f"✅ 生成 {len(chunks)} 个语义块")
    
    # Stage 2a
    print("\n🔍 Stage 2a: 提取知识点...")
    all_topics = []
    for i, chunk in enumerate(chunks):
        print(f"  处理块 {i+1}/{len(chunks)}...", end="\r")
        result = extract_topics_from_chunk(chunk)
        all_topics.extend(result.get('topics', []))
    
    # 去重
    seen = set()
    unique_topics = []
    for t in all_topics:
        if t['name'] not in seen:
            seen.add(t['name'])
            unique_topics.append(t)
    
    print(f"✅ 提取到 {len(unique_topics)} 个知识点")
    
    # Stage 2b
    print("\n🔗 Stage 2b: 提取先修关系...")
    prereq_result = extract_prerequisites(unique_topics)
    prerequisites = prereq_result.get('prerequisites', [])
    print(f"✅ 发现 {len(prerequisites)} 条先修关系")
    
    # Stage 2c
    print("\n🔄 Stage 2c: 计算相似关系...")
    similarity_edges = compute_similarity_edges(unique_topics)
    print(f"✅ 发现 {len(similarity_edges)} 条相似关系")
    
    # Stage 3
    print("\n📝 Stage 3: 生成节点摘要...")
    for i, topic in enumerate(unique_topics):
        print(f"  处理节点 {i+1}/{len(unique_topics)}...", end="\r")
        topic['summary'] = generate_node_summary(topic)
    print(f"✅ 节点摘要生成完毕")
    
    # 构建NetworkX图
    print("\n🏗️ 构建知识图谱...")
    G = nx.DiGraph()
    
    for topic in unique_topics:
        G.add_node(topic['name'],
                   description=topic['description'],
                   summary=topic.get('summary', {}))
    
    for rel in prerequisites:
        if rel['from'] in G and rel['to'] in G:
            G.add_edge(rel['from'], rel['to'],
                      relation='prerequisite',
                      reason=rel.get('reason', ''))
    
    for edge in similarity_edges:
        if edge['from'] in G and edge['to'] in G:
            G.add_edge(edge['from'], edge['to'],
                      relation='similarity',
                      score=edge['similarity'])
    
    print(f"\n{'='*50}")
    print(f"🎉 知识图谱构建完成！")
    print(f"   节点数：{G.number_of_nodes()}")
    print(f"   边数：  {G.number_of_edges()}")
    print(f"{'='*50}")
    
    # 保存结果
    nx.write_gexf(G, "knowledge_graph.gexf")
    with open("topics.json", "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        json.dump(unique_topics, f, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)
    
    print("\n📁 已保存：")
    print("   knowledge_graph.gexf（可用Gephi可视化）")
    print("   topics.json（知识点详情）")
    
    return G, unique_topics


# 运行！
if __name__ == "__main__":
    # 把你的PDF路径放这里
    PDF_PATH = "your_ml_slides.pdf"
    
    G, topics = build_knowledge_graph(PDF_PATH)
    
    # 打印前5个知识点预览
    print("\n📋 知识点预览（前5个）：")
    for topic in topics[:5]:
        print(f"\n  [{topic['name']}]")
        print(f"  描述：{topic['description'][:50]}...")
        if topic.get('summary'):
            print(f"  难度：{topic['summary'].get('difficulty_level', '?')}/5")
            print(f"  学习时间：{topic['summary'].get('estimated_learning_time', '?')}")
快速开始检查清单

# ## 你问的重点：第一步“文档分块”原理（超简版）

TextTiling 的核心是：

- 把文本按小窗口切成一串片段
- 比较相邻片段的词汇相似度
- **相似度突然下降的地方**，认为是“话题切换点”
- 在切换点断开，得到语义块

所以它比“每 800 词硬切”更好，因为它尽量在**语义边界**切，而不是在句子中间乱切。  
然后你再加一个长度上限（800 tokens）做保险，避免块太大喂不进模型。

下面这个脚本是 **单文件版**，按“先跑通、再优化”的思路写的：  
- 默认用 **$$gpt-4o-mini$$**（省钱）  
- 支持：PDF提取 → 语义分块 → Topic抽取 → 先修关系 → 相似关系 → 节点摘要 → 导出图  
- 带基础容错和进度打印

## **1）先安装依赖**

```bash
pip install openai pdfplumber nltk sentence-transformers networkx numpy matplotlib
python -c "import nltk; nltk.download('stopwords'); nltk.download('punkt')"
```

## **2）设置 API Key**

（重开终端）

- Mac/Linux:
```bash

```

## **3）新建文件：`kg_pipeline_week1.py`**

```python
import os
import re
import json
import time
import numpy as np
import pdfplumber
import networkx as nx
import matplotlib.pyplot as plt

from openai import OpenAI
from nltk.tokenize import TextTilingTokenizer
from sentence_transformers import SentenceTransformer


# ========= 配置区 =========
PDF_PATH = "ml_slides.pdf"          # 你的PDF文件名
MODEL_NAME = "gpt-4o-mini"          # 先用mini省钱
MAX_WORDS_PER_CHUNK = 800           # 块长度上限（近似token）
MIN_WORDS_PER_CHUNK = 50            # 太短块过滤
TOPIC_CHUNK_LIMIT = None            # 调试时可设为3（只跑前3块）
SIM_THRESHOLD = 0.75                # 相似关系阈值
SLEEP_BETWEEN_CALLS = 0.3           # API调用间隔，减少限流风险


# ========= 初始化 =========
client = OpenAI(api_key=os.getenv("OPENAI_API_KEY"))
sbert = SentenceTransformer("paraphrase-multilingual-MiniLM-L12-v2")


def extract_text_from_pdf(pdf_path: str) -> str:
    """从PDF逐页提取文本"""
    if not os.path.exists(pdf_path):
        raise FileNotFoundError(f"找不到PDF文件: {pdf_path}")

    texts = []
    with pdfplumber.open(pdf_path) as pdf:
        for i, page in enumerate(pdf.pages):
            t = page.extract_text()
            if t and t.strip():
                texts.append(t.strip())
    return "\n\n".join(texts)


def safe_sentence_split(text: str):
    """简单句子切分（中英文兼容基础版）"""
    text = text.replace("\n", " ")
    sents = re.split(r'(?<=[。！？.!?])\s+', text)
    return [s.strip() for s in sents if s.strip()]


def chunk_document(text: str, max_words: int = 800, min_words: int = 50):
    """
    Stage 1:
    先TextTiling语义分块，再对超长块做句级二次切分
    """
    tiler = TextTilingTokenizer(w=20, k=10)
    try:
        tiles = tiler.tokenize(text)
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"[Warn] TextTiling失败，回退到段落分块: {e}")
        tiles = [p.strip() for p in text.split("\n\n") if p.strip()]

    chunks = []
    for tile in tiles:
        words = tile.split()
        if len(words) <= max_words:
            chunks.append(tile.strip())
        else:
            # 超长块：句子级切分
            sents = safe_sentence_split(tile)
            cur, cur_len = [], 0
            for s in sents:
                wl = len(s.split())
                if cur and cur_len + wl > max_words:
                    chunks.append(" ".join(cur).strip())
                    cur, cur_len = [s], wl
                else:
                    cur.append(s)
                    cur_len += wl
            if cur:
                chunks.append(" ".join(cur).strip())

    # 过滤太短块
    chunks = [c for c in chunks if len(c.split()) >= min_words]
    return chunks


def call_json_llm(prompt: str, model: str = MODEL_NAME):
    """统一的JSON调用"""
    resp = client.chat.completions.create(
        model=model,
        messages=[{"role": "user", "content": prompt}],
        response_format={"type": "json_object"},
        temperature=0
    )
    content = resp.choices[0].message.content
    return json.loads(content)


def extract_topics_from_chunk(chunk: str):
    """
    Stage 2a: 从单个chunk提取 Topic + Sub-Topic
    """
    prompt = f"""
你是机器学习课程知识图谱助手。请从材料中提取知识结构。

材料：
{chunk[:4000]}

规则：
1) Topic 是相对抽象的知识点（例如：梯度下降）
2) Sub-Topic 是更具体子概念（例如：学习率选择）
3) 如果材料没有明确知识点，返回空列表
4) 严格输出JSON，不要额外文本

输出格式：
{{
  "topics": [
    {{
      "name": "topic名称",
      "description": "一句话描述",
      "sub_topics": [
        {{"name": "sub1", "description": "一句话描述"}}
      ]
    }}
  ]
}}
"""
    try:
        result = call_json_llm(prompt)
        topics = result.get("topics", [])
        if not isinstance(topics, list):
            return {"topics": []}
        return {"topics": topics}
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"[Warn] extract_topics失败: {e}")
        return {"topics": []}


def deduplicate_topics(topics):
    """按topic name去重（保留首个）"""
    seen = set()
    unique = []
    for t in topics:
        name = t.get("name", "").strip()
        if not name or name in seen:
            continue
        seen.add(name)
        # 补齐字段
        t.setdefault("description", "")
        t.setdefault("sub_topics", [])
        unique.append(t)
    return unique


def extract_prerequisites(topics):
    """
    Stage 2b: 推断先修关系
    """
    if len(topics) < 2:
        return {"prerequisites": []}

    topic_lines = "\n".join([f"- {t['name']}: {t.get('description','')}" for t in topics])

    prompt = f"""
下面是机器学习课程知识点列表：
{topic_lines}

任务：找出“强先修关系”。
定义：学习B之前必须先掌握A（A -> B）。
只输出强依赖，不要弱相关。

输出JSON：
{{
  "prerequisites": [
    {{
      "from": "A",
      "to": "B",
      "reason": "简短原因"
    }}
  ]
}}
"""
    try:
        result = call_json_llm(prompt)
        rels = result.get("prerequisites", [])
        if not isinstance(rels, list):
            rels = []
        return {"prerequisites": rels}
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"[Warn] extract_prerequisites失败: {e}")
        return {"prerequisites": []}


def compute_similarity_edges(topics, threshold=0.75):
    """
    Stage 2c: SBERT计算topic相似关系
    """
    if len(topics) < 2:
        return []

    names = [t["name"] for t in topics]
    texts = [f"{t['name']}: {t.get('description','')}" for t in topics]
    embs = sbert.encode(texts, normalize_embeddings=True)

    edges = []
    n = len(names)
    for i in range(n):
        for j in range(i + 1, n):
            sim = float(np.dot(embs[i], embs[j]))  # 已归一化，点积=余弦相似度
            if sim >= threshold:
                edges.append({
                    "from": names[i],
                    "to": names[j],
                    "similarity": sim
                })
    return edges


def generate_node_summary(topic):
    """
    Stage 3: 节点结构化摘要
    """
    prompt = f"""
请为以下机器学习知识点生成结构化学习摘要。

知识点：{topic['name']}
描述：{topic.get('description','')}

输出JSON：
{{
  "difficulty_level": 1,
  "target_audience": "本科生",
  "estimated_learning_time": "2小时",
  "prerequisites_summary": "需要先掌握...",
  "key_sub_concepts": ["概念1", "概念2"],
  "common_misconceptions": ["误解1", "误解2"],
  "practical_applications": ["应用1", "应用2"]
}}

要求：
- difficulty_level范围1-5
- 内容简洁、可执行
"""
    try:
        result = call_json_llm(prompt)
        return result
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"[Warn] generate_node_summary失败({topic['name']}): {e}")
        return {}


def build_graph(topics, prerequisites, similarity_edges):
    """
    构建NetworkX有向图
    """
    G = nx.DiGraph()

    # 节点
    for t in topics:
        G.add_node(
            t["name"],
            description=t.get("description", ""),
            sub_topics=t.get("sub_topics", []),
            summary=t.get("summary", {})
        )

    # 先修边
    for r in prerequisites:
        a, b = r.get("from"), r.get("to")
        if a in G.nodes and b in G.nodes and a != b:
            G.add_edge(a, b, relation="prerequisite", reason=r.get("reason", ""))

    # 相似边（避免覆盖已有先修边）
    for e in similarity_edges:
        a, b = e["from"], e["to"]
        if a in G.nodes and b in G.nodes and a != b:
            if G.has_edge(a, b):
                # 已有边则补充属性
                G[a][b]["similarity"] = e["similarity"]
            else:
                G.add_edge(a, b, relation="similarity", score=e["similarity"])

    return G


def visualize_prereq_graph(G, out_png="prerequisite_graph.png"):
    """
    简单可视化：只画先修关系
    """
    H = nx.DiGraph()
    for u, v, d in G.edges(data=True):
        if d.get("relation") == "prerequisite":
            H.add_edge(u, v)

    if H.number_of_nodes() == 0:
        print("[Info] 没有先修关系可视化。")
        return

    plt.figure(figsize=(12, 8))
    pos = nx.spring_layout(H, seed=42, k=1.2)
    nx.draw_networkx_nodes(H, pos, node_size=1800)
    nx.draw_networkx_edges(H, pos, arrows=True)
    nx.draw_networkx_labels(H, pos, font_size=9)
    plt.title("Prerequisite Graph")
    plt.axis("off")
    plt.tight_layout()
    plt.savefig(out_png, dpi=200)
    plt.close()
    print(f"[Saved] {out_png}")


def visualize_similarity_graph(topics, similarity_edges, out_png="similarity_graph.png"):
    """
    简单可视化：无向相似图
    """
    H = nx.Graph()
    for t in topics:
        H.add_node(t["name"])
    for e in similarity_edges:
        H.add_edge(e["from"], e["to"], weight=e["similarity"])

    if H.number_of_edges() == 0:
        print("[Info] 没有相似关系可视化。")
        return

    plt.figure(figsize=(12, 8))
    pos = nx.spring_layout(H, seed=42, k=1.2)
    widths = [1 + 4 * H[u][v]["weight"] for u, v in H.edges()]
    nx.draw_networkx_nodes(H, pos, node_size=1800)
    nx.draw_networkx_edges(H, pos, width=widths)
    nx.draw_networkx_labels(H, pos, font_size=9)
    plt.title("Similarity Graph")
    plt.axis("off")
    plt.tight_layout()
    plt.savefig(out_png, dpi=200)
    plt.close()
    print(f"[Saved] {out_png}")


def main():
    print("=" * 60)
    print("KG Pipeline Week1 Demo")
    print("=" * 60)

    if not os.getenv("OPENAI_API_KEY"):
        raise EnvironmentError("未检测到 OPENAI_API_KEY，请先设置环境变量。")

    # Step 1: PDF -> text
    print("\n[Stage 1] 提取PDF文本...")
    text = extract_text_from_pdf(PDF_PATH)
    print(f"文本长度（词数估算）: {len(text.split())}")

    # Step 2: chunk
    print("\n[Stage 1] 语义分块...")
    chunks = chunk_document(text, max_words=MAX_WORDS_PER_CHUNK, min_words=MIN_WORDS_PER_CHUNK)
    if TOPIC_CHUNK_LIMIT is not None:
        chunks = chunks[:TOPIC_CHUNK_LIMIT]
    print(f"语义块数量: {len(chunks)}")

    # 保存chunks供验收展示
    with open("chunks_preview.json", "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        json.dump(
            [{"id": i + 1, "words": len(c.split()), "preview": c[:200]} for i, c in enumerate(chunks)],
            f, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2
        )
    print("[Saved] chunks_preview.json")

    # Step 3: extract topics
    print("\n[Stage 2a] 提取Topic/Sub-Topic...")
    all_topics = []
    for i, c in enumerate(chunks, 1):
        print(f"  处理chunk {i}/{len(chunks)}")
        res = extract_topics_from_chunk(c)
        all_topics.extend(res.get("topics", []))
        time.sleep(SLEEP_BETWEEN_CALLS)

    topics = deduplicate_topics(all_topics)
    print(f"去重后Topic数量: {len(topics)}")

    with open("topics_raw.json", "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        json.dump(topics, f, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)
    print("[Saved] topics_raw.json")

    # Step 4: prerequisites
    print("\n[Stage 2b] 提取先修关系...")
    prereq = extract_prerequisites(topics).get("prerequisites", [])
    print(f"先修关系数量: {len(prereq)}")

    with open("prerequisites.json", "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        json.dump(prereq, f, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)
    print("[Saved] prerequisites.json")

    # Step 5: similarity
    print("\n[Stage 2c] 计算相似关系...")
    sim_edges = compute_similarity_edges(topics, threshold=SIM_THRESHOLD)
    print(f"相似关系数量: {len(sim_edges)}")

    with open("similarity_edges.json", "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        json.dump(sim_edges, f, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)
    print("[Saved] similarity_edges.json")

    # Step 6: summaries
    print("\n[Stage 3] 生成节点摘要...")
    for i, t in enumerate(topics, 1):
        print(f"  处理topic {i}/{len(topics)}: {t['name']}")
        t["summary"] = generate_node_summary(t)
        time.sleep(SLEEP_BETWEEN_CALLS)

    with open("topics_with_summary.json", "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        json.dump(topics, f, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)
    print("[Saved] topics_with_summary.json")

    # Step 7: build graph + save
    print("\n[Stage 4] 构建图并导出...")
    G = build_graph(topics, prereq, sim_edges)
    nx.write_gexf(G, "knowledge_graph.gexf")
    print("[Saved] knowledge_graph.gexf")

    # 可视化
    visualize_prereq_graph(G, "prerequisite_graph.png")
    visualize_similarity_graph(topics, sim_edges, "similarity_graph.png")

    print("\n" + "=" * 60)
    print("🎉 完成！验收文件如下：")
    print("- chunks_preview.json")
    print("- topics_raw.json")
    print("- prerequisites.json")
    print("- similarity_edges.json")
    print("- topics_with_summary.json")
    print("- knowledge_graph.gexf")
    print("- prerequisite_graph.png")
    print("- similarity_graph.png")
    print("=" * 60)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```

## **2）把文件内容全删掉，替换成下面这段最小可运行代码**

```python
import os
import pdfplumber
from nltk.tokenize import TextTilingTokenizer

PDF_PATH = "ml_slides.pdf"

def extract_text_from_pdf(pdf_path: str) -> str:
    if not os.path.exists(pdf_path):
        raise FileNotFoundError(f"找不到PDF: {pdf_path}")
    texts = []
    with pdfplumber.open(pdf_path) as pdf:
        for page in pdf.pages:
            t = page.extract_text()
            if t:
                texts.append(t)
    return "\n\n".join(texts)

def chunk_document(text: str, max_words: int = 800):
    tiler = TextTilingTokenizer(w=20, k=10)
    try:
        tiles = tiler.tokenize(text)
    except Exception:
        tiles = [p for p in text.split("\n\n") if p.strip()]

    chunks = []
    for tile in tiles:
        words = tile.split()
        if len(words) <= max_words:
            chunks.append(tile.strip())
        else:
            # 超长按句子切
            sents = tile.replace("\n", " ").split(". ")
            cur, cur_len = [], 0
            for s in sents:
                wl = len(s.split())
                if cur and cur_len + wl > max_words:
                    chunks.append(". ".join(cur).strip())
                    cur, cur_len = [s], wl
                else:
                    cur.append(s)
                    cur_len += wl
            if cur:
                chunks.append(". ".join(cur).strip())
    return [c for c in chunks if len(c.split()) > 50]

if __name__ == "__main__":
    text = extract_text_from_pdf(PDF_PATH)
    chunks = chunk_document(text, max_words=800)
    print(f"文本词数（估算）：{len(text.split())}")
    print(f"语义块数量：{len(chunks)}")
    for i, c in enumerate(chunks[:3], 1):
        print(f"\n--- Chunk {i} preview ---\n{c[:300]}")
```

## ## 常见结果解释

- 输出 `API OK`：环境和trial可用，进入主代码阶段  
- `401`：key错了或没读到环境变量  
- `429`：额度不足/限流（free trial常见）  
- `model not found`：账号当前不可用该模型，换可用模型名测试

## ## 你刚才那条“升级 pip 报错”是什么意思？

它提示你要这样升级：


## ## 给你一个“额度不足时不崩溃”的写法（关键）

```python
from openai import OpenAI
import openai

client = OpenAI()

def safe_llm_call(messages, model="gpt-4o-mini"):
    try:
        resp = client.chat.completions.create(
            model=model,
            messages=messages,
            temperature=0
        )
        return resp.choices[0].message.content, None
    except openai.RateLimitError as e:
        return None, "QUOTA_EXCEEDED"
    except Exception as e:
        return None, str(e)
```

然后你的 pipeline 里判断：

- 如果 `QUOTA_EXCEEDED` → 跳过该步骤，写入 placeholder，流程继续

我们走一个“**无API离线版**”流程：

## ## 给你一份可直接运行的离线脚本

新建：`kg_pipeline_offline.py`

```python
import os
import re
import json
import numpy as np
import pdfplumber
import networkx as nx
import matplotlib.pyplot as plt

from nltk.tokenize import TextTilingTokenizer
from sentence_transformers import SentenceTransformer
from keybert import KeyBERT


# ========= 配置 =========
PDF_PATH = "ml_slides.pdf"
MAX_WORDS_PER_CHUNK = 800
MIN_WORDS_PER_CHUNK = 40
TOP_N_KEYWORDS_PER_CHUNK = 8
SIM_THRESHOLD = 0.72

# 先修规则关键词（可自行扩展）
PREREQ_RULES = [
    ("线性代数", "梯度下降", "梯度与向量运算依赖线性代数基础"),
    ("微积分", "梯度下降", "梯度与导数概念来自微积分"),
    ("概率论", "贝叶斯", "贝叶斯方法依赖概率论"),
    ("统计学", "损失函数", "损失与估计思想依赖统计学基础"),
    ("梯度下降", "反向传播", "反向传播常与梯度优化联合理解"),
    ("线性回归", "逻辑回归", "逻辑回归可视作广义线性模型扩展"),
    ("过拟合", "正则化", "正则化用于缓解过拟合"),
]

# ========= 模型 =========
sbert = SentenceTransformer("paraphrase-multilingual-MiniLM-L12-v2")
kw_model = KeyBERT(model=sbert)  # 直接复用句向量模型


def extract_text_from_pdf(pdf_path: str) -> str:
    if not os.path.exists(pdf_path):
        raise FileNotFoundError(f"找不到PDF: {pdf_path}")

    texts = []
    with pdfplumber.open(pdf_path) as pdf:
        for page in pdf.pages:
            t = page.extract_text()
            if t and t.strip():
                texts.append(t.strip())
    return "\n\n".join(texts)


def safe_sentence_split(text: str):
    text = text.replace("\n", " ")
    sents = re.split(r"(?<=[。！？.!?])\s+", text)
    return [s.strip() for s in sents if s.strip()]


def chunk_document(text: str, max_words=800, min_words=40):
    tiler = TextTilingTokenizer(w=20, k=10)
    try:
        tiles = tiler.tokenize(text)
    except Exception:
        tiles = [p.strip() for p in text.split("\n\n") if p.strip()]

    chunks = []
    for tile in tiles:
        words = tile.split()
        if len(words) <= max_words:
            chunks.append(tile.strip())
        else:
            sents = safe_sentence_split(tile)
            cur, cur_len = [], 0
            for s in sents:
                wl = len(s.split())
                if cur and cur_len + wl > max_words:
                    chunks.append(" ".join(cur).strip())
                    cur, cur_len = [s], wl
                else:
                    cur.append(s)
                    cur_len += wl
            if cur:
                chunks.append(" ".join(cur).strip())

    chunks = [c for c in chunks if len(c.split()) >= min_words]
    return chunks


def extract_topics_offline(chunks, top_n=8):
    """
    用 KeyBERT 从每个 chunk 提取关键词，当作 topic 候选
    """
    candidate_topics = []
    for i, c in enumerate(chunks, 1):
        kws = kw_model.extract_keywords(
            c,
            keyphrase_ngram_range=(1, 3),
            stop_words=None,
            top_n=top_n
        )
        # kws: List[(keyword, score)]
        for kw, score in kws:
            name = kw.strip()
            if len(name) < 2:
                continue
            candidate_topics.append({
                "name": name,
                "description": f"由第{i}个语义块自动提取，关键词相关度{score:.2f}",
                "sub_topics": []
            })

    # 按名称去重
    uniq = {}
    for t in candidate_topics:
        if t["name"] not in uniq:
            uniq[t["name"]] = t
    topics = list(uniq.values())

    return topics


def infer_prerequisites_by_rules(topics):
    """
    规则推断先修关系
    """
    names = set([t["name"] for t in topics])
    rels = []
    for a, b, reason in PREREQ_RULES:
        if a in names and b in names:
            rels.append({"from": a, "to": b, "reason": reason})
    return rels


def compute_similarity_edges(topics, threshold=0.72):
    if len(topics) < 2:
        return []

    names = [t["name"] for t in topics]
    texts = [f"{t['name']}. {t.get('description','')}" for t in topics]
    embs = sbert.encode(texts, normalize_embeddings=True)

    edges = []
    n = len(names)
    for i in range(n):
        for j in range(i + 1, n):
            sim = float(np.dot(embs[i], embs[j]))
            if sim >= threshold:
                edges.append({"from": names[i], "to": names[j], "similarity": sim})
    return edges


def generate_template_summary(topic_name):
    """
    无LLM时的模板摘要
    """
    # 粗略难度规则
    hard_keywords = ["优化", "反向传播", "贝叶斯", "泛化", "凸优化", "拉格朗日", "SVM"]
    diff = 2
    if any(k in topic_name for k in hard_keywords):
        diff = 4

    return {
        "difficulty_level": diff,
        "target_audience": "本科生",
        "estimated_learning_time": "1-2小时",
        "prerequisites_summary": "建议先掌握基础数学与机器学习入门概念",
        "key_sub_concepts": [],
        "common_misconceptions": ["只记结论不理解适用条件"],
        "practical_applications": ["课程作业实现", "模型调参与误差分析"]
    }


def build_graph(topics, prereq_edges, sim_edges):
    G = nx.DiGraph()

    for t in topics:
        if "summary" not in t:
            t["summary"] = generate_template_summary(t["name"])
        G.add_node(
            t["name"],
            description=t.get("description", ""),
            summary=t.get("summary", {}),
            sub_topics=t.get("sub_topics", [])
        )

    for r in prereq_edges:
        a, b = r["from"], r["to"]
        if a in G.nodes and b in G.nodes and a != b:
            G.add_edge(a, b, relation="prerequisite", reason=r.get("reason", ""))

    for e in sim_edges:
        a, b = e["from"], e["to"]
        if a in G.nodes and b in G.nodes and a != b:
            if not G.has_edge(a, b):
                G.add_edge(a, b, relation="similarity", score=e["similarity"])
            else:
                G[a][b]["similarity"] = e["similarity"]

    return G


def visualize_prereq_graph(G, out_png="prerequisite_graph_offline.png"):
    H = nx.DiGraph()
    for u, v, d in G.edges(data=True):
        if d.get("relation") == "prerequisite":
            H.add_edge(u, v)

    if H.number_of_nodes() == 0:
        print("[Info] 无先修关系可画图。")
        return

    plt.figure(figsize=(12, 8))
    pos = nx.spring_layout(H, seed=42, k=1.2)
    nx.draw_networkx_nodes(H, pos, node_size=1800)
    nx.draw_networkx_edges(H, pos, arrows=True)
    nx.draw_networkx_labels(H, pos, font_size=9)
    plt.title("Prerequisite Graph (Offline)")
    plt.axis("off")
    plt.tight_layout()
    plt.savefig(out_png, dpi=200)
    plt.close()
    print(f"[Saved] {out_png}")


def visualize_similarity_graph(topics, sim_edges, out_png="similarity_graph_offline.png"):
    H = nx.Graph()
    for t in topics:
        H.add_node(t["name"])
    for e in sim_edges:
        H.add_edge(e["from"], e["to"], weight=e["similarity"])

    if H.number_of_edges() == 0:
        print("[Info] 无相似关系可画图。")
        return

    plt.figure(figsize=(12, 8))
    pos = nx.spring_layout(H, seed=42, k=1.2)
    widths = [1 + 4 * H[u][v]["weight"] for u, v in H.edges()]
    nx.draw_networkx_nodes(H, pos, node_size=1800)
    nx.draw_networkx_edges(H, pos, width=widths)
    nx.draw_networkx_labels(H, pos, font_size=9)
    plt.title("Similarity Graph (Offline)")
    plt.axis("off")
    plt.tight_layout()
    plt.savefig(out_png, dpi=200)
    plt.close()
    print(f"[Saved] {out_png}")


def main():
    print("=== Offline KG Pipeline ===")

    # 1) PDF -> text
    text = extract_text_from_pdf(PDF_PATH)
    print(f"[1] 文本词数估算: {len(text.split())}")

    # 2) chunk
    chunks = chunk_document(text, max_words=MAX_WORDS_PER_CHUNK, min_words=MIN_WORDS_PER_CHUNK)
    print(f"[2] 语义块数量: {len(chunks)}")

    with open("chunks_preview_offline.json", "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        json.dump(
            [{"id": i + 1, "words": len(c.split()), "preview": c[:220]} for i, c in enumerate(chunks)],
            f, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2
        )
    print("[Saved] chunks_preview_offline.json")

    # 3) topics (offline)
    topics = extract_topics_offline(chunks, top_n=TOP_N_KEYWORDS_PER_CHUNK)
    print(f"[3] 提取Topic数量: {len(topics)}")
    with open("topics_offline.json", "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        json.dump(topics, f, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)
    print("[Saved] topics_offline.json")

    # 4) prerequisites (rules)
    prereq = infer_prerequisites_by_rules(topics)
    print(f"[4] 先修关系数量: {len(prereq)}")
    with open("prerequisites_offline.json", "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        json.dump(prereq, f, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)
    print("[Saved] prerequisites_offline.json")

    # 5) similarity (sbert)
    sim_edges = compute_similarity_edges(topics, threshold=SIM_THRESHOLD)
    print(f"[5] 相似关系数量: {len(sim_edges)}")
    with open("similarity_edges_offline.json", "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        json.dump(sim_edges, f, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)
    print("[Saved] similarity_edges_offline.json")

    # 6) summary template
    for t in topics:
        t["summary"] = generate_template_summary(t["name"])
    with open("topics_with_summary_offline.json", "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        json.dump(topics, f, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)
    print("[Saved] topics_with_summary_offline.json")

    # 7) build graph + visualize
    G = build_graph(topics, prereq, sim_edges)
    nx.write_gexf(G, "knowledge_graph_offline.gexf")
    print(f"[7] 图谱节点: {G.number_of_nodes()}, 边: {G.number_of_edges()}")
    print("[Saved] knowledge_graph_offline.gexf")

    visualize_prereq_graph(G)
    visualize_similarity_graph(topics, sim_edges)

    print("\nDone. 你本周可展示离线demo了。")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```

---

## ## 运行命令

```powershell
python "d:/ic/master project/project_code/KG_construction/kg_pipeline_offline.py"
```

---

## ## 你可以对导师这样解释（很专业）

> 当前由于 API 配额限制，我先完成了离线版 pipeline 验证：

## ## Stage 1 目标（本次）

输入：`ml_slides.pdf`  
输出：

- `stage1_text.txt`（提取的完整文本）
- `stage1_chunks.json`（分块结果）
- 终端打印：块数量 + 前3块预览

## ## 1) 新建文件：`stage1_chunking.py`

```python
import os
import re
import json
import pdfplumber
from nltk.tokenize import TextTilingTokenizer

# ========= 配置 =========
PDF_PATH = "ml_slides.pdf"      # 你的PDF文件名（放在同目录）
MAX_WORDS_PER_CHUNK = 800       # 每块词数上限（近似token控制）
MIN_WORDS_PER_CHUNK = 40        # 过滤过短块


def extract_text_from_pdf(pdf_path: str) -> str:
    """
    从PDF逐页提取文本并拼接
    """
    if not os.path.exists(pdf_path):
        raise FileNotFoundError(f"找不到PDF文件: {pdf_path}")

    pages_text = []
    with pdfplumber.open(pdf_path) as pdf:
        for i, page in enumerate(pdf.pages, 1):
            t = page.extract_text()
            if t and t.strip():
                pages_text.append(t.strip())

    if not pages_text:
        raise ValueError("PDF提取不到文本（可能是扫描版PDF）")

    return "\n\n".join(pages_text)


def split_sentences(text: str):
    """
    简单句子切分（中英文基础版）
    """
    text = text.replace("\n", " ")
    sents = re.split(r'(?<=[。！？.!?])\s+', text)
    return [s.strip() for s in sents if s.strip()]


def chunk_with_texttiling(text: str, max_words=800, min_words=40):
    """
    先 TextTiling 语义分块，再对超长块进行句级切分
    """
    tokenizer = TextTilingTokenizer(w=20, k=10)

    try:
        tiles = tokenizer.tokenize(text)
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"[Warn] TextTiling失败，回退段落分块: {e}")
        tiles = [p.strip() for p in text.split("\n\n") if p.strip()]

    final_chunks = []
    for tile in tiles:
        words = tile.split()

        if len(words) <= max_words:
            final_chunks.append(tile.strip())
        else:
            # 超长块 -> 句子级二次切分
            sents = split_sentences(tile)
            current, cur_len = [], 0

            for s in sents:
                wl = len(s.split())
                if current and (cur_len + wl > max_words):
                    final_chunks.append(" ".join(current).strip())
                    current, cur_len = [s], wl
                else:
                    current.append(s)
                    cur_len += wl

            if current:
                final_chunks.append(" ".join(current).strip())

    # 过滤短块
    final_chunks = [c for c in final_chunks if len(c.split()) >= min_words]
    return final_chunks


def main():
    print("=== Stage 1: PDF文本提取 + 语义分块 ===")

    # Step 1: 提取文本
    text = extract_text_from_pdf(PDF_PATH)
    total_words = len(text.split())
    print(f"[1] 文本提取完成，词数估算: {total_words}")

    with open("stage1_text.txt", "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        f.write(text)
    print("[Saved] stage1_text.txt")

    # Step 2: 分块
    chunks = chunk_with_texttiling(
        text,
        max_words=MAX_WORDS_PER_CHUNK,
        min_words=MIN_WORDS_PER_CHUNK
    )
    print(f"[2] 分块完成，语义块数量: {len(chunks)}")

    # 保存分块结果
    data = []
    for i, c in enumerate(chunks, 1):
        data.append({
            "chunk_id": i,
            "word_count": len(c.split()),
            "text": c
        })

    with open("stage1_chunks.json", "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        json.dump(data, f, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)
    print("[Saved] stage1_chunks.json")

    # 预览前3块
    preview_n = min(3, len(chunks))
    print(f"\n--- 前{preview_n}块预览 ---")
    for i in range(preview_n):
        snippet = chunks[i][:250].replace("\n", " ")
        print(f"\n[Chunk {i+1}] words={len(chunks[i].split())}")
        print(snippet + ("..." if len(chunks[i]) > 250 else ""))

    print("\n✅ Stage 1 完成")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```

## ## 新建文件：`stage1_chunking_select_file.py`

```python
import os
import re
import json
import pdfplumber
from nltk.tokenize import TextTilingTokenizer

# ========= 可调参数 =========
MAX_WORDS_PER_CHUNK = 800
MIN_WORDS_PER_CHUNK = 40


def ask_pdf_path() -> str:
    """
    交互式获取PDF路径
    - 允许带引号粘贴
    - 自动去掉首尾空格和引号
    """
    p = input("请输入PDF文件完整路径（例如 D:\\data\\ml_slides.pdf）：\n> ").strip()
    p = p.strip('"').strip("'")  # 去引号
    return p


def extract_text_from_pdf(pdf_path: str) -> str:
    if not os.path.exists(pdf_path):
        raise FileNotFoundError(f"找不到PDF文件: {pdf_path}")

    pages_text = []
    with pdfplumber.open(pdf_path) as pdf:
        for i, page in enumerate(pdf.pages, 1):
            t = page.extract_text()
            if t and t.strip():
                pages_text.append(t.strip())

    if not pages_text:
        raise ValueError("PDF提取不到文本（可能是扫描版PDF）")

    return "\n\n".join(pages_text)


def split_sentences(text: str):
    text = text.replace("\n", " ")
    sents = re.split(r'(?<=[。！？.!?])\s+', text)
    return [s.strip() for s in sents if s.strip()]


def chunk_with_texttiling(text: str, max_words=800, min_words=40):
    tokenizer = TextTilingTokenizer(w=20, k=10)

    try:
        tiles = tokenizer.tokenize(text)
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"[Warn] TextTiling失败，回退到段落分块: {e}")
        tiles = [p.strip() for p in text.split("\n\n") if p.strip()]

    final_chunks = []
    for tile in tiles:
        words = tile.split()

        if len(words) <= max_words:
            final_chunks.append(tile.strip())
        else:
            sents = split_sentences(tile)
            current, cur_len = [], 0

            for s in sents:
                wl = len(s.split())
                if current and (cur_len + wl > max_words):
                    final_chunks.append(" ".join(current).strip())
                    current, cur_len = [s], wl
                else:
                    current.append(s)
                    cur_len += wl

            if current:
                final_chunks.append(" ".join(current).strip())

    final_chunks = [c for c in final_chunks if len(c.split()) >= min_words]
    return final_chunks


def main():
    print("=== Stage 1: 文件选择版（PDF提取 + 语义分块）===")

    pdf_path = ask_pdf_path()
    print(f"\n你选择的文件：{pdf_path}")

    # 输出目录：默认放在脚本当前目录下
    out_dir = os.getcwd()
    print(f"输出目录：{out_dir}")

    # 1) 提取文本
    text = extract_text_from_pdf(pdf_path)
    total_words = len(text.split())
    print(f"[1] 文本提取完成，词数估算: {total_words}")

    text_out = os.path.join(out_dir, "stage1_text.txt")
    with open(text_out, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        f.write(text)
    print(f"[Saved] {text_out}")

    # 2) 分块
    chunks = chunk_with_texttiling(
        text,
        max_words=MAX_WORDS_PER_CHUNK,
        min_words=MIN_WORDS_PER_CHUNK
    )
    print(f"[2] 分块完成，语义块数量: {len(chunks)}")

    chunk_data = []
    for i, c in enumerate(chunks, 1):
        chunk_data.append({
            "chunk_id": i,
            "word_count": len(c.split()),
            "text": c
        })

    chunks_out = os.path.join(out_dir, "stage1_chunks.json")
    with open(chunks_out, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        json.dump(chunk_data, f, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)
    print(f"[Saved] {chunks_out}")

    # 预览
    n = min(3, len(chunks))
    print(f"\n--- 前{n}块预览 ---")
    for i in range(n):
        snippet = chunks[i][:250].replace("\n", " ")
        print(f"\n[Chunk {i+1}] words={len(chunks[i].split())}")
        print(snippet + ("..." if len(chunks[i]) > 250 else ""))

    print("\n✅ Stage 1 完成")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```

[06/07/2026 13:04:27 +00:00] MI, YU:
Stage 2a 完整可运行代码（保持文件选择/路径友好版本）

Response:
# ## Stage 2a（KeyBERT版）完整可运行代码 ✅  
**目标**：从 `stage1_chunks.json` 提取 Topic / Sub-Topic（离线，不用 API）

---

## ## 新建文件：`stage2a_topics_keybert.py`

```python
import os
import re
import json
from keybert import KeyBERT
from sentence_transformers import SentenceTransformer


# ========= 可调参数 =========
TOP_N_PER_CHUNK = 8               # 每个chunk提取关键词数量
NGRAM_RANGE = (1, 3)              # 关键词n-gram范围
MIN_TOPIC_LEN = 2                 # 最短topic字符数
MAX_TOPICS_FINAL = 120            # 最终最多保留多少个topic
SUBTOPIC_TOP_K = 3                # 每个topic最多挂多少子主题
SCORE_THRESHOLD = 0.15            # 关键词最低分阈值（可根据效果调整）


def ask_input_json_path() -> str:
    p = input("请输入 stage1_chunks.json 的完整路径：\n> ").strip().strip('"').strip("'")
    return p


def ask_output_json_path(default_name="stage2a_topics_keybert.json") -> str:
    p = input(f"请输入输出JSON路径（直接回车=当前目录/{default_name}）：\n> ").strip().strip('"').strip("'")
    if not p:
        p = os.path.join(os.getcwd(), default_name)
    return p


def normalize_text(s: str) -> str:
    s = s.strip()
    s = re.sub(r"\s+", " ", s)
    return s


def clean_topic_name(s: str) -> str:
    s = normalize_text(s)
    # 去掉一些明显噪声字符
    s = s.strip(".,;:()[]{}<>|/\\\"'")
    return s


def load_chunks(chunks_json_path: str):
    if not os.path.exists(chunks_json_path):
        raise FileNotFoundError(f"找不到文件: {chunks_json_path}")

    with open(chunks_json_path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        data = json.load(f)

    # 兼容格式：[{chunk_id, text, ...}, ...]
    chunks = []
    for item in data:
        t = item.get("text", "")
        if t and t.strip():
            chunks.append({
                "chunk_id": item.get("chunk_id", len(chunks) + 1),
                "text": t.strip(),
                "word_count": item.get("word_count", len(t.split()))
            })
    return chunks


def build_keybert():
    # 复用多语种句向量模型
    sbert = SentenceTransformer("paraphrase-multilingual-MiniLM-L12-v2")
    kw_model = KeyBERT(model=sbert)
    return kw_model


def extract_candidates_from_chunk(kw_model, chunk_text: str, top_n=8):
    kws = kw_model.extract_keywords(
        chunk_text,
        keyphrase_ngram_range=NGRAM_RANGE,
        stop_words=None,              # 保持中英文兼容
        use_maxsum=True,              # 降低重复关键词
        nr_candidates=30,
        top_n=top_n
    )
    # kws: [(keyword, score), ...]
    cleaned = []
    for kw, sc in kws:
        name = clean_topic_name(kw)
        if len(name) < MIN_TOPIC_LEN:
            continue
        if sc < SCORE_THRESHOLD:
            continue
        cleaned.append((name, float(sc)))
    return cleaned


def aggregate_topics(chunks, kw_model):
    """
    聚合策略：
    - 对每个chunk提关键词
    - 全局按topic名聚合（累计分数、出现次数、来源chunk）
    """
    topic_map = {}

    for ch in chunks:
        chunk_id = ch["chunk_id"]
        text = ch["text"]

        candidates = extract_candidates_from_chunk(
            kw_model=kw_model,
            chunk_text=text,
            top_n=TOP_N_PER_CHUNK
        )

        for name, score in candidates:
            if name not in topic_map:
                topic_map[name] = {
                    "name": name,
                    "score_sum": 0.0,
                    "count": 0,
                    "chunk_ids": set(),
                }
            topic_map[name]["score_sum"] += score
            topic_map[name]["count"] += 1
            topic_map[name]["chunk_ids"].add(chunk_id)

    # 转list并排序
    topics = []
    for _, v in topic_map.items():
        avg_score = v["score_sum"] / max(v["count"], 1)
        topics.append({
            "name": v["name"],
            "avg_score": round(avg_score, 4),
            "frequency": v["count"],
            "chunk_ids": sorted(list(v["chunk_ids"]))
        })

    # 排序：先按频次，再按平均分
    topics.sort(key=lambda x: (x["frequency"], x["avg_score"]), reverse=True)
    return topics


def build_subtopics_simple(topics):
    """
    简单规则：
    - 如果A是B的子串，且A更短，A更可能是topic，B作为sub-topic候选
    - 每个topic最多挂 SUBTOPIC_TOP_K 个
    """
    names = [t["name"] for t in topics]
    name_set = set(names)

    sub_map = {n: [] for n in names}

    for parent in names:
        for child in names:
            if parent == child:
                continue
            # child包含parent，且child更长 => 作为子主题候选
            if parent in child and len(child) > len(parent):
                sub_map[parent].append(child)

    # 去重 + 截断
    for k in sub_map:
        uniq = []
        seen = set()
        for x in sorted(sub_map[k], key=lambda z: len(z)):
            if x not in seen:
                seen.add(x)
                uniq.append(x)
        sub_map[k] = uniq[:SUBTOPIC_TOP_K]

    # 组装最终结构
    final = []
    for t in topics[:MAX_TOPICS_FINAL]:
        name = t["name"]
        sub_topics = [{"name": s, "description": f"{name} 的细化概念"} for s in sub_map.get(name, [])]

        final.append({
            "name": name,
            "description": f"由KeyBERT从课程语义块自动提取（出现{t['frequency']}次，平均相关度{t['avg_score']}）",
            "sub_topics": sub_topics,
            "meta": {
                "avg_score": t["avg_score"],
                "frequency": t["frequency"],
                "chunk_ids": t["chunk_ids"]
            }
        })
    return final


def main():
    print("=== Stage 2a: KeyBERT Topic提取（离线） ===")

    in_path = ask_input_json_path()
    out_path = ask_output_json_path()

    chunks = load_chunks(in_path)
    print(f"[1] 读取chunks成功：{len(chunks)} 个")

    kw_model = build_keybert()
    print("[2] KeyBERT模型加载完成")

    ranked_topics = aggregate_topics(chunks, kw_model)
    print(f"[3] 候选Topic数：{len(ranked_topics)}")

    final_topics = build_subtopics_simple(ranked_topics)
    print(f"[4] 最终Topic数（截断后）：{len(final_topics)}")

    output = {
        "stage": "2a",
        "method": "keybert_offline",
        "input_chunks": len(chunks),
        "topics_count": len(final_topics),
        "topics": final_topics
    }

    os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(out_path), exist_ok=True) if os.path.dirname(out_path) else None
    with open(out_path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        json.dump(output, f, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)

    print(f"[Saved] {out_path}")

    # 预览前10个
    print("\n--- Top 10 Topics Preview ---")
    for i, t in enumerate(final_topics[:10], 1):
        print(f"{i:02d}. {t['name']}  | freq={t['meta']['frequency']} | score={t['meta']['avg_score']}")

    print("\n✅ Stage 2a 完成（离线KeyBERT版）")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```

- `sub_topics`

[06/07/2026 13:06:03 +00:00] MI, YU:
我怎么判断每一步的质量？

Response:
# ## 非常好的问题：你需要“验收标准”而不是只看能不能跑 ✅

给你一个**实用、低成本**的质量评估框架（适合你当前离线+后续GPT对比）。

---

## ## 总原则（每个Stage都看两类指标）

1. **工程指标**：是否稳定、完整、可复现  
2. **语义指标**：结果是否“像人类认为合理”

---

## ## Stage 1（分块）怎么评估？

### **你要看这4项：**

- **块大小是否合理**：大多数块在约 `100~800` 词之间  
- **块内主题是否连贯**：同一块别同时混太多章节  
- **块间边界是否自然**：切点在话题切换处  
- **覆盖率**：提取文本是否接近原PDF主要内容

### **快速打分法（人工抽样）**
随机看 `10` 个 chunk，给每块打：

- 连贯性（1~5）
- 边界自然度（1~5）

取平均。  
如果平均 `< 3`，就调参数（`w/k`、`min_words`、二次切分策略）。

---

## ## Stage 2a（Topic/Sub-topic）怎么评估？

### **核心看3个：**

- **Precision（准确率）**：提取出来的是不是“知识点”而不是噪声词
- **Coverage（覆盖率）**：课程关键概念是否都出现
- **Granularity（粒度）**：不应全是超大词，也不应全是碎词

### **建议做一个“小金标准”**
你自己先手工写一个 `gold_topics`（比如20个课程核心概念）。  
然后对比自动提取结果：

- 命中数 / 20  （近似 recall）
- 明显错误topic占比（近似 error rate）

> 目标（离线KeyBERT阶段）：  
> 命中率先到 `60%+` 就不错；后续可用GPT提升。

---

## ## Stage 2b（先修关系）怎么评估？

### **看这3条：**

- **方向正确**：A->B 有没有反了  
- **强依赖真实性**：是不是“必须先学”  
- **图结构健康**：是否出现大量不合理环路

### **最实用检查**
做一个你自己的“必备先修关系列表”（10条）：

例如：
- 线性代数 -> 梯度下降  
- 微积分 -> 反向传播  
- 概率论 -> 贝叶斯方法

然后算命中率：

$$
\text{HitRate} = \frac{\text{命中的标准关系条数}}{\text{标准关系总数}}
$$

---

## ## Stage 2c（相似关系）怎么评估？

### **看2点：**

- 高相似边是否“语义接近”（人工抽查前20条）
- 阈值变化是否稳定（例如 `0.70/0.75/0.80`）

你可以做个小表：

- 阈值低：边太多，噪声高  
- 阈值高：边太少，漏掉合理关系  

找到一个平衡点（通常 `0.72~0.80`）。

---

## ## Stage 3（节点摘要）怎么评估？

即使模板版也能评：

- 字段完整率（7个字段是否齐全）
- 内容一致性（摘要是否和topic匹配）
- 可用性（是否能辅助学习，不空泛）

可定义：

$$
\text{Completeness} = \frac{\text{非空字段数}}{\text{总字段数}}
$$

---

## ## 你这周最该准备的“验收表”（建议直接做Excel）

列可以这样：

- Stage
- 指标
- 计算方式
- 当前值
- 目标值
- 是否达标
- 后续改进动作

---

## ## 一句话建议

你现在不要追求“绝对准确”，先追求：

- **流程完整可复现**
- **每步有量化指标**
- **能解释误差来源与改进计划**

Response:
# ## 不慌，这种情况**大概率不是卡死**，而是在做 CPU 密集计算 ✅

你日志显示模型已下载并加载完成，到：

`[2] KeyBERT模型加载完成`

下一步是对每个 chunk 做关键词提取，这一步在 Windows + CPU 下可能会“看起来没输出”一段时间。

---

## ## 为什么会“像卡住”

1. **第一次运行要建缓存/加载模型到内存**（你已经经历了大部分）  
2. `KeyBERT + SBERT` 在 CPU 上每个 chunk 都要算向量，相对慢  
3. 你当前脚本在 `aggregate_topics()` 里没有逐块打印进度，所以看起来静止

---

## ## 先判断是不是“真卡死”

等 `2~5` 分钟（你的数据只有 7 chunks，通常能结束）。  
如果 CPU 占用仍明显（任务管理器里 python 有占用），就是在跑，不是死掉。

---

## ## 立刻可做的3个加速/排查动作

## 1）给 Stage2a 加进度打印（最重要）
在 `aggregate_topics` 的 `for ch in chunks:` 里加：

```python
for idx, ch in enumerate(chunks, 1):
    print(f"[KeyBERT] chunk {idx}/{len(chunks)} ...")
    ...
```

这样你能看到它有没有继续前进。

---

## 2）先降参数做 smoke test
把这几个改小：

```python
TOP_N_PER_CHUNK = 5
MAX_TOPICS_FINAL = 60
```

并把 `extract_keywords` 里：

```python
use_maxsum=False
nr_candidates=20
```

先跑通再调回。

---

## 3）单块测试（确认函数正常）
临时加一个 quick test：

```python
test = chunks[0]["text"][:1200]
print(kw_model.extract_keywords(test, keyphrase_ngram_range=(1,2), top_n=5))
```

如果能立刻出结果，说明整体没问题，只是全量慢。

- `HF_TOKEN` 未设置：只影响下载限速，不影响功能  
- `symlink warning`：只影响缓存占用，不影响运行

都不是错误，可忽略。

那我可以用gpu吗？

Response:
# ## 可以，用 GPU 会明显更快（尤其是 SBERT 编码）✅

你这套 Stage2a 里，真正吃算力的是 `sentence-transformers`。  
如果 PyTorch 检测到 CUDA，就能走 GPU。

---

## ## 先判断你当前环境是否识别 GPU

在终端运行：

```powershell
python -c "import torch; print('torch:', torch.__version__); print('cuda_available:', torch.cuda.is_available()); print('cuda_device_count:', torch.cuda.device_count())"
```

如果 `cuda_available: True`，就可以用 GPU。

---

## ## 代码里怎么强制用 GPU

把你 `build_keybert()` 改成这样：

```python
import torch
from keybert import KeyBERT
from sentence_transformers import SentenceTransformer

def build_keybert():
    device = "cuda" if torch.cuda.is_available() else "cpu"
    print(f"[Info] Using device: {device}")
    sbert = SentenceTransformer("paraphrase-multilingual-MiniLM-L12-v2", device=device)
    kw_model = KeyBERT(model=sbert)
    return kw_model
```

---

## ## 如果 `cuda_available=False` 怎么办？

说明你当前 `.venv` 里装的是 CPU 版 torch。  
需要安装 CUDA 版 PyTorch（与你显卡/CUDA版本匹配）。

最常见（示例）：

```powershell
python -m pip uninstall -y torch torchvision torchaudio
python -m pip install torch torchvision torchaudio --index-url https://download.pytorch.org/whl/cu121
```

> 前提：NVIDIA 驱动正常。  
> 不确定版本时，先告诉我你显卡型号，我给你精确命令。

---

## ## 实际预期

- 你这个 7 chunks 的小数据，GPU提速有限但会更流畅  
- 后面 chunks 多了（教材级），GPU价值很大

09. imperialcollegelondon machinelearningfordesignengineers universalapproximationtheorem  | freq=1 | score=0.7025

但**质量确实不理想**（你判断得很对）：topic 变成了很长“粘连句子”。

这通常是 PDF 文本抽取导致空格/换行异常，KeyBERT把整段当短语抽出来。

## ## 先给结论

你现在要做两步优化再跑一次：

1. **先清洗文本**（修复 `linearseparation` 这类粘连）  
2. **限制关键词形态**（只保留短语，不要长句）

## ## 我给你一版“增强版 Stage 2a”关键改动（可直接替换）

把你脚本里这几处改掉：

### **A) 增加清洗函数**

```python
def clean_chunk_text(text: str) -> str:
    # 1) 去多余空白
    text = re.sub(r"\s+", " ", text)

    # 2) 在小写->大写之间补空格（如 linearseparationWe -> linearseparation We）
    text = re.sub(r"([a-z])([A-Z])", r"\1 \2", text)

    # 3) 在字母和数字之间补空格
    text = re.sub(r"([a-zA-Z])(\d)", r"\1 \2", text)
    text = re.sub(r"(\d)([a-zA-Z])", r"\1 \2", text)

    return text.strip()
```

---

### **B) 提取关键词时更严格（防长句）**

把 `extract_candidates_from_chunk` 改成：

```python
def extract_candidates_from_chunk(kw_model, chunk_text: str, top_n=8):
    chunk_text = clean_chunk_text(chunk_text)

    kws = kw_model.extract_keywords(
        chunk_text,
        keyphrase_ngram_range=(1, 2),   # 从(1,3)改为(1,2)
        stop_words="english",           # 英文slides建议加英文停用词
        use_maxsum=True,
        nr_candidates=20,
        top_n=top_n
    )

    cleaned = []
    for kw, sc in kws:
        name = clean_topic_name(kw)

        # 过滤条件：太长短语、包含过多词
        wc = len(name.split())
        if len(name) < 3:
            continue
        if wc > 4:                      # 最多4词
            continue
        if sc < 0.20:                  # 提高阈值
            continue

        # 过滤明显噪声
        bad_tokens = ["imperial", "london", "figure", "week", "machinelearningfordesignengineers"]
        low = name.lower().replace(" ", "")
        if any(b in low for b in bad_tokens):
            continue

        cleaned.append((name, float(sc)))

    return cleaned
```

---

### **C) 参数建议**

```python
TOP_N_PER_CHUNK = 6
SCORE_THRESHOLD = 0.20
MAX_TOPICS_FINAL = 40
```

特点：

- 自动检测 GPU（CUDA）
- 文本清洗（修复粘连）
- 更严格关键词过滤（避免长句垃圾）
- 实时进度打印（不再“像卡住”）
- 输出可直接给后续 Stage 2b 用

## ## 新建文件：`stage2a_topics_keybert_gpu.py`

```python
import os
import re
import json
import torch
from keybert import KeyBERT
from sentence_transformers import SentenceTransformer

# ========= 参数 =========
TOP_N_PER_CHUNK = 6
NGRAM_RANGE = (1, 2)          # 限制到1~2gram，减少长句
MIN_TOPIC_CHAR = 3
MAX_TOPIC_WORDS = 4
MAX_TOPICS_FINAL = 50
SCORE_THRESHOLD = 0.20

BAD_WORDS = {
    "imperial", "london", "figure", "week",
    "machinelearningfordesignengineers",
    "tableof", "content"
}


def ask_input_path():
    p = input("请输入 stage1_chunks.json 完整路径：\n> ").strip().strip('"').strip("'")
    return p


def ask_output_path(default_name="stage2a_topics_keybert_gpu.json"):
    p = input(f"请输入输出JSON路径（回车=当前目录/{default_name}）：\n> ").strip().strip('"').strip("'")
    if not p:
        p = os.path.join(os.getcwd(), default_name)
    return p


def load_chunks(path):
    if not os.path.exists(path):
        raise FileNotFoundError(f"找不到输入文件: {path}")
    with open(path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        data = json.load(f)

    chunks = []
    for item in data:
        text = item.get("text", "")
        if text and text.strip():
            chunks.append({
                "chunk_id": item.get("chunk_id", len(chunks) + 1),
                "text": text.strip(),
                "word_count": item.get("word_count", len(text.split()))
            })
    return chunks


def clean_chunk_text(text: str) -> str:
    # 换行和多空格清理
    text = re.sub(r"\s+", " ", text)

    # 小写->大写处补空格：linearseparationWe -> linearseparation We
    text = re.sub(r"([a-z])([A-Z])", r"\1 \2", text)

    # 字母数字边界补空格
    text = re.sub(r"([a-zA-Z])(\d)", r"\1 \2", text)
    text = re.sub(r"(\d)([a-zA-Z])", r"\1 \2", text)

    # 去掉过多符号
    text = re.sub(r"[|•·■◆►]", " ", text)
    text = re.sub(r"\s+", " ", text)
    return text.strip()


def clean_topic_name(s: str) -> str:
    s = s.strip()
    s = re.sub(r"\s+", " ", s)
    s = s.strip(".,;:()[]{}<>|/\\\"'")
    return s


def is_good_topic(name: str, score: float) -> bool:
    if score < SCORE_THRESHOLD:
        return False
    if len(name) < MIN_TOPIC_CHAR:
        return False

    wc = len(name.split())
    if wc == 0 or wc > MAX_TOPIC_WORDS:
        return False

    low = name.lower()
    low_no_space = low.replace(" ", "")

    # 纯数字/符号过滤
    if re.fullmatch(r"[\d\W_]+", name):
        return False

    # 噪声词过滤
    for b in BAD_WORDS:
        if b in low_no_space:
            return False

    # 太长token（常见粘连垃圾）
    for token in name.split():
        if len(token) > 25:
            return False

    return True


def build_keybert_gpu():
    device = "cuda" if torch.cuda.is_available() else "cpu"
    print(f"[Info] torch={torch.__version__}, cuda_available={torch.cuda.is_available()}")
    if device == "cuda":
        print(f"[Info] Using GPU: {torch.cuda.get_device_name(0)}")
    else:
        print("[Info] Using CPU (未检测到CUDA)")

    sbert = SentenceTransformer("paraphrase-multilingual-MiniLM-L12-v2", device=device)
    kw_model = KeyBERT(model=sbert)
    return kw_model


def extract_candidates(kw_model, chunk_text: str, top_n=6):
    text = clean_chunk_text(chunk_text)

    kws = kw_model.extract_keywords(
        text,
        keyphrase_ngram_range=NGRAM_RANGE,
        stop_words="english",   # 英文slides建议启用
        use_maxsum=True,
        nr_candidates=20,
        top_n=top_n
    )

    out = []
    for kw, sc in kws:
        name = clean_topic_name(kw)
        if is_good_topic(name, float(sc)):
            out.append((name, float(sc)))
    return out


def aggregate_topics(chunks, kw_model):
    topic_map = {}
    total = len(chunks)

    for i, ch in enumerate(chunks, 1):
        print(f"[KeyBERT] chunk {i}/{total} ...")
        cands = extract_candidates(kw_model, ch["text"], TOP_N_PER_CHUNK)

        for name, score in cands:
            if name not in topic_map:
                topic_map[name] = {
                    "name": name,
                    "score_sum": 0.0,
                    "count": 0,
                    "chunk_ids": set()
                }
            topic_map[name]["score_sum"] += score
            topic_map[name]["count"] += 1
            topic_map[name]["chunk_ids"].add(ch["chunk_id"])

    # 排序
    topics = []
    for _, v in topic_map.items():
        avg_score = v["score_sum"] / max(v["count"], 1)
        topics.append({
            "name": v["name"],
            "avg_score": round(avg_score, 4),
            "frequency": v["count"],
            "chunk_ids": sorted(list(v["chunk_ids"]))
        })

    topics.sort(key=lambda x: (x["frequency"], x["avg_score"]), reverse=True)
    return topics


def build_subtopics_simple(ranked_topics):
    names = [t["name"] for t in ranked_topics]
    sub_map = {n: [] for n in names}

    for p in names:
        for c in names:
            if p == c:
                continue
            if p in c and len(c) > len(p):
                sub_map[p].append(c)

    # 每个topic最多2个子主题
    for k in sub_map:
        uniq = []
        seen = set()
        for x in sorted(sub_map[k], key=lambda z: len(z)):
            if x not in seen:
                seen.add(x)
                uniq.append(x)
        sub_map[k] = uniq[:2]

    final_topics = []
    for t in ranked_topics[:MAX_TOPICS_FINAL]:
        name = t["name"]
        subs = [{"name": s, "description": f"{name} 的细化概念"} for s in sub_map.get(name, [])]
        final_topics.append({
            "name": name,
            "description": f"KeyBERT离线提取（出现{t['frequency']}次，平均相关度{t['avg_score']}）",
            "sub_topics": subs,
            "meta": {
                "avg_score": t["avg_score"],
                "frequency": t["frequency"],
                "chunk_ids": t["chunk_ids"]
            }
        })
    return final_topics


def main():
    print("=== Stage 2a 增强版（GPU优先）===")

    in_path = ask_input_path()
    out_path = ask_output_path()

    chunks = load_chunks(in_path)
    print(f"[1] 读取chunks成功：{len(chunks)} 个")

    kw_model = build_keybert_gpu()
    print("[2] KeyBERT模型加载完成")

    ranked = aggregate_topics(chunks, kw_model)
    print(f"[3] 候选Topic数：{len(ranked)}")

    final_topics = build_subtopics_simple(ranked)
    print(f"[4] 最终Topic数：{len(final_topics)}")

    output = {
        "stage": "2a",
        "method": "keybert_offline_gpu_enhanced",
        "input_chunks": len(chunks),
        "topics_count": len(final_topics),
        "topics": final_topics
    }

    out_dir = os.path.dirname(out_path)
    if out_dir:
        os.makedirs(out_dir, exist_ok=True)

    with open(out_path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        json.dump(output, f, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)

    print(f"[Saved] {out_path}")

    print("\n--- Top 15 Topics Preview ---")
    for i, t in enumerate(final_topics[:15], 1):
        print(f"{i:02d}. {t['name']} | freq={t['meta']['frequency']} | score={t['meta']['avg_score']}")

    print("\n✅ Stage 2a 增强版完成")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```

## ## 新建文件：`stage2a_topics_keybert_cpu_opt.py`

```python
import os
import re
import json
import torch
from keybert import KeyBERT
from sentence_transformers import SentenceTransformer

# ========= CPU优化参数 =========
TOP_N_PER_CHUNK = 5
NGRAM_RANGE = (1, 2)
MIN_TOPIC_CHAR = 3
MAX_TOPIC_WORDS = 4
MAX_TOPICS_FINAL = 35
SCORE_THRESHOLD = 0.22

BAD_WORDS = {
    "imperial", "london", "figure", "week",
    "machinelearningfordesignengineers",
    "tableof", "content"
}


def ask_input_path():
    p = input("请输入 stage1_chunks.json 完整路径：\n> ").strip().strip('"').strip("'")
    return p


def ask_output_path(default_name="stage2a_topics_keybert_cpu_opt.json"):
    p = input(f"请输入输出JSON路径（回车=当前目录/{default_name}）：\n> ").strip().strip('"').strip("'")
    if not p:
        p = os.path.join(os.getcwd(), default_name)
    return p


def load_chunks(path):
    if not os.path.exists(path):
        raise FileNotFoundError(f"找不到输入文件: {path}")
    with open(path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        data = json.load(f)

    chunks = []
    for item in data:
        text = item.get("text", "")
        if text and text.strip():
            chunks.append({
                "chunk_id": item.get("chunk_id", len(chunks) + 1),
                "text": text.strip(),
                "word_count": item.get("word_count", len(text.split()))
            })
    return chunks


def clean_chunk_text(text: str) -> str:
    text = re.sub(r"\s+", " ", text)
    text = re.sub(r"([a-z])([A-Z])", r"\1 \2", text)
    text = re.sub(r"([a-zA-Z])(\d)", r"\1 \2", text)
    text = re.sub(r"(\d)([a-zA-Z])", r"\1 \2", text)
    text = re.sub(r"[|•·■◆►]", " ", text)
    text = re.sub(r"\s+", " ", text)
    return text.strip()


def clean_topic_name(s: str) -> str:
    s = s.strip()
    s = re.sub(r"\s+", " ", s)
    s = s.strip(".,;:()[]{}<>|/\\\"'")
    return s


def is_good_topic(name: str, score: float) -> bool:
    if score < SCORE_THRESHOLD:
        return False
    if len(name) < MIN_TOPIC_CHAR:
        return False

    wc = len(name.split())
    if wc == 0 or wc > MAX_TOPIC_WORDS:
        return False

    low = name.lower()
    low_no_space = low.replace(" ", "")

    if re.fullmatch(r"[\d\W_]+", name):
        return False

    for b in BAD_WORDS:
        if b in low_no_space:
            return False

    for token in name.split():
        if len(token) > 25:
            return False

    return True


def build_keybert():
    device = "cuda" if torch.cuda.is_available() else "cpu"
    print(f"[Info] torch={torch.__version__}, cuda_available={torch.cuda.is_available()}, device={device}")
    if device == "cuda":
        print(f"[Info] GPU: {torch.cuda.get_device_name(0)}")

    sbert = SentenceTransformer("paraphrase-multilingual-MiniLM-L12-v2", device=device)
    kw_model = KeyBERT(model=sbert)
    return kw_model


def extract_candidates(kw_model, chunk_text: str, top_n=5):
    text = clean_chunk_text(chunk_text)

    kws = kw_model.extract_keywords(
        text,
        keyphrase_ngram_range=NGRAM_RANGE,
        stop_words="english",
        use_maxsum=False,      # CPU更快
        nr_candidates=15,      # CPU更快
        top_n=top_n
    )

    out = []
    for kw, sc in kws:
        name = clean_topic_name(kw)
        if is_good_topic(name, float(sc)):
            out.append((name, float(sc)))
    return out


def aggregate_topics(chunks, kw_model):
    topic_map = {}
    total = len(chunks)

    for i, ch in enumerate(chunks, 1):
        print(f"[KeyBERT] chunk {i}/{total} ...")
        cands = extract_candidates(kw_model, ch["text"], TOP_N_PER_CHUNK)

        for name, score in cands:
            if name not in topic_map:
                topic_map[name] = {
                    "name": name,
                    "score_sum": 0.0,
                    "count": 0,
                    "chunk_ids": set()
                }
            topic_map[name]["score_sum"] += score
            topic_map[name]["count"] += 1
            topic_map[name]["chunk_ids"].add(ch["chunk_id"])

    topics = []
    for _, v in topic_map.items():
        avg_score = v["score_sum"] / max(v["count"], 1)
        topics.append({
            "name": v["name"],
            "avg_score": round(avg_score, 4),
            "frequency": v["count"],
            "chunk_ids": sorted(list(v["chunk_ids"]))
        })

    topics.sort(key=lambda x: (x["frequency"], x["avg_score"]), reverse=True)
    return topics


def build_subtopics_simple(ranked_topics):
    names = [t["name"] for t in ranked_topics]
    sub_map = {n: [] for n in names}

    for p in names:
        for c in names:
            if p == c:
                continue
            if p in c and len(c) > len(p):
                sub_map[p].append(c)

    for k in sub_map:
        uniq = []
        seen = set()
        for x in sorted(sub_map[k], key=lambda z: len(z)):
            if x not in seen:
                seen.add(x)
                uniq.append(x)
        sub_map[k] = uniq[:2]

    final_topics = []
    for t in ranked_topics[:MAX_TOPICS_FINAL]:
        name = t["name"]
        subs = [{"name": s, "description": f"{name} 的细化概念"} for s in sub_map.get(name, [])]
        final_topics.append({
            "name": name,
            "description": f"KeyBERT离线提取（出现{t['frequency']}次，平均相关度{t['avg_score']}）",
            "sub_topics": subs,
            "meta": {
                "avg_score": t["avg_score"],
                "frequency": t["frequency"],
                "chunk_ids": t["chunk_ids"]
            }
        })
    return final_topics


def main():
    print("=== Stage 2a: KeyBERT CPU优化版 ===")

    in_path = ask_input_path()
    out_path = ask_output_path()

    chunks = load_chunks(in_path)
    print(f"[1] 读取chunks成功：{len(chunks)} 个")

    kw_model = build_keybert()
    print("[2] KeyBERT模型加载完成")

    ranked = aggregate_topics(chunks, kw_model)
    print(f"[3] 候选Topic数：{len(ranked)}")

    final_topics = build_subtopics_simple(ranked)
    print(f"[4] 最终Topic数：{len(final_topics)}")

    output = {
        "stage": "2a",
        "method": "keybert_cpu_optimized",
        "input_chunks": len(chunks),
        "topics_count": len(final_topics),
        "topics": final_topics
    }

    out_dir = os.path.dirname(out_path)
    if out_dir:
        os.makedirs(out_dir, exist_ok=True)

    with open(out_path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        json.dump(output, f, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)

    print(f"[Saved] {out_path}")

    print("\n--- Top 15 Topics Preview ---")
    for i, t in enumerate(final_topics[:15], 1):
        print(f"{i:02d}. {t['name']} | freq={t['meta']['frequency']} | score={t['meta']['avg_score']}")

    print("\n✅ Stage 2a CPU优化版完成")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```

## ## 但质量上还需要再提一档（你现在结果里仍有粘连词）

比如：
- `linearlyseperable`
- `deep learningistheneuron`
- `learningistheneuron wx`

说明 **PDF抽取文本粘连** 仍在影响关键词质量。

---

## ## 现在最该做的优化（不加API也能明显提升）

## 1）先做“领域词典纠错/保留”
把常见ML核心词手工保留优先，例如：

- neuron
- artificial neural network
- activation function
- loss function
- backpropagation
- linear separable
- gradient descent

然后对提取结果做一次映射清洗（如 `linearlyseperable -> linearly separable`）。

## 2）加噪声过滤规则
过滤包含这些模式的词组：
- 超长连续小写串（长度 > 15 且无空格）
- 含 `learningfor` / `istheneuron` 这类拼接片段
- 课程抬头词（engineers / imperial / week）

## 3）把 `ngram` 再收紧
先试 `NGRAM_RANGE=(1,1)` 跑一版（全单词），看纯净度，再决定是否回到 `(1,2)`。

---

## ## 回答你“这次怎么这么快”

一句话：**首次下载+高参数组合的开销消失了**，现在是缓存命中+轻量参数，所以速度明显上来了。

但你现在账号 `insufficient_quota`，所以我给你一个**可直接切换**的版本：

- 没额度：自动回退到 KeyBERT（不中断）
