## ## 新建文件：`stage2a_topics_llm_with_fallback.py`

```python
import os
import json
import time
from typing import List, Dict, Any

from openai import OpenAI

# ====== 可选：本地fallback ======
USE_FALLBACK_KEYBERT = True
if USE_FALLBACK_KEYBERT:
    from keybert import KeyBERT
    from sentence_transformers import SentenceTransformer


# ========= 配置 =========
MODEL_NAME = "gpt-4o-mini"      # 有额度时优先用这个
MAX_CHUNK_CHARS = 3500          # 防止单次prompt过长
SLEEP_BETWEEN_CALLS = 0.4

# fallback参数
TOP_N_PER_CHUNK = 6


def ask_input_path():
    p = input("请输入 stage1_chunks.json 完整路径：\n> ").strip().strip('"').strip("'")
    return p


def ask_output_path(default_name="stage2a_topics_llm.json"):
    p = input(f"请输入输出JSON路径（回车=当前目录/{default_name}）：\n> ").strip().strip('"').strip("'")
    if not p:
        p = os.path.join(os.getcwd(), default_name)
    return p


def load_chunks(path: str):
    if not os.path.exists(path):
        raise FileNotFoundError(f"找不到输入文件: {path}")
    with open(path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        data = json.load(f)

    chunks = []
    for item in data:
        t = item.get("text", "")
        if t and t.strip():
            chunks.append({
                "chunk_id": item.get("chunk_id", len(chunks) + 1),
                "text": t.strip()
            })
    return chunks


# ---------------- LLM PART ----------------
def get_client():
    api_key = os.getenv("OPENAI_API_KEY")
    if not api_key:
        raise EnvironmentError("未检测到 OPENAI_API_KEY")
    return OpenAI(api_key=api_key)


def extract_topics_llm(client: OpenAI, chunk_text: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    text = chunk_text[:MAX_CHUNK_CHARS]

    prompt = f"""
你是机器学习课程知识图谱助手。请从材料中提取 Topic 和 Sub-Topic。

材料：
{text}

要求：
1) Topic 是课程知识概念（例如：Gradient Descent）
2) Sub-Topic 是该概念下细分点（例如：Learning Rate）
3) 不要提取课程抬头、学校名、页眉页脚
4) 不要输出长句，只输出概念短语
5) 若没有明确知识点，返回空列表
6) 严格输出JSON，不要任何额外文本

输出格式：
{{
  "topics": [
    {{
      "name": "topic name",
      "description": "one-sentence description",
      "sub_topics": [
        {{"name": "sub topic", "description": "one-sentence description"}}
      ]
    }}
  ]
}}
"""

    resp = client.chat.completions.create(
        model=MODEL_NAME,
        messages=[{"role": "user", "content": prompt}],
        response_format={"type": "json_object"},
        temperature=0
    )
    return json.loads(resp.choices[0].message.content)


def dedup_topics(topics: List[Dict[str, Any]]):
    seen = set()
    out = []
    for t in topics:
        name = (t.get("name") or "").strip()
        if not name:
            continue
        key = name.lower()
        if key in seen:
            continue
        seen.add(key)

        t["name"] = name
        t["description"] = (t.get("description") or "").strip()
        subs = t.get("sub_topics", [])
        if not isinstance(subs, list):
            subs = []
        clean_subs = []
        sub_seen = set()
        for s in subs:
            sn = (s.get("name") or "").strip()
            if not sn:
                continue
            sk = sn.lower()
            if sk in sub_seen:
                continue
            sub_seen.add(sk)
            clean_subs.append({
                "name": sn,
                "description": (s.get("description") or "").strip()
            })
        t["sub_topics"] = clean_subs
        out.append(t)
    return out


# ---------------- FALLBACK PART ----------------
def build_keybert():
    sbert = SentenceTransformer("paraphrase-multilingual-MiniLM-L12-v2")
    return KeyBERT(model=sbert)


def extract_topics_keybert_fallback(chunks):
    kw_model = build_keybert()
    topic_map = {}

    for i, ch in enumerate(chunks, 1):
        print(f"[Fallback-KeyBERT] chunk {i}/{len(chunks)}")
        kws = kw_model.extract_keywords(
            ch["text"],
            keyphrase_ngram_range=(1, 2),
            stop_words="english",
            use_maxsum=False,
            nr_candidates=15,
            top_n=TOP_N_PER_CHUNK
        )
        for kw, sc in kws:
            name = kw.strip()
            if len(name) < 3 or len(name.split()) > 4:
                continue
            key = name.lower()
            if key not in topic_map:
                topic_map[key] = {
                    "name": name,
                    "description": f"KeyBERT fallback topic (score={sc:.2f})",
                    "sub_topics": []
                }

    return list(topic_map.values())


def main():
    print("=== Stage 2a: LLM优先 + 无额度自动回退 ===")

    in_path = ask_input_path()
    out_path = ask_output_path()

    chunks = load_chunks(in_path)
    print(f"[1] 读取chunks成功：{len(chunks)} 个")

    all_topics = []
    used_method = "llm"

    try:
        client = get_client()

        for i, ch in enumerate(chunks, 1):
            print(f"[LLM] chunk {i}/{len(chunks)}")
            try:
                result = extract_topics_llm(client, ch["text"])
                topics = result.get("topics", [])
                if isinstance(topics, list):
                    all_topics.extend(topics)
            except Exception as e:
                msg = str(e)
                # 额度不足或限流：回退
                if "insufficient_quota" in msg or "RateLimitError" in msg or "429" in msg:
                    print("[Warn] 检测到额度/限流问题，切换到KeyBERT fallback。")
                    used_method = "keybert_fallback"
                    all_topics = extract_topics_keybert_fallback(chunks)
                    break
                else:
                    print(f"[Warn] chunk {i} LLM失败，跳过: {e}")

            time.sleep(SLEEP_BETWEEN_CALLS)

    except Exception as e:
        print(f"[Warn] LLM初始化失败: {e}")
        if USE_FALLBACK_KEYBERT:
            print("[Info] 使用KeyBERT fallback继续。")
            used_method = "keybert_fallback"
            all_topics = extract_topics_keybert_fallback(chunks)
        else:
            raise

    # 去重规范化
    final_topics = dedup_topics(all_topics)
    print(f"[2] 最终Topic数：{len(final_topics)}，方法：{used_method}")

    output = {
        "stage": "2a",
        "method": used_method,
        "input_chunks": len(chunks),
        "topics_count": len(final_topics),
        "topics": final_topics
    }

    out_dir = os.path.dirname(out_path)
    if out_dir:
        os.makedirs(out_dir, exist_ok=True)

    with open(out_path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        json.dump(output, f, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)

    print(f"[Saved] {out_path}")

    print("\n--- Top 15 Topics Preview ---")
    for i, t in enumerate(final_topics[:15], 1):
        print(f"{i:02d}. {t['name']}")

    print("\n✅ Stage 2a 完成")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```

## ## 运行方式

```powershell
python "d:/ic/master project/project_code/KG_construction/stage2a_topics_llm_with_fallback.py"
```

## ## 说明（很关键）

- 如果你仍然没额度，它会自动回退 KeyBERT，不会报错中断  
- 一旦你之后有额度，不改代码，自动走 LLM

---

如果你愿意，我下一条接着给你 **Stage 2b（同样 LLM优先 + 规则回退版）**，你整条 pipeline 就连起来了。

[06/07/2026 13:39:33 +00:00] MI, YU:
有没有keybBERT和llm同时用的必要？

Response:
# ## 有必要，而且很常见（推荐“混合式”）✅

简短结论：**有**。  
尤其你现在预算/额度不稳定时，`KeyBERT + LLM` 组合是性价比最高的。

---

## ## 为什么要同时用？

- **KeyBERT**：快、便宜、本地可跑，适合“广覆盖候选召回”
- **LLM**：语义理解强，适合“清洗、归并、结构化”

也就是经典两阶段：

1. **召回（Recall）**：KeyBERT 提很多候选
2. **精排/规范化（Precision）**：LLM 把噪声去掉，合并同义词，补 sub-topics

---

## ## 什么时候不需要同时用？

- 文档很小、你只要 demo：只用 LLM 也行  
- 完全离线且没额度：只用 KeyBERT  
- 对一致性要求极高、预算足：LLM主导 + 规则校验

---

## ## 你现在最适合的方案（结合你现状）

因为你暂时配额不足，建议：

- 平时：先跑 **KeyBERT 全量**
- 有额度时：用 LLM 只处理 **Top-K 候选**（比如前 30~50 个）
- 这样成本低、质量比纯KeyBERT高很多

---

## ## 一句话建议

**值得同时用**，但不是“每一步都让LLM做”。  
让 KeyBERT 做“粗提”，LLM做“精修”，这是你当前阶段最稳的工程方案。

[06/07/2026 13:40:44 +00:00] MI, YU:
现在的代码就是混合是吗？我的意思是有额度的时候需要也结合keyBERT判断吗？

Response:
# ## 你问得很专业：  
你现在那份代码是 **“LLM优先，失败才回退KeyBERT”**，不算真正“同时混合”。

---

## ## 有额度时，要不要也结合 KeyBERT？

**建议：要结合，但方式要轻量。**

最实用的是：

1. **KeyBERT先召回候选词**（便宜、稳定）  
2. **LLM只做清洗/归一化/分层**（贵但强）  

而不是让 LLM 从长文本里从零抽取全部。这样更稳、更省钱。

---

## ## 推荐你采用的“有额度版混合策略”

- Stage 2a:
  - KeyBERT 产出候选 topics（比如每块前 `5~8` 个）
  - 合并后给 LLM 一个候选列表，让它：
    - 去噪
    - 合并同义词
    - 生成 topic 描述和 sub-topics
- 好处：
  - token 用量明显下降
  - 结果通常比纯KeyBERT好、比纯LLM更可控

---

## ## 一句话判断

- **你现在代码**：不是混合，只是 fallback  
- **你想要的最佳实践**：有额度时也“KeyBERT + LLM协同”，是值得的 ✅

---

如果你愿意，我可以下一条直接给你一个 **真正混合版 Stage 2a 脚本**（KeyBERT召回 + LLM精修，带无额度自动降级）。

[06/15/2026 17:43:19 +00:00] MI, YU:
真正混合版 Stage 2a 脚本（KeyBERT召回 + LLM精修，带无额度自动降级）

Response:
# ## 太好了，给你「真正混合版 Stage 2a」✅  
**流程**：`KeyBERT召回候选 -> LLM精修结构化 -> 无额度自动降级为本地规则精修`

---

## ## 新建文件：`stage2a_hybrid_keybert_llm.py`

```python
import os
import re
import json
import time
from typing import List, Dict, Any

import torch
from keybert import KeyBERT
from sentence_transformers import SentenceTransformer
from openai import OpenAI


# ================= 配置 =================
MODEL_NAME = "gpt-4o-mini"
TOP_N_PER_CHUNK = 6
NGRAM_RANGE = (1, 2)
MAX_CANDIDATES_FOR_LLM = 80
MIN_SCORE = 0.18
MAX_TOPIC_WORDS = 4
SLEEP_BETWEEN_CALLS = 0.2

BAD_WORDS = {
    "imperial", "london", "figure", "week", "table", "content",
    "machinelearningfordesignengineers", "copyright"
}


# ================= IO =================
def ask_input_path():
    p = input("请输入 stage1_chunks.json 完整路径：\n> ").strip().strip('"').strip("'")
    return p


def ask_output_path(default_name="stage2a_topics_hybrid.json"):
    p = input(f"请输入输出JSON路径（回车=当前目录/{default_name}）：\n> ").strip().strip('"').strip("'")
    if not p:
        p = os.path.join(os.getcwd(), default_name)
    return p


def load_chunks(path: str):
    if not os.path.exists(path):
        raise FileNotFoundError(f"找不到输入文件: {path}")
    with open(path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        data = json.load(f)

    chunks = []
    for item in data:
        t = item.get("text", "")
        if t and t.strip():
            chunks.append({
                "chunk_id": item.get("chunk_id", len(chunks) + 1),
                "text": t.strip()
            })
    return chunks


# ================= KeyBERT召回 =================
def clean_text(text: str) -> str:
    text = re.sub(r"\s+", " ", text)
    text = re.sub(r"([a-z])([A-Z])", r"\1 \2", text)
    text = re.sub(r"([a-zA-Z])(\d)", r"\1 \2", text)
    text = re.sub(r"(\d)([a-zA-Z])", r"\1 \2", text)
    return text.strip()


def clean_phrase(s: str) -> str:
    s = re.sub(r"\s+", " ", s.strip())
    s = s.strip(".,;:()[]{}<>|/\\\"'")
    return s


def is_good_candidate(phrase: str, score: float) -> bool:
    if score < MIN_SCORE:
        return False
    if len(phrase) < 3:
        return False
    wc = len(phrase.split())
    if wc == 0 or wc > MAX_TOPIC_WORDS:
        return False

    low = phrase.lower()
    low_nospace = low.replace(" ", "")
    if re.fullmatch(r"[\d\W_]+", phrase):
        return False
    if any(b in low_nospace for b in BAD_WORDS):
        return False
    if any(len(tok) > 25 for tok in phrase.split()):
        return False
    return True


def build_keybert():
    device = "cuda" if torch.cuda.is_available() else "cpu"
    print(f"[Info] torch={torch.__version__}, cuda_available={torch.cuda.is_available()}, device={device}")
    if device == "cuda":
        print(f"[Info] GPU: {torch.cuda.get_device_name(0)}")
    sbert = SentenceTransformer("paraphrase-multilingual-MiniLM-L12-v2", device=device)
    return KeyBERT(model=sbert)


def keybert_recall(chunks: List[Dict[str, Any]]):
    kw_model = build_keybert()
    topic_map = {}

    for i, ch in enumerate(chunks, 1):
        print(f"[KeyBERT] chunk {i}/{len(chunks)} ...")
        text = clean_text(ch["text"])

        kws = kw_model.extract_keywords(
            text,
            keyphrase_ngram_range=NGRAM_RANGE,
            stop_words="english",
            use_maxsum=False,     # CPU友好
            nr_candidates=20,
            top_n=TOP_N_PER_CHUNK
        )

        for kw, sc in kws:
            phrase = clean_phrase(kw)
            score = float(sc)
            if not is_good_candidate(phrase, score):
                continue

            key = phrase.lower()
            if key not in topic_map:
                topic_map[key] = {
                    "name": phrase,
                    "score_sum": 0.0,
                    "freq": 0,
                    "chunk_ids": set()
                }
            topic_map[key]["score_sum"] += score
            topic_map[key]["freq"] += 1
            topic_map[key]["chunk_ids"].add(ch["chunk_id"])

    cands = []
    for _, v in topic_map.items():
        avg = v["score_sum"] / max(v["freq"], 1)
        cands.append({
            "name": v["name"],
            "avg_score": round(avg, 4),
            "frequency": v["freq"],
            "chunk_ids": sorted(list(v["chunk_ids"]))
        })

    cands.sort(key=lambda x: (x["frequency"], x["avg_score"]), reverse=True)
    cands = cands[:MAX_CANDIDATES_FOR_LLM]
    return cands


# ================= LLM精修 =================
def get_openai_client():
    api_key = os.getenv("OPENAI_API_KEY")
    if not api_key:
        raise EnvironmentError("未检测到 OPENAI_API_KEY")
    return OpenAI(api_key=api_key)


def llm_refine_topics(client: OpenAI, candidates: List[Dict[str, Any]]) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
    # 只给候选列表，不喂全文，省token
    candidate_lines = []
    for c in candidates:
        candidate_lines.append(f"- {c['name']} (freq={c['frequency']}, score={c['avg_score']})")
    cand_text = "\n".join(candidate_lines)

    prompt = f"""
You are building a Machine Learning course knowledge graph.

Given candidate topic phrases, refine them into clean Topics + Sub-Topics.

Candidate list:
{cand_text}

Rules:
1) Keep only true ML concepts (no headers/school names/noise).
2) Merge synonyms/variants (e.g., "artificial neural", "neural networks" if appropriate).
3) Topic name should be short noun phrase.
4) Provide concise description.
5) Each topic can have 0-3 sub_topics.
6) Output JSON only.

Output JSON format:
{{
  "topics": [
    {{
      "name": "...",
      "description": "...",
      "sub_topics": [
        {{"name": "...", "description": "..."}}
      ]
    }}
  ]
}}
"""

    resp = client.chat.completions.create(
        model=MODEL_NAME,
        messages=[{"role": "user", "content": prompt}],
        response_format={"type": "json_object"},
        temperature=0
    )
    data = json.loads(resp.choices[0].message.content)
    topics = data.get("topics", [])
    if not isinstance(topics, list):
        topics = []
    return topics


# ================= 无额度降级（本地精修） =================
def local_refine_topics(candidates: List[Dict[str, Any]]) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
    """
    简单规则精修：
    - 去重
    - 保留高频高分
    - 生成模板描述
    - 子主题用包含关系构建
    """
    # 先拿前40
    base = candidates[:40]
    names = [c["name"] for c in base]

    # 构建包含型子主题
    sub_map = {n: [] for n in names}
    for p in names:
        for ch in names:
            if p == ch:
                continue
            if p.lower() in ch.lower() and len(ch) > len(p):
                sub_map[p].append(ch)

    # 去重并截断
    for k in sub_map:
        uniq, seen = [], set()
        for x in sorted(sub_map[k], key=lambda z: len(z)):
            lx = x.lower()
            if lx not in seen:
                seen.add(lx)
                uniq.append(x)
        sub_map[k] = uniq[:2]

    topics = []
    for c in base:
        name = c["name"]
        topics.append({
            "name": name,
            "description": f"Auto-refined from KeyBERT candidates (freq={c['frequency']}, score={c['avg_score']}).",
            "sub_topics": [{"name": s, "description": f"Sub concept of {name}"} for s in sub_map.get(name, [])]
        })
    return topics


def dedup_topics(topics: List[Dict[str, Any]]) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
    out, seen = [], set()
    for t in topics:
        name = (t.get("name") or "").strip()
        if not name:
            continue
        k = name.lower()
        if k in seen:
            continue
        seen.add(k)

        desc = (t.get("description") or "").strip()
        subs = t.get("sub_topics", [])
        if not isinstance(subs, list):
            subs = []

        clean_subs, sub_seen = [], set()
        for s in subs:
            sn = (s.get("name") or "").strip()
            if not sn:
                continue
            sk = sn.lower()
            if sk in sub_seen:
                continue
            sub_seen.add(sk)
            clean_subs.append({
                "name": sn,
                "description": (s.get("description") or "").strip()
            })

        out.append({
            "name": name,
            "description": desc,
            "sub_topics": clean_subs
        })
    return out


def main():
    print("=== Stage 2a 真正混合版：KeyBERT召回 + LLM精修 ===")
    in_path = ask_input_path()
    out_path = ask_output_path()

    chunks = load_chunks(in_path)
    print(f"[1] 读取chunks成功：{len(chunks)}")

    # Step A: KeyBERT召回
    candidates = keybert_recall(chunks)
    print(f"[2] KeyBERT候选数：{len(candidates)}")

    # Step B: LLM精修（失败则本地降级）
    method = "hybrid_keybert_llm"
    topics = []
    try:
        client = get_openai_client()
        topics = llm_refine_topics(client, candidates)
        time.sleep(SLEEP_BETWEEN_CALLS)
        print("[3] LLM精修完成")
    except Exception as e:
        msg = str(e)
        if ("insufficient_quota" in msg) or ("429" in msg) or ("RateLimitError" in msg):
            print("[Warn] LLM额度不足/限流，自动降级为本地精修")
            method = "hybrid_keybert_local_fallback"
            topics = local_refine_topics(candidates)
        else:
            print(f"[Warn] LLM调用失败，自动降级。原因: {e}")
            method = "hybrid_keybert_local_fallback"
            topics = local_refine_topics(candidates)

    topics = dedup_topics(topics)
    print(f"[4] 最终Topic数：{len(topics)}，method={method}")

    output = {
        "stage": "2a",
        "method": method,
        "input_chunks": len(chunks),
        "candidate_count": len(candidates),
        "topics_count": len(topics),
        "candidates": candidates,   # 保留候选，便于你做对比分析
        "topics": topics
    }

    out_dir = os.path.dirname(out_path)
    if out_dir:
        os.makedirs(out_dir, exist_ok=True)

    with open(out_path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        json.dump(output, f, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)

    print(f"[Saved] {out_path}")

    print("\n--- Top 15 Final Topics Preview ---")
    for i, t in enumerate(topics[:15], 1):
        print(f"{i:02d}. {t['name']}")

    print("\n✅ Stage 2a 混合版完成")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```

[06/15/2026 18:09:03 +00:00] MI, YU:
这个stage1_chunks是什么来着 我的分块策略我不记得了

Response:
# ## `stage1_chunks.json` 是你 **Stage 1 文档分块** 的输出文件

你之前已经跑成功过 Stage 1，它做了两件事：

1. 从 PDF 提取文本  
2. 把文本按语义切成多个 chunk（块）

输出就是这个 `stage1_chunks.json`。

---

## ## 你当时的分块策略（你这版代码）

你用的是 **“TextTiling + 长度上限二次切分”**：

- 先用 `TextTilingTokenizer` 按主题变化做语义分块  
- 如果某块太长（超过 `MAX_WORDS_PER_CHUNK`，例如 800词），再按句子切  
- 过滤太短块（`MIN_WORDS_PER_CHUNK`，你当时大概是 40）

所以不是固定长度硬切，而是**语义优先 + 长度约束**。

---

## ## `stage1_chunks.json` 里面有什么？

通常每个元素像这样：

```json
{
  "chunk_id": 1,
  "word_count": 116,
  "text": "......"
}
```

也就是：

- `chunk_id`：第几块
- `word_count`：词数
- `text`：块内容（后续 Stage 2a 就吃这个）

---

## ## 快速确认你本地这个文件内容

在 PowerShell 里看前几行：

```powershell

```

或者用 Python 快速看统计：

```powershell
python -c "import json; p=r'D:/ic/master project/project_code/KG_construction/stage1_chunks.json'; d=json.load(open(p,'r',encoding='utf-8')); print('chunks=',len(d)); print('keys=',d[0].keys())"
```

## ## 直接可用的 Prompt（英文稳一点）

```python
prompt = f"""
You are refining candidate concepts for a university Machine Learning course knowledge graph.

Candidate phrases:
{cand_text}

Task:
Convert candidates into a practical Topic/Sub-Topic structure for learning graph construction.

Hard constraints:
1) Output between 8 and 15 topics (unless candidates are truly insufficient).
2) Prefer specific technical concepts over overly broad labels.
3) Do NOT keep only generic topics such as "Machine Learning" unless needed as one parent topic.
4) Remove header/footer/institution noise.
5) Merge obvious duplicates/synonyms, but DO NOT over-merge distinct concepts.
6) Topic names must be short noun phrases (1-4 words preferred).
7) Each topic should have 1-3 sub_topics when possible.

Coverage requirements (very important):
- Preserve major concepts that appear in candidates related to:
  neuron, artificial neural network, activation function, loss function,
  backpropagation, linear separation/separability, regression/classification, training.
- If a concept appears in candidates with reasonable frequency/score, try to keep it.

Output JSON only:
{{
  "topics": [
    {{
      "name": "...",
      "description": "1 concise sentence",
      "sub_topics": [
        {{"name": "...", "description": "1 concise sentence"}}
      ]
    }}
  ]
}}
"""
```

---

## ## 再加一个“数量保险”后处理（推荐）

在 `topics = data.get("topics", [])` 后面加：

```python
# 若LLM返回过少，触发一次补充请求
if len(topics) < 8:
    repair_prompt = f"""
You previously returned too few topics ({len(topics)}).
Please expand to 8-15 topics using the same candidates below.
Keep technical granularity and avoid only broad labels.

Candidates:
{cand_text}

Return JSON with key "topics" only.
"""
    resp2 = client.chat.completions.create(
        model=MODEL_NAME,
        messages=[{"role": "user", "content": repair_prompt}],
        response_format={"type": "json_object"},
        temperature=0
    )
    data2 = json.loads(resp2.choices[0].message.content)
    topics2 = data2.get("topics", [])
    if isinstance(topics2, list) and len(topics2) > len(topics):
        topics = topics2
```

[06/15/2026 18:14:21 +00:00] MI, YU:
我怎么判断是stage1的质量问题还是stage2？

Response:
# ## 这是个非常关键的诊断问题：**先定位 Stage1 还是 Stage2 的锅** ✅

你可以用一个很实用的“二分诊断法”：

---

## ## 一、先看 Stage1 输出文本是否“可读、可切分”

你检查 `stage1_chunks.json` 里的 chunk 文本，重点看：

- 是否大量出现粘连词（如 `learningistheneuron`）  
- 是否有页眉页脚噪声反复出现（学校名、课程标题）  
- 每个 chunk 是否是完整语义段，而不是碎片

### 判断标准
- **如果 chunk 本身就脏/粘连严重** → 主要是 **Stage1 问题**
- **如果 chunk 文本还可以读，但 Topic 很差** → 主要是 **Stage2 问题**

---

## ## 二、再看 Stage2 是否“过度压缩/过度泛化”

你这次 Stage2 最终只剩 3 个 Topic（太少），这通常是：

- prompt 约束太保守  
- LLM 过度合并  
- 或 KeyBERT 候选质量差导致 LLM 只能保留大类词

### 判断标准
- 候选有 15~30 个合理术语，但最后只出 2~4 个泛词  
  → **Stage2 精修策略问题**（不是 Stage1 主因）

---

## ## 三、你当前这份结果的初步判断（基于你贴的日志）

你有：

- Stage1：7 chunks，能提取到文本  
- Stage2候选：21  
- LLM后：3 topics

这很像：  
**Stage1有一定文本粘连噪声 + Stage2精修过强（主要问题在 Stage2）**

---

## ## 四、最小诊断实验（10分钟内完成）

做两个对照：

1. **只看 KeyBERT候选前20个**（不经LLM）  
2. **LLM精修结果**

- 若候选里已有 `activation function / backpropagation / loss` 等，但LLM结果没了  
  => Stage2问题
- 若候选本身几乎全是垃圾短语  
  => Stage1问题（文本清洗优先）

---

## ## 五、一句话结论模板（你可直接对导师说）

> 我用对照诊断区分了误差来源：  
> 若候选层已包含关键术语而最终主题缺失，则问题在 Stage2 精修策略；  
> 若候选层本身噪声高且术语缺失，则问题在 Stage1 文本质量与分块清洗。  
> 当前实验中两者均有影响，但主要瓶颈在 Stage2 过度泛化。

如果你要，我下一条直接给你一个 **“自动诊断脚本”**（读取这两个JSON后自动输出：噪声比、候选覆盖、过合并风险分），以后每次跑完都能一键出报告。

[06/15/2026 18:21:41 +00:00] MI, YU:
import os
import re
import json
import time
from typing import List, Dict, Any

import torch
from keybert import KeyBERT
from sentence_transformers import SentenceTransformer
from openai import OpenAI


# ================= 配置 =================
MODEL_NAME = "gpt-4o-mini"
TOP_N_PER_CHUNK = 6
NGRAM_RANGE = (1, 2)
MAX_CANDIDATES_FOR_LLM = 80
MIN_SCORE = 0.18
MAX_TOPIC_WORDS = 4
SLEEP_BETWEEN_CALLS = 0.2

BAD_WORDS = {
    "imperial", "london", "figure", "week", "table", "content",
    "machinelearningfordesignengineers", "copyright"
}


# ================= IO =================
def ask_input_path():
    p = input("请输入 stage1_chunks.json 完整路径：\n> ").strip().strip('"').strip("'")
    return p


def ask_output_path(default_name="stage2a_topics_hybrid.json"):
    p = input(f"请输入输出JSON路径（回车=当前目录/{default_name}）：\n> ").strip().strip('"').strip("'")
    if not p:
        p = os.path.join(os.getcwd(), default_name)
    return p


def load_chunks(path: str):
    if not os.path.exists(path):
        raise FileNotFoundError(f"找不到输入文件: {path}")
    with open(path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        data = json.load(f)

    chunks = []
    for item in data:
        t = item.get("text", "")
        if t and t.strip():
            chunks.append({
                "chunk_id": item.get("chunk_id", len(chunks) + 1),
                "text": t.strip()
            })
    return chunks


# ================= KeyBERT召回 =================
def clean_text(text: str) -> str:
    text = re.sub(r"\s+", " ", text)
    text = re.sub(r"([a-z])([A-Z])", r"\1 \2", text)
    text = re.sub(r"([a-zA-Z])(\d)", r"\1 \2", text)
    text = re.sub(r"(\d)([a-zA-Z])", r"\1 \2", text)
    return text.strip()


def clean_phrase(s: str) -> str:
    s = re.sub(r"\s+", " ", s.strip())
    s = s.strip(".,;:()[]{}<>|/\\\"'")
    return s


def is_good_candidate(phrase: str, score: float) -> bool:
    if score < MIN_SCORE:
        return False
    if len(phrase) < 3:
        return False
    wc = len(phrase.split())
    if wc == 0 or wc > MAX_TOPIC_WORDS:
        return False

    low = phrase.lower()
    low_nospace = low.replace(" ", "")
    if re.fullmatch(r"[\d\W_]+", phrase):
        return False
    if any(b in low_nospace for b in BAD_WORDS):
        return False
    if any(len(tok) > 25 for tok in phrase.split()):
        return False
    return True


def build_keybert():
    device = "cuda" if torch.cuda.is_available() else "cpu"
    print(f"[Info] torch={torch.__version__}, cuda_available={torch.cuda.is_available()}, device={device}")
    if device == "cuda":
        print(f"[Info] GPU: {torch.cuda.get_device_name(0)}")
    sbert = SentenceTransformer("paraphrase-multilingual-MiniLM-L12-v2", device=device)
    return KeyBERT(model=sbert)


def keybert_recall(chunks: List[Dict[str, Any]]):
    kw_model = build_keybert()
    topic_map = {}

    for i, ch in enumerate(chunks, 1):
        print(f"[KeyBERT] chunk {i}/{len(chunks)} ...")
        text = clean_text(ch["text"])

        kws = kw_model.extract_keywords(
            text,
            keyphrase_ngram_range=NGRAM_RANGE,
            stop_words="english",
            use_maxsum=False,     # CPU友好
            nr_candidates=20,
            top_n=TOP_N_PER_CHUNK
        )

        for kw, sc in kws:
            phrase = clean_phrase(kw)
            score = float(sc)
            if not is_good_candidate(phrase, score):
                continue

            key = phrase.lower()
            if key not in topic_map:
                topic_map[key] = {
                    "name": phrase,
                    "score_sum": 0.0,
                    "freq": 0,
                    "chunk_ids": set()
                }
            topic_map[key]["score_sum"] += score
            topic_map[key]["freq"] += 1
            topic_map[key]["chunk_ids"].add(ch["chunk_id"])

    cands = []
    for _, v in topic_map.items():
        avg = v["score_sum"] / max(v["freq"], 1)
        cands.append({
            "name": v["name"],
            "avg_score": round(avg, 4),
            "frequency": v["freq"],
            "chunk_ids": sorted(list(v["chunk_ids"]))
        })

    cands.sort(key=lambda x: (x["frequency"], x["avg_score"]), reverse=True)
    cands = cands[:MAX_CANDIDATES_FOR_LLM]
    return cands


# ================= LLM精修 =================
def get_openai_client():
    api_key = os.getenv("OPENAI_API_KEY")
    if not api_key:
        raise EnvironmentError("未检测到 OPENAI_API_KEY")
    return OpenAI(api_key=api_key)


def llm_refine_topics(client: OpenAI, candidates: List[Dict[str, Any]]) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
    # 只给候选列表，不喂全文，省token
    candidate_lines = []
    for c in candidates:
        candidate_lines.append(f"- {c['name']} (freq={c['frequency']}, score={c['avg_score']})")
    cand_text = "\n".join(candidate_lines)

    prompt = f"""
You are refining candidate concepts for a university Machine Learning course knowledge graph.

Candidate phrases:
{cand_text}

Task:
Convert candidates into a practical Topic/Sub-Topic structure for learning graph construction.

Hard constraints:
1) Output between 8 and 15 topics (unless candidates are truly insufficient).
2) Prefer specific technical concepts over overly broad labels.
3) Do NOT keep only generic topics such as "Machine Learning" unless needed as one parent topic.
4) Remove header/footer/institution noise.
5) Merge obvious duplicates/synonyms, but DO NOT over-merge distinct concepts.
6) Topic names must be short noun phrases (1-4 words preferred).
7) Each topic should have 1-3 sub_topics when possible.

Coverage requirements (very important):
- Preserve major concepts that appear in candidates related to:
  neuron, artificial neural network, activation function, loss function,
  backpropagation, linear separation/separability, regression/classification, training.
- If a concept appears in candidates with reasonable frequency/score, try to keep it.

Output JSON only:
{{
  "topics": [
    {{
      "name": "...",
      "description": "1 concise sentence",
      "sub_topics": [
        {{"name": "...", "description": "1 concise sentence"}}
      ]
    }}
  ]
}}
"""

    resp = client.chat.completions.create(
        model=MODEL_NAME,
        messages=[{"role": "user", "content": prompt}],
        response_format={"type": "json_object"},
        temperature=0
    )
    data = json.loads(resp.choices[0].message.content)
    topics = data.get("topics", [])
    # 若LLM返回过少，触发一次补充请求
if len(topics) < 8:
    repair_prompt = f"""
You previously returned too few topics ({len(topics)}).
Please expand to 8-15 topics using the same candidates below.
Keep technical granularity and avoid only broad labels.

Candidates:
{cand_text}

Return JSON with key "topics" only.
"""
    resp2 = client.chat.completions.create(
        model=MODEL_NAME,
        messages=[{"role": "user", "content": repair_prompt}],
        response_format={"type": "json_object"},
        temperature=0
    )
    data2 = json.loads(resp2.choices[0].message.content)
    topics2 = data2.get("topics", [])
    if isinstance(topics2, list) and len(topics2) > len(topics):
        topics = topics2
    if not isinstance(topics, list):
        topics = []
    return topics


# ================= 无额度降级（本地精修） =================
def local_refine_topics(candidates: List[Dict[str, Any]]) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
    """
    简单规则精修：
    - 去重
    - 保留高频高分
    - 生成模板描述
    - 子主题用包含关系构建
    """
    # 先拿前40
    base = candidates[:40]
    names = [c["name"] for c in base]

    # 构建包含型子主题
    sub_map = {n: [] for n in names}
    for p in names:
        for ch in names:
            if p == ch:
                continue
            if p.lower() in ch.lower() and len(ch) > len(p):
                sub_map[p].append(ch)

    # 去重并截断
    for k in sub_map:
        uniq, seen = [], set()
        for x in sorted(sub_map[k], key=lambda z: len(z)):
            lx = x.lower()
            if lx not in seen:
                seen.add(lx)
                uniq.append(x)
        sub_map[k] = uniq[:2]

    topics = []
    for c in base:
        name = c["name"]
        topics.append({
            "name": name,
            "description": f"Auto-refined from KeyBERT candidates (freq={c['frequency']}, score={c['avg_score']}).",
            "sub_topics": [{"name": s, "description": f"Sub concept of {name}"} for s in sub_map.get(name, [])]
        })
    return topics


def dedup_topics(topics: List[Dict[str, Any]]) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
    out, seen = [], set()
    for t in topics:
        name = (t.get("name") or "").strip()
        if not name:
            continue
        k = name.lower()
        if k in seen:
            continue
        seen.add(k)

        desc = (t.get("description") or "").strip()
        subs = t.get("sub_topics", [])
        if not isinstance(subs, list):
            subs = []

        clean_subs, sub_seen = [], set()
        for s in subs:
            sn = (s.get("name") or "").strip()
            if not sn:
                continue
            sk = sn.lower()
            if sk in sub_seen:
                continue
            sub_seen.add(sk)
            clean_subs.append({
                "name": sn,
                "description": (s.get("description") or "").strip()
            })

        out.append({
            "name": name,
            "description": desc,
            "sub_topics": clean_subs
        })
    return out


def main():
    print("=== Stage 2a 真正混合版：KeyBERT召回 + LLM精修 ===")
    in_path = ask_input_path()
    out_path = ask_output_path()

    chunks = load_chunks(in_path)
    print(f"[1] 读取chunks成功：{len(chunks)}")

    # Step A: KeyBERT召回
    candidates = keybert_recall(chunks)
    print(f"[2] KeyBERT候选数：{len(candidates)}")

    # Step B: LLM精修（失败则本地降级）
    method = "hybrid_keybert_llm"
    topics = []
    try:
        client = get_openai_client()
        topics = llm_refine_topics(client, candidates)
        time.sleep(SLEEP_BETWEEN_CALLS)
        print("[3] LLM精修完成")
    except Exception as e:
        msg = str(e)
        if ("insufficient_quota" in msg) or ("429" in msg) or ("RateLimitError" in msg):
            print("[Warn] LLM额度不足/限流，自动降级为本地精修")
            method = "hybrid_keybert_local_fallback"
            topics = local_refine_topics(candidates)
        else:
            print(f"[Warn] LLM调用失败，自动降级。原因: {e}")
            method = "hybrid_keybert_local_fallback"
            topics = local_refine_topics(candidates)

    topics = dedup_topics(topics)
    print(f"[4] 最终Topic数：{len(topics)}，method={method}")

    output = {
        "stage": "2a",
        "method": method,
        "input_chunks": len(chunks),
        "candidate_count": len(candidates),
        "topics_count": len(topics),
        "candidates": candidates,   # 保留候选，便于你做对比分析
        "topics": topics
    }

    out_dir = os.path.dirname(out_path)
    if out_dir:
        os.makedirs(out_dir, exist_ok=True)

    with open(out_path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        json.dump(output, f, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)

    print(f"[Saved] {out_path}")

    print("\n--- Top 15 Final Topics Preview ---")
    for i, t in enumerate(topics[:15], 1):
        print(f"{i:02d}. {t['name']}")

    print("\n✅ Stage 2a 混合版完成")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

def llm_refine_topics(client: OpenAI, candidates: List[Dict[str, Any]]) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
    # 只给候选列表，不喂全文，省token
    candidate_lines = []
    for c in candidates:
        candidate_lines.append(f"- {c['name']} (freq={c['frequency']}, score={c['avg_score']})")
    cand_text = "\n".join(candidate_lines)

    prompt = f"""
You are refining candidate concepts for a university Machine Learning course knowledge graph.

Candidate phrases:
{cand_text}

Task:
Convert candidates into a practical Topic/Sub-Topic structure for learning graph construction.

Hard constraints:
1) Output between 8 and 15 topics (unless candidates are truly insufficient).
2) Prefer specific technical concepts over overly broad labels.
3) Do NOT keep only generic topics such as "Machine Learning" unless needed as one parent topic.
4) Remove header/footer/institution noise.
5) Merge obvious duplicates/synonyms, but DO NOT over-merge distinct concepts.
6) Topic names must be short noun phrases (1-4 words preferred).
7) Each topic should have 1-3 sub_topics when possible.

Coverage requirements (very important):
- Preserve major concepts that appear in candidates related to:
  neuron, artificial neural network, activation function, loss function,
  backpropagation, linear separation/separability, regression/classification, training.
- If a concept appears in candidates with reasonable frequency/score, try to keep it.

Output JSON only:
{{
  "topics": [
    {{
      "name": "...",
      "description": "1 concise sentence",
      "sub_topics": [
        {{"name": "...", "description": "1 concise sentence"}}
      ]
    }}
  ]
}}
"""

    resp = client.chat.completions.create(
        model=MODEL_NAME,
        messages=[{"role": "user", "content": prompt}],
        response_format={"type": "json_object"},
        temperature=0
    )

    data = json.loads(resp.choices[0].message.content)
    topics = data.get("topics", [])
    if not isinstance(topics, list):
        topics = []

    # 若LLM返回过少，触发一次补充请求
    if len(topics) < 8:
        repair_prompt = f"""
You previously returned too few topics ({len(topics)}).
Please expand to 8-15 topics using the same candidates below.
Keep technical granularity and avoid only broad labels.

Candidates:
{cand_text}

Return JSON with key "topics" only.
"""
        resp2 = client.chat.completions.create(
            model=MODEL_NAME,
            messages=[{"role": "user", "content": repair_prompt}],
            response_format={"type": "json_object"},
            temperature=0
        )
        data2 = json.loads(resp2.choices[0].message.content)
        topics2 = data2.get("topics", [])
        if isinstance(topics2, list) and len(topics2) > len(topics):
            topics = topics2

    return topics

# ## 结论先说（为了你这周“跑通”）：  
**Stage1 不要大改算法，做 3 个“轻量优化”就够了。**  
目标是：让 Stage2 输入更干净，避免粘连词和页眉噪声。

---

## ## 你这周最实用的 Stage1 优化清单（按优先级）

## **P0：PDF文本清洗（必须做）**
在分块前加一个 `clean_raw_text()`：

- 去页眉页脚噪声（如学校名、课程标题反复行）
- 修复粘连边界（`[a-z][A-Z]` 之间补空格）
- 多空格/换行归一化

---

## **P1：分块后过滤短块 + 噪声块（必须做）**
- 丢弃词数太短块（例如 `< 60`）
- 丢弃“字母比例过低”的块（图注/符号堆）

---

## **P2：保存可诊断元数据（建议做）**
在 `stage1_chunks.json` 每块增加：
- `word_count`
- `source_page_range`（若能做）
- `quality_flag`（normal/noisy）

这样你之后排查非常快。

# ## 给你可直接替换的 Stage1 核心代码（精简版）

把你 Stage1 脚本里文本提取和分块前处理改成下面结构：

```python
import re
from collections import Counter

NOISE_PATTERNS = [
    r"Imperial College London",
    r"Machine Learning for Design Engineers",
    r"Week\s*\d+",
    r"Table of Content[s]?"
]

def clean_raw_text(text: str) -> str:
    # 1) 去重复页眉页脚
    lines = [ln.strip() for ln in text.splitlines() if ln.strip()]
    cleaned_lines = []
    for ln in lines:
        skip = False
        for p in NOISE_PATTERNS:
            if re.search(p, ln, flags=re.IGNORECASE):
                skip = True
                break
        if not skip:
            cleaned_lines.append(ln)

    text = "\n".join(cleaned_lines)

    # 2) 修复小写->大写粘连
    text = re.sub(r"([a-z])([A-Z])", r"\1 \2", text)

    # 3) 字母数字边界补空格
    text = re.sub(r"([a-zA-Z])(\d)", r"\1 \2", text)
    text = re.sub(r"(\d)([a-zA-Z])", r"\1 \2", text)

    # 4) 空白归一化
    text = re.sub(r"[ \t]+", " ", text)
    text = re.sub(r"\n{3,}", "\n\n", text)

    return text.strip()


def is_noisy_chunk(chunk: str) -> bool:
    words = chunk.split()
    if len(words) < 60:
        return True

    # 字母字符占比太低（图注/符号块）
    chars = "".join(words)
    alpha_ratio = sum(c.isalpha() for c in chars) / max(len(chars), 1)
    if alpha_ratio < 0.6:
        return True

    return False
```

然后在主流程里：

1. `text = extract_text_from_pdf(...)`  
2. `text = clean_raw_text(text)`  
3. `chunks = chunk_with_texttiling(text, ...)`  
4. `chunks = [c for c in chunks if not is_noisy_chunk(c)]`

[06/15/2026 18:25:11 +00:00] MI, YU:
import os
import re
import json
import pdfplumber
from nltk.tokenize import TextTilingTokenizer

# ========= 可调参数 =========
MAX_WORDS_PER_CHUNK = 800
MIN_WORDS_PER_CHUNK = 40


def ask_pdf_path() -> str:
    """
    交互式获取PDF路径
    - 允许带引号粘贴
    - 自动去掉首尾空格和引号
    """
    p = input("请输入PDF文件完整路径（例如 D:\\data\\ml_slides.pdf）：\n> ").strip()
    p = p.strip('"').strip("'")  # 去引号
    return p


def extract_text_from_pdf(pdf_path: str) -> str:
    if not os.path.exists(pdf_path):
        raise FileNotFoundError(f"找不到PDF文件: {pdf_path}")

    pages_text = []
    with pdfplumber.open(pdf_path) as pdf:
        for i, page in enumerate(pdf.pages, 1):
            t = page.extract_text()
            if t and t.strip():
                pages_text.append(t.strip())

    if not pages_text:
        raise ValueError("PDF提取不到文本（可能是扫描版PDF）")

    return "\n\n".join(pages_text)


def split_sentences(text: str):
    text = text.replace("\n", " ")
    sents = re.split(r'(?<=[。！？.!?])\s+', text)
    return [s.strip() for s in sents if s.strip()]


def chunk_with_texttiling(text: str, max_words=800, min_words=40):
    tokenizer = TextTilingTokenizer(w=20, k=10)

    try:
        tiles = tokenizer.tokenize(text)
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"[Warn] TextTiling失败，回退到段落分块: {e}")
        tiles = [p.strip() for p in text.split("\n\n") if p.strip()]

    final_chunks = []
    for tile in tiles:
        words = tile.split()

        if len(words) <= max_words:
            final_chunks.append(tile.strip())
        else:
            sents = split_sentences(tile)
            current, cur_len = [], 0

            for s in sents:
                wl = len(s.split())
                if current and (cur_len + wl > max_words):
                    final_chunks.append(" ".join(current).strip())
                    current, cur_len = [s], wl
                else:
                    current.append(s)
                    cur_len += wl

            if current:
                final_chunks.append(" ".join(current).strip())

    final_chunks = [c for c in final_chunks if len(c.split()) >= min_words]
    return final_chunks


def main():
    print("=== Stage 1: 文件选择版（PDF提取 + 语义分块）===")

    pdf_path = ask_pdf_path()
    print(f"\n你选择的文件：{pdf_path}")

    # 输出目录：默认放在脚本当前目录下
    out_dir = os.getcwd()
    print(f"输出目录：{out_dir}")

    # 1) 提取文本
    text = extract_text_from_pdf(pdf_path)
    total_words = len(text.split())
    print(f"[1] 文本提取完成，词数估算: {total_words}")

    text_out = os.path.join(out_dir, "stage1_text.txt")
    with open(text_out, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        f.write(text)
    print(f"[Saved] {text_out}")

    # 2) 分块
    chunks = chunk_with_texttiling(
        text,
        max_words=MAX_WORDS_PER_CHUNK,
        min_words=MIN_WORDS_PER_CHUNK
    )
    print(f"[2] 分块完成，语义块数量: {len(chunks)}")

    chunk_data = []
    for i, c in enumerate(chunks, 1):
        chunk_data.append({
            "chunk_id": i,
            "word_count": len(c.split()),
            "text": c
        })

    chunks_out = os.path.join(out_dir, "stage1_chunks.json")
    with open(chunks_out, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        json.dump(chunk_data, f, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)
    print(f"[Saved] {chunks_out}")

    # 预览
    n = min(3, len(chunks))
    print(f"\n--- 前{n}块预览 ---")
    for i in range(n):
        snippet = chunks[i][:250].replace("\n", " ")
        print(f"\n[Chunk {i+1}] words={len(chunks[i].split())}")
        print(snippet + ("..." if len(chunks[i]) > 250 else ""))

    print("\n✅ Stage 1 完成")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

## ## ① 在 `extract_text_from_pdf` 后面新增这段函数

```python
NOISE_PATTERNS = [
    r"Imperial College London",
    r"Machine Learning for Design Engineers",
    r"Week\s*\d+",
    r"Table of Content[s]?"
]

def clean_raw_text(text: str) -> str:
    """
    轻量文本清洗：
    - 去页眉页脚噪声行
    - 修复小写->大写粘连
    - 字母数字边界加空格
    - 空白归一化
    """
    lines = [ln.strip() for ln in text.splitlines() if ln.strip()]
    cleaned_lines = []

    for ln in lines:
        if any(re.search(p, ln, flags=re.IGNORECASE) for p in NOISE_PATTERNS):
            continue
        cleaned_lines.append(ln)

    text = "\n".join(cleaned_lines)

    text = re.sub(r"([a-z])([A-Z])", r"\1 \2", text)
    text = re.sub(r"([a-zA-Z])(\d)", r"\1 \2", text)
    text = re.sub(r"(\d)([a-zA-Z])", r"\1 \2", text)

    text = re.sub(r"[ \t]+", " ", text)
    text = re.sub(r"\n{3,}", "\n\n", text)

    return text.strip()


def is_noisy_chunk(chunk: str) -> bool:
    """
    分块后噪声过滤：
    - 太短
    - 字母比例过低（常见符号/图注块）
    """
    words = chunk.split()
    if len(words) < 60:
        return True

    chars = "".join(words)
    alpha_ratio = sum(ch.isalpha() for ch in chars) / max(len(chars), 1)
    if alpha_ratio < 0.6:
        return True

    return False
```

- 再合并成 200~350 词的块

## ## 你可以这样分策略（实战版）

## **1) Slides 专用策略**
特点：短句、标题多、页眉页脚多、版式噪声重。  
策略：
- 弱化 TextTiling，改“段落/页面窗口 + 长度切分”

## **2) 论文/书刊专用策略**

- TextTiling/章节标题切分效果更好
- 可以用更大 chunk（如 $$600\text{~}1000$$ 词）
- LLM可直接从 chunk 抽 topic + prerequisite

- 先识别“定义、定理、例题、练习”段落类型
- Topic抽取可结合规则词典（如“Definition”, “Example”）
- 先修关系可结合讲义顺序 + LLM推断

你这两天要把 KG 跑通，建议 Stage1 用“稳健优先”，不要死磕单一 TextTiling。  
下面这个脚本做了：

- 自动识别文档类型：`slides / paper_book / lecture_notes`
- 根据类型切不同分块策略
- 输出统一的 `stage1_chunks.json`
- 保留诊断信息，便于你后续解释

## ## 新建文件：`stage1_adaptive_chunking.py`

```python
import os
import re
import json
import pdfplumber
from collections import Counter
from nltk.tokenize import TextTilingTokenizer

# ========= 全局参数 =========
MIN_WORDS_PER_CHUNK = 40
MAX_WORDS_SLIDES = 280
MAX_WORDS_PAPER = 800
MAX_WORDS_NOTES = 450


def ask_pdf_path() -> str:
    p = input("请输入PDF完整路径：\n> ").strip().strip('"').strip("'")
    return p


def ask_output_path(default_name="stage1_chunks.json") -> str:
    p = input(f"请输入输出JSON路径（回车=当前目录/{default_name}）：\n> ").strip().strip('"').strip("'")
    if not p:
        p = os.path.join(os.getcwd(), default_name)
    return p


def extract_text_from_pdf(pdf_path: str) -> str:
    if not os.path.exists(pdf_path):
        raise FileNotFoundError(f"找不到PDF: {pdf_path}")

    texts = []
    with pdfplumber.open(pdf_path) as pdf:
        for page in pdf.pages:
            t = page.extract_text()
            if t and t.strip():
                texts.append(t.strip())

    if not texts:
        raise ValueError("PDF提取不到文本（可能是扫描版）")

    return "\n\n".join(texts)


def clean_raw_text(text: str) -> str:
    # 空白归一化
    text = re.sub(r"[ \t]+", " ", text)
    text = re.sub(r"\n{3,}", "\n\n", text)

    # 粘连修复：aB / a1 / 1a
    text = re.sub(r"([a-z])([A-Z])", r"\1 \2", text)
    text = re.sub(r"([a-zA-Z])(\d)", r"\1 \2", text)
    text = re.sub(r"(\d)([a-zA-Z])", r"\1 \2", text)

    return text.strip()


def estimate_doc_type(text: str) -> str:
    """
    粗粒度文档类型识别：
    - slides: 短行多、标题词多、重复头尾词多
    - paper_book: 长段多、句子更完整
    - lecture_notes: 介于两者，常含definition/example等教学词
    """
    lines = [ln.strip() for ln in text.splitlines() if ln.strip()]
    if not lines:
        return "paper_book"

    avg_line_len = sum(len(x.split()) for x in lines) / len(lines)
    short_line_ratio = sum(1 for x in lines if len(x.split()) <= 8) / len(lines)

    lower_text = text.lower()
    slide_markers = ["table of", "overview", "contents", "week", "lecture", "imperial", "slide"]
    notes_markers = ["definition", "example", "exercise", "theorem", "proof"]

    slide_score = sum(1 for m in slide_markers if m in lower_text) + (2 if short_line_ratio > 0.45 else 0)
    notes_score = sum(1 for m in notes_markers if m in lower_text)

    if slide_score >= 3:
        return "slides"
    if notes_score >= 2:
        return "lecture_notes"
    return "paper_book"


def split_sentences(text: str):
    text = text.replace("\n", " ")
    sents = re.split(r'(?<=[。！？.!?])\s+', text)
    return [s.strip() for s in sents if s.strip()]


def pack_by_word_limit(units, max_words):
    chunks = []
    cur, cur_len = [], 0
    for u in units:
        wl = len(u.split())
        if wl == 0:
            continue
        if cur and (cur_len + wl > max_words):
            chunks.append(" ".join(cur).strip())
            cur, cur_len = [u], wl
        else:
            cur.append(u)
            cur_len += wl
    if cur:
        chunks.append(" ".join(cur).strip())
    return chunks


def chunk_slides(text: str):
    """
    Slides策略：
    段落优先 + 词数窗口，避免TextTiling在幻灯片上失效
    """
    paras = [p.strip() for p in text.split("\n\n") if p.strip()]
    chunks = pack_by_word_limit(paras, MAX_WORDS_SLIDES)

    # 超长块再句切
    final = []
    for c in chunks:
        if len(c.split()) <= MAX_WORDS_SLIDES:
            final.append(c)
        else:
            final.extend(pack_by_word_limit(split_sentences(c), MAX_WORDS_SLIDES))
    return final


def chunk_paper_book(text: str):
    """
    书/论文策略：
    TextTiling + 超长句切
    """
    tokenizer = TextTilingTokenizer(w=20, k=10)
    try:
        tiles = tokenizer.tokenize(text)
    except Exception:
        tiles = [p.strip() for p in text.split("\n\n") if p.strip()]

    final = []
    for t in tiles:
        if len(t.split()) <= MAX_WORDS_PAPER:
            final.append(t.strip())
        else:
            final.extend(pack_by_word_limit(split_sentences(t), MAX_WORDS_PAPER))
    return final


def chunk_lecture_notes(text: str):
    """
    讲义策略：
    段落 + 句子混合，粒度中等
    """
    paras = [p.strip() for p in text.split("\n\n") if p.strip()]
    chunks = pack_by_word_limit(paras, MAX_WORDS_NOTES)

    final = []
    for c in chunks:
        if len(c.split()) <= MAX_WORDS_NOTES:
            final.append(c)
        else:
            final.extend(pack_by_word_limit(split_sentences(c), MAX_WORDS_NOTES))
    return final


def is_noisy_chunk(chunk: str):
    words = chunk.split()
    if len(words) < MIN_WORDS_PER_CHUNK:
        return True
    chars = "".join(words)
    alpha_ratio = sum(ch.isalpha() for ch in chars) / max(len(chars), 1)
    if alpha_ratio < 0.6:
        return True
    return False


def main():
    print("=== Stage1 自适应分块 ===")
    pdf_path = ask_pdf_path()
    out_path = ask_output_path()

    # 1) 提取文本
    raw_text = extract_text_from_pdf(pdf_path)
    print(f"[1] 原始词数: {len(raw_text.split())}")

    # 2) 清洗
    text = clean_raw_text(raw_text)
    print(f"[2] 清洗后词数: {len(text.split())}")

    # 3) 类型识别
    doc_type = estimate_doc_type(text)
    print(f"[3] 文档类型识别: {doc_type}")

    # 4) 分块
    if doc_type == "slides":
        chunks = chunk_slides(text)
    elif doc_type == "lecture_notes":
        chunks = chunk_lecture_notes(text)
    else:
        chunks = chunk_paper_book(text)

    before_filter = len(chunks)
    chunks = [c for c in chunks if not is_noisy_chunk(c)]
    after_filter = len(chunks)

    print(f"[4] 分块数（过滤前）: {before_filter}")
    print(f"[5] 分块数（过滤后）: {after_filter}")

    # 5) 输出
    out = []
    for i, c in enumerate(chunks, 1):
        out.append({
            "chunk_id": i,
            "word_count": len(c.split()),
            "doc_type": doc_type,
            "text": c
        })

    out_dir = os.path.dirname(out_path)
    if out_dir:
        os.makedirs(out_dir, exist_ok=True)

    with open(out_path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        json.dump(out, f, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)

    # 另存清洗文本
    text_out = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(out_path) if os.path.dirname(out_path) else os.getcwd(), "stage1_text_cleaned.txt")
    with open(text_out, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        f.write(text)

    print(f"[Saved] {out_path}")
    print(f"[Saved] {text_out}")

    # 预览
    n = min(3, len(chunks))
    print(f"\n--- 前{n}块预览 ---")
    for i in range(n):
        snippet = chunks[i][:220].replace("\n", " ")
        print(f"\n[Chunk {i+1}] words={len(chunks[i].split())}")
        print(snippet + ("..." if len(chunks[i]) > 220 else ""))

    print("\n✅ Stage1 完成，可进入 Stage2")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```

## ## 最稳妥做法（直接替换整个函数）

把你文件里的 `llm_refine_topics` 整段删掉，替换为下面这个：

```python
def llm_refine_topics(client: OpenAI, candidates: List[Dict[str, Any]]) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
    candidate_lines = []
    for c in candidates:
        candidate_lines.append(f"- {c['name']} (freq={c['frequency']}, score={c['avg_score']})")
    cand_text = "\n".join(candidate_lines)

    prompt = f"""
You are refining candidate concepts for a university Machine Learning course knowledge graph.

Candidate phrases:
{cand_text}

Task:
Convert candidates into a practical Topic/Sub-Topic structure for learning graph construction.

Hard constraints:
1) Output between 8 and 15 topics (unless candidates are truly insufficient).
2) Prefer specific technical concepts over overly broad labels.
3) Do NOT keep only generic topics such as "Machine Learning" unless needed as one parent topic.
4) Remove header/footer/institution noise.
5) Merge obvious duplicates/synonyms, but DO NOT over-merge distinct concepts.
6) Topic names must be short noun phrases (1-4 words preferred).
7) Each topic should have 1-3 sub_topics when possible.

Coverage requirements (very important):
- Preserve major concepts that appear in candidates related to:
  neuron, artificial neural network, activation function, loss function,
  backpropagation, linear separation/separability, regression/classification, training.
- If a concept appears in candidates with reasonable frequency/score, try to keep it.

Output JSON only:
{{
  "topics": [
    {{
      "name": "...",
      "description": "1 concise sentence",
      "sub_topics": [
        {{"name": "...", "description": "1 concise sentence"}}
      ]
    }}
  ]
}}
"""

    resp = client.chat.completions.create(
        model=MODEL_NAME,
        messages=[{"role": "user", "content": prompt}],
        response_format={"type": "json_object"},
        temperature=0
    )

    data = json.loads(resp.choices[0].message.content)
    topics = data.get("topics", [])
    if not isinstance(topics, list):
        topics = []

    # 二次补充请求：防止topic过少
    if len(topics) < 8:
        repair_prompt = f"""
You previously returned too few topics ({len(topics)}).
Please expand to 8-15 topics using the same candidates below.
Keep technical granularity and avoid only broad labels.

Candidates:
{cand_text}

Return JSON with key "topics" only.
"""
        resp2 = client.chat.completions.create(
            model=MODEL_NAME,
            messages=[{"role": "user", "content": repair_prompt}],
            response_format={"type": "json_object"},
            temperature=0
        )
        data2 = json.loads(resp2.choices[0].message.content)
        topics2 = data2.get("topics", [])
        if isinstance(topics2, list) and len(topics2) > len(topics):
            topics = topics2

    return topics
```

## ## 新建文件：`stage2b_prerequisites_hybrid.py`

```python
import os
import json
from typing import List, Dict, Any
from openai import OpenAI

# ========= 配置 =========
MODEL_NAME = "gpt-4o-mini"

# 规则回退用（可按你的课程补充）
RULES = [
    ("Linear Algebra", "Gradient Descent", "Gradient updates rely on vector/matrix operations."),
    ("Calculus", "Gradient Descent", "Gradients and derivatives come from calculus."),
    ("Probability", "Bayesian Methods", "Bayesian inference is built on probability theory."),
    ("Neurons", "Artificial Neural Networks", "Neural networks are compositions of neurons."),
    ("Activation Function", "Backpropagation", "Backprop depends on activation derivatives."),
    ("Loss Function", "Backpropagation", "Backprop computes gradients of the loss."),
    ("Backpropagation", "Training", "Training neural nets typically uses backprop."),
]


def ask_input_path():
    p = input("请输入 stage2a_topics_hybrid.json 完整路径：\n> ").strip().strip('"').strip("'")
    return p


def ask_output_path(default_name="stage2b_prerequisites.json"):
    p = input(f"请输入输出JSON路径（回车=当前目录/{default_name}）：\n> ").strip().strip('"').strip("'")
    if not p:
        p = os.path.join(os.getcwd(), default_name)
    return p


def load_topics(path: str) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
    if not os.path.exists(path):
        raise FileNotFoundError(f"找不到输入文件: {path}")

    with open(path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        data = json.load(f)

    # 兼容结构：{"topics":[...]} 或直接 [...]
    if isinstance(data, dict):
        topics = data.get("topics", [])
    elif isinstance(data, list):
        topics = data
    else:
        topics = []

    clean = []
    for t in topics:
        name = (t.get("name") or "").strip()
        if not name:
            continue
        clean.append({
            "name": name,
            "description": (t.get("description") or "").strip()
        })
    return clean


def get_client():
    api_key = os.getenv("OPENAI_API_KEY")
    if not api_key:
        raise EnvironmentError("未检测到 OPENAI_API_KEY")
    return OpenAI(api_key=api_key)


def extract_prereq_with_llm(client: OpenAI, topics: List[Dict[str, Any]]) -> List[Dict[str, str]]:
    topic_lines = "\n".join([f"- {t['name']}: {t['description']}" for t in topics])

    prompt = f"""
You are building a knowledge graph for a machine learning course.

Given topics:
{topic_lines}

Task:
Infer ONLY strong prerequisite relations.
Definition:
A -> B means mastering A is necessary before learning B.

Rules:
1) Output only strong dependencies, not weak associations.
2) Avoid cycles when possible.
3) Use exact topic names from the list.
4) If uncertain, skip it.

Output JSON only:
{{
  "prerequisites": [
    {{
      "from": "Topic A",
      "to": "Topic B",
      "reason": "short reason"
    }}
  ]
}}
"""

    resp = client.chat.completions.create(
        model=MODEL_NAME,
        messages=[{"role": "user", "content": prompt}],
        response_format={"type": "json_object"},
        temperature=0
    )
    data = json.loads(resp.choices[0].message.content)
    rels = data.get("prerequisites", [])
    if not isinstance(rels, list):
        rels = []
    return rels


def extract_prereq_with_rules(topics: List[Dict[str, Any]]) -> List[Dict[str, str]]:
    names = {t["name"] for t in topics}
    rels = []

    # 简单大小写宽松匹配
    def has_topic(target):
        target_low = target.lower()
        for n in names:
            if n.lower() == target_low:
                return n
        return None

    for a, b, reason in RULES:
        aa = has_topic(a)
        bb = has_topic(b)
        if aa and bb and aa != bb:
            rels.append({"from": aa, "to": bb, "reason": reason})

    return rels


def normalize_and_filter(rels: List[Dict[str, str]], valid_names: set) -> List[Dict[str, str]]:
    out = []
    seen = set()

    for r in rels:
        a = (r.get("from") or "").strip()
        b = (r.get("to") or "").strip()
        reason = (r.get("reason") or "").strip()

        if not a or not b or a == b:
            continue
        if a not in valid_names or b not in valid_names:
            continue

        key = (a.lower(), b.lower())
        if key in seen:
            continue
        seen.add(key)

        out.append({
            "from": a,
            "to": b,
            "reason": reason if reason else "Prerequisite relation inferred."
        })

    return out


def main():
    print("=== Stage 2b: 先修关系（LLM优先 + 规则回退）===")

    in_path = ask_input_path()
    out_path = ask_output_path()

    topics = load_topics(in_path)
    print(f"[1] 读取Topic数量: {len(topics)}")
    if len(topics) < 2:
        raise ValueError("Topic数量不足，无法推断先修关系。")

    names = {t["name"] for t in topics}

    method = "llm"
    rels = []
    try:
        client = get_client()
        rels = extract_prereq_with_llm(client, topics)
        print("[2] LLM先修关系提取完成")
    except Exception as e:
        msg = str(e)
        if ("insufficient_quota" in msg) or ("429" in msg) or ("RateLimitError" in msg):
            print("[Warn] LLM额度不足/限流，回退规则法")
        else:
            print(f"[Warn] LLM调用失败，回退规则法: {e}")
        method = "rules_fallback"
        rels = extract_prereq_with_rules(topics)

    rels = normalize_and_filter(rels, names)
    print(f"[3] 最终先修关系数量: {len(rels)} | method={method}")

    output = {
        "stage": "2b",
        "method": method,
        "topic_count": len(topics),
        "prerequisite_count": len(rels),
        "prerequisites": rels
    }

    out_dir = os.path.dirname(out_path)
    if out_dir:
        os.makedirs(out_dir, exist_ok=True)

    with open(out_path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        json.dump(output, f, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)

    print(f"[Saved] {out_path}")

    print("\n--- Prerequisite Preview (Top 15) ---")
    for i, r in enumerate(rels[:15], 1):
        print(f"{i:02d}. {r['from']} -> {r['to']} | {r['reason']}")

    print("\n✅ Stage 2b 完成")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```

输入文件时填你现在的：  
`stage2a_topics_hybrid.json`

[06/15/2026 18:57:42 +00:00] MI, YU:
我的2a跑到现在还没跑出来合理吗？我这个kg做出来是可以同时起到知识图谱的作用和rag的作用的吧？

Response:
## **先答你两个问题**

### **1) 2a 跑很久合理吗？**
**看步骤。**  
- **KeyBERT召回**：对你这个体量（17 chunks）通常几分钟内。  
- **LLM精修**：一般几十秒到几分钟。  
如果“很久”是 **10-20分钟以上**，多半是：
- 卡在模型下载/CPU推理（首次）
- 或代码里有重复请求/重试循环
- 或网络/API请求阻塞

你已经跑出过结果，所以现在“很久”不太正常，建议先看是否卡在哪一段日志（KeyBERT还是LLM）。

---

### **2) 这个KG能同时做知识图谱+RAG吗？**
**可以，而且很适合。**  
你这条链路天然支持双用途：

- **KG作用**：节点（Topic）、边（先修/相似），用于路径学习、课程结构、依赖推理。
- **RAG作用**：把 chunk 作为检索文档，把 topic 作为索引标签/路由条件，检索后再生成答案。

简单架构是：
1. 文档 chunk 入向量库（RAG检索）
2. KG存 topic/relations（导航与约束）
3. 查询时先走 KG 找相关主题，再去向量库检索对应 chunks，最后 LLM 回答

这叫 **Graph-RAG**（或 KG-augmented RAG）。

---

## **我看了你上传的两个文件，给你结论（简要）**
你现在这个阶段是：
- Stage1 有产出（chunks存在）
- Stage2a 有产出（topics存在）

但你之前表现出来的问题是 **2a过度合并**（topic太少、太抽象）。  
这会影响 KG 和 RAG 两边：
- KG：节点太少，图太稀疏
- RAG：topic路由不精准

---

## **给你今天就能落地的建议（最短路径）**

1. **先把2a结果门槛设定**：topic 数至少 `8-15`  
2. 若 `<8`，触发二次LLM精修（你已加）  
3. 直接进入2b/2c先跑通全链路  
4. 最后回头调2a质量（不要卡死在2a）

---

如果你愿意，我下一条可以直接给你一个**“2a质量门禁脚本”**：  
自动检查 `topics_count / 平均topic长度 / 泛化词占比`，不达标就提示重跑参数。

# ## 给你 Stage 2c：相似关系（可直接运行版）

新建文件：`stage2c_similarity.py`

```python
import os
import json
import numpy as np
from typing import List, Dict, Any
from sentence_transformers import SentenceTransformer

# ========= 配置 =========
SIM_THRESHOLD = 0.72
TOP_K_PER_TOPIC = 5
MODEL_NAME = "paraphrase-multilingual-MiniLM-L12-v2"


def ask_input_path():
    p = input("请输入 stage2a_topics_hybrid.json 完整路径：\n> ").strip().strip('"').strip("'")
    return p


def ask_output_path(default_name="stage2c_similarity_edges.json"):
    p = input(f"请输入输出JSON路径（回车=当前目录/{default_name}）：\n> ").strip().strip('"').strip("'")
    if not p:
        p = os.path.join(os.getcwd(), default_name)
    return p


def load_topics(path: str) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
    if not os.path.exists(path):
        raise FileNotFoundError(f"找不到输入文件: {path}")

    with open(path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        data = json.load(f)

    if isinstance(data, dict):
        topics = data.get("topics", [])
    elif isinstance(data, list):
        topics = data
    else:
        topics = []

    clean = []
    for t in topics:
        name = (t.get("name") or "").strip()
        if not name:
            continue
        clean.append({
            "name": name,
            "description": (t.get("description") or "").strip()
        })
    return clean


def build_topic_text(t: Dict[str, str]) -> str:
    # 用 name + description 做语义表示
    if t["description"]:
        return f"{t['name']}. {t['description']}"
    return t["name"]


def cosine_sim_matrix(embeddings: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
    # embeddings 已归一化时，点积=余弦相似度
    return np.matmul(embeddings, embeddings.T)


def compute_similarity_edges(topics: List[Dict[str, str]], threshold=0.72, top_k=5):
    texts = [build_topic_text(t) for t in topics]
    names = [t["name"] for t in topics]

    model = SentenceTransformer(MODEL_NAME)
    embs = model.encode(texts, normalize_embeddings=True)  # shape: [n, d]
    sim = cosine_sim_matrix(np.array(embs))

    n = len(names)
    edges = []

    # 为了防止太密，按每个topic只保留top_k个高相似邻居
    for i in range(n):
        pairs = []
        for j in range(n):
            if i == j:
                continue
            s = float(sim[i, j])
            if s >= threshold:
                pairs.append((j, s))

        # 每个点只取 top_k
        pairs = sorted(pairs, key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)[:top_k]

        for j, s in pairs:
            a, b = names[i], names[j]
            # 用有序键去重（无向相似）
            u, v = sorted([a, b])
            edges.append((u, v, s))

    # 全局去重：同一对只保留最高分
    best = {}
    for u, v, s in edges:
        key = (u, v)
        if key not in best or s > best[key]:
            best[key] = s

    final_edges = [
        {"from": k[0], "to": k[1], "similarity": round(v, 4)}
        for k, v in best.items()
    ]
    final_edges.sort(key=lambda x: x["similarity"], reverse=True)
    return final_edges


def main():
    print("=== Stage 2c: Topic相似关系（SBERT）===")

    in_path = ask_input_path()
    out_path = ask_output_path()

    topics = load_topics(in_path)
    print(f"[1] 读取Topic数量: {len(topics)}")
    if len(topics) < 2:
        raise ValueError("Topic数量不足，无法计算相似关系")

    edges = compute_similarity_edges(
        topics=topics,
        threshold=SIM_THRESHOLD,
        top_k=TOP_K_PER_TOPIC
    )
    print(f"[2] 相似关系数量: {len(edges)} (threshold={SIM_THRESHOLD}, top_k={TOP_K_PER_TOPIC})")

    output = {
        "stage": "2c",
        "method": "sbert_cosine",
        "model": MODEL_NAME,
        "topic_count": len(topics),
        "similarity_count": len(edges),
        "threshold": SIM_THRESHOLD,
        "top_k_per_topic": TOP_K_PER_TOPIC,
        "similarity_edges": edges
    }

    out_dir = os.path.dirname(out_path)
    if out_dir:
        os.makedirs(out_dir, exist_ok=True)

    with open(out_path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        json.dump(output, f, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)

    print(f"[Saved] {out_path}")

    print("\n--- Similarity Preview (Top 15) ---")
    for i, e in enumerate(edges[:15], 1):
        print(f"{i:02d}. {e['from']} <-> {e['to']} | sim={e['similarity']}")

    print("\n✅ Stage 2c 完成")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```

## ## 一、先判断“卡住”还是“慢”

### **1）看终端是否还有进度打印**
如果长期没有新日志，可能卡在：
- LLM请求
- SBERT编码
- 文件读写/JSON大对象处理

### **2）看 CPU/内存**
打开任务管理器：
- Python CPU持续有占用：通常是慢，不是死
- CPU接近0并持续很久：可能阻塞/卡住

### **3）加“心跳日志”**
每步前后都打印时间戳。

---

## ## 二、给你一个通用“防卡模板”（直接加到脚本顶部）

```python
import time

def tic(msg):
    print(f"\n[START] {msg} | {time.strftime('%H:%M:%S')}")

def toc(msg, t0):
    print(f"[DONE ] {msg} | cost={time.time()-t0:.2f}s | {time.strftime('%H:%M:%S')}")
```

用法：

```python
t0 = time.time(); tic("load topics")
topics = load_topics(path)
toc("load topics", t0)
```

---

## ## 三、你当前最可能慢的点 & 提速方法

### **A) Stage2a 混合版慢**
- KeyBERT CPU推理慢：把
  - `TOP_N_PER_CHUNK` 调到 `4~5`
  - `nr_candidates` 调到 `10~15`
  - `MAX_CANDIDATES_FOR_LLM` 调到 `40`
- LLM慢：只做一次精修，不要多轮重试

### **B) Stage2c 慢**
- topic多时两两相似是 $$O(n^2)$$  
  提速：
  - 先限制 topic 数（例如前 50）
  - `TOP_K_PER_TOPIC=3`
  - 阈值提高到 `0.75`

---

import json, time

p1 = r"/mnt/data/stage1_chunks.json"  # 本地请换你路径

    c=json.load(f)

print("topics:", len(topics), "cost", time.time()-t)

## ## 为什么会这样（你这个 case 很常见）

你的 9 个 topic 里有很多跨类型概念（理论+应用）：

- Reinforcement Learning …

它们语义距离本来就可能不够近，尤其只用短标题做 embedding 时。

## ## Part A：先跑通“合图展示”（本周必须完成）

新建文件：`stage4_build_and_visualize_kg.py`

```python
import os
import json
import networkx as nx
import matplotlib.pyplot as plt


def ask_path(prompt):
    return input(prompt + "\n> ").strip().strip('"').strip("'")


def load_json(path):
    if not os.path.exists(path):
        raise FileNotFoundError(f"文件不存在: {path}")
    with open(path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        return json.load(f)


def extract_topics(data):
    # 兼容 {"topics":[...]} 或直接 [...]
    if isinstance(data, dict):
        topics = data.get("topics", [])
    elif isinstance(data, list):
        topics = data
    else:
        topics = []

    out = []
    for t in topics:
        name = (t.get("name") or "").strip()
        if not name:
            continue
        out.append({
            "name": name,
            "description": (t.get("description") or "").strip(),
            "sub_topics": t.get("sub_topics", [])
        })
    return out


def extract_prereq(data):
    if isinstance(data, dict):
        rels = data.get("prerequisites", [])
    else:
        rels = data
    return rels if isinstance(rels, list) else []


def extract_similarity(data):
    if isinstance(data, dict):
        rels = data.get("similarity_edges", [])
    else:
        rels = data
    return rels if isinstance(rels, list) else []


def build_graph(topics, prereq, sim):
    G = nx.DiGraph()

    # 节点
    for t in topics:
        G.add_node(
            t["name"],
            description=t["description"],
            sub_topics=t["sub_topics"]
        )

    # 先修边（有向）
    for r in prereq:
        a = (r.get("from") or "").strip()
        b = (r.get("to") or "").strip()
        if a in G.nodes and b in G.nodes and a != b:
            G.add_edge(a, b, relation="prerequisite", reason=r.get("reason", ""))

    # 相似边（无向语义，放入有向图里用双向或单向都可；这里单向避免过密）
    for e in sim:
        a = (e.get("from") or "").strip()
        b = (e.get("to") or "").strip()
        s = float(e.get("similarity", 0))
        if a in G.nodes and b in G.nodes and a != b:
            if not G.has_edge(a, b):
                G.add_edge(a, b, relation="similarity", score=s)

    return G


def visualize_prerequisite(G, out_png="kg_prerequisite.png"):
    H = nx.DiGraph()
    for u, v, d in G.edges(data=True):
        if d.get("relation") == "prerequisite":
            H.add_edge(u, v)

    if H.number_of_edges() == 0:
        print("[Info] 先修边为0，跳过先修图绘制。")
        return

    plt.figure(figsize=(12, 8))
    pos = nx.spring_layout(H, seed=42, k=1.2)
    nx.draw_networkx_nodes(H, pos, node_size=1800)
    nx.draw_networkx_edges(H, pos, arrows=True)
    nx.draw_networkx_labels(H, pos, font_size=9)
    plt.title("Prerequisite Graph")
    plt.axis("off")
    plt.tight_layout()
    plt.savefig(out_png, dpi=220)
    plt.close()
    print(f"[Saved] {out_png}")


def visualize_similarity(G, out_png="kg_similarity.png"):
    H = nx.Graph()
    for n in G.nodes:
        H.add_node(n)

    for u, v, d in G.edges(data=True):
        if d.get("relation") == "similarity":
            H.add_edge(u, v, weight=d.get("score", 0.0))

    if H.number_of_edges() == 0:
        print("[Info] 相似边为0，跳过相似图绘制。")
        return

    plt.figure(figsize=(12, 8))
    pos = nx.spring_layout(H, seed=42, k=1.2)
    widths = [1 + 4 * H[u][v]["weight"] for u, v in H.edges()]
    nx.draw_networkx_nodes(H, pos, node_size=1800)
    nx.draw_networkx_edges(H, pos, width=widths)
    nx.draw_networkx_labels(H, pos, font_size=9)
    plt.title("Similarity Graph")
    plt.axis("off")
    plt.tight_layout()
    plt.savefig(out_png, dpi=220)
    plt.close()
    print(f"[Saved] {out_png}")


def main():
    print("=== Stage 4: 合图与可视化 ===")

    p_topics = ask_path("请输入 stage2a_topics_hybrid.json 路径")
    p_prereq = ask_path("请输入 stage2b_prerequisites.json 路径")
    p_sim = ask_path("请输入 stage2c_similarity_edges.json 路径")

    topics = extract_topics(load_json(p_topics))
    prereq = extract_prereq(load_json(p_prereq))
    sim = extract_similarity(load_json(p_sim))

    print(f"[1] Topics: {len(topics)}")
    print(f"[2] Prerequisites: {len(prereq)}")
    print(f"[3] Similarity edges: {len(sim)}")

    G = build_graph(topics, prereq, sim)
    print(f"[4] Graph nodes={G.number_of_nodes()}, edges={G.number_of_edges()}")

    nx.write_gexf(G, "knowledge_graph.gexf")
    print("[Saved] knowledge_graph.gexf")

    # 也存一份json，方便网站读取
    graph_json = {
        "nodes": [
            {"id": n, **G.nodes[n]} for n in G.nodes
        ],
        "edges": [
            {"from": u, "to": v, **d} for u, v, d in G.edges(data=True)
        ]
    }
    with open("knowledge_graph.json", "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        json.dump(graph_json, f, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)
    print("[Saved] knowledge_graph.json")

    visualize_prerequisite(G)
    visualize_similarity(G)

    print("\n✅ 合图完成，可用于Demo展示")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```

运行：

```powershell

```

Response:
# ## 定位到了：这是 `GEXF` 不支持你节点属性里的“复杂类型”导致的 ✅

报错栈在 `nx.write_gexf()`，通常是因为节点属性里有 `list/dict`（比如你的 `sub_topics`）  
GEXF 只稳定支持标量（字符串/数字/布尔）。

---

## ## 你现在立刻这样改（最小修复）

在 `build_graph()` 里加一层“属性扁平化”：

把这段（你原来）：

```python
G.add_node(
    t["name"],
    description=t["description"],
    sub_topics=t["sub_topics"]
)
```

改成：

```python
sub_topics_text = "; ".join(
    [s.get("name", "") for s in t.get("sub_topics", []) if isinstance(s, dict)]
)

G.add_node(
    t["name"],
    description=str(t.get("description", "")),
    sub_topics=sub_topics_text
)
```

---

## ## 再保险：边属性也转成标量字符串/数字

先修边：

```python
G.add_edge(a, b, relation="prerequisite", reason=str(r.get("reason", "")))
```

相似边：

```python
G.add_edge(a, b, relation="similarity", score=float(s))
```

---

## ## 如果你想最稳（推荐）

在写 GEXF 前做一次清洗函数：

```python
def sanitize_graph_for_gexf(G):
    H = nx.DiGraph()
    for n, d in G.nodes(data=True):
        clean_d = {}
        for k, v in d.items():
            if isinstance(v, (str, int, float, bool)) or v is None:
                clean_d[k] = v
            else:
                clean_d[k] = json.dumps(v, ensure_ascii=False)
        H.add_node(n, **clean_d)

    for u, v, d in G.edges(data=True):
        clean_d = {}
        for k, val in d.items():
            if isinstance(val, (str, int, float, bool)) or val is None:
                clean_d[k] = val
            else:
                clean_d[k] = json.dumps(val, ensure_ascii=False)
        H.add_edge(u, v, **clean_d)
    return H
```

然后：

```python
G_safe = sanitize_graph_for_gexf(G)
nx.write_gexf(G_safe, "knowledge_graph.gexf")
```

---

知识图谱通过一个分阶段的混合式流水线从教育文档自动构建。首先，在 **Stage 1** 中采用文档类型自适应分块策略：对原始 PDF 文本进行清洗（页眉页脚噪声过滤、粘连词修复、空白归一化），并按文档体裁选择分块方式（slides 使用段落窗口分块，论文/书刊使用 TextTiling 语义分块并结合长度约束，讲义使用段落与句子混合分块），从而在保持语义连贯性的同时控制块长度。其次，在 **Stage 2** 中进行实体与关系抽取：先用 KeyBERT 进行候选概念召回，再用 GPT-4o-mini 对候选进行结构化精修（Topic/Sub-topic 归并、去噪、短语规范化）；先修关系由 GPT-4o-mini 推断（额度不足时回退规则法），相似关系由 Sentence-BERT 余弦相似度计算得到（阈值可调，默认中高阈值并支持阈值扫描）。最后，在图构建阶段融合 Topic 节点、先修边和相似边，导出 NetworkX 图及可视化结果，用于后续教学智能体调用。图中包含两类关键关系：先修关系支持 Planning Agent 的学习路径规划，相似关系支持 Adaptation Agent 的补救内容与邻近知识推荐。

[06/16/2026 13:07:22 +00:00] MI, YU:
在这个方案里，是否考虑难度等级？如何做到难度等级的适配？

Response:
# ## 有考虑，而且你这个方案**非常适合做难度适配** ✅

你目前流程里已经有可用基础（topics、先修边、相似边），再补一层“难度建模”就完整了。

---

## ## 1）现在方案里是否已有难度信息？

严格说：**部分有、但还不完整**。

- 你原设计里 Stage 3 有 `difficulty_level` 字段（1-5）
- 但你当前实际跑通版本重点在 2a/2b/2c，Stage 3 可能还没系统接入

所以现在是“可加速落地”的状态。

---

## ## 2）如何做难度等级适配（推荐最小可用方案）

给每个知识点节点一个难度分：

$$
\text{difficulty}(v)\in\{1,2,3,4,5\}
$$

可由三部分融合：

$$
\text{difficulty}(v)=\alpha \cdot d_{\text{llm}}+\beta \cdot d_{\text{graph}}+\gamma \cdot d_{\text{text}}
$$

其中：

- $$d_{\text{llm}}$$：LLM给出的主观难度（如Stage 3摘要）
- $$d_{\text{graph}}$$：图结构难度（如入度先修多、路径深）
- $$d_{\text{text}}$$：文本复杂度（术语密度、句长等）

常用初始权重可设：$$\alpha=0.5,\beta=0.3,\gamma=0.2$$。

---

## ## 3）在你的两个 Agent 里怎么用

- **Planning Agent（先修图）**  
  从当前能力等级出发，只推荐“可达且难度不跳级”的节点。  
  例如限制下一步满足：
  $$
  \text{difficulty}_{next}\le \text{difficulty}_{current}+1
  $$

- **Adaptation Agent（相似图）**  
  若当前节点太难，跳到相似且难度更低的补救节点：
  $$
  \text{similarity}(u,v)\ge \tau,\quad \text{difficulty}(v)<\text{difficulty}(u)
  $$

---

## ## 4）你这周可直接实现的“轻量版”

1. 在 `topics` 里新增字段：`difficulty_level`（先用 LLM 或规则打1-5）  
2. 先修路径规划时加“难度约束”  
3. 相似推荐时优先“相似且更低难度”  
4. Demo里展示同一topic的“标准路径 vs 降难路径”

---

## ## 5）给导师可说的一句话

> 是的，方案已支持难度适配：我们在节点层引入难度分级，并在先修路径规划与相似补救推荐中同时施加难度约束，实现个性化、可渐进的学习路径。

# ## 为什么会“跳过 Stage 3”？

- Stage 1 分块  
- Stage 2a 主题抽取  
- Stage 2b 先修关系  
- Stage 2c 相似关系  
- Stage 4 合图导出

而 **Stage 3（节点摘要/难度等级）是增强层**，不影响“图能不能先构出来”，所以通常会被暂时后置。

---

## ## 具体到你这次，常见原因有 3 个

1. **时间优先级**：你要在两天内先交一个可运行 KG demo  
2. **API成本/稳定性**：Stage 3 需要对每个 topic 再调一次 LLM，调用次数会增加  
3. **脚本拆分**：你现在的 stage4 合图脚本没有强依赖 `summary` 字段，所以不会报错，也就“自然跳过”了

---

## ## 结论

不是你做错了，而是你在做一个合理的工程取舍：  
先保证 **结构图跑通**，再补 **教学属性层**（difficulty、time、misconceptions）。

---

# ## 1）**可直接运行的 Stage 3 代码**（LLM优先 + 本地回退）

> 输入：`stage2a_topics_hybrid.json`  
> 输出：`stage3_topics_with_summary.json`（每个节点带难度等摘要）

新建文件：`stage3_node_summary_hybrid.py`

```python
import os
import json
from typing import List, Dict, Any
from openai import OpenAI

MODEL_NAME = "gpt-4o-mini"

def ask_input_path():
    p = input("请输入 stage2a_topics_hybrid.json 路径：\n> ").strip().strip('"').strip("'")
    return p

def ask_output_path(default_name="stage3_topics_with_summary.json"):
    p = input(f"请输入输出路径（回车=当前目录/{default_name}）：\n> ").strip().strip('"').strip("'")
    if not p:
        p = os.path.join(os.getcwd(), default_name)
    return p

def load_topics(path: str) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
    with open(path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        data = json.load(f)
    topics = data.get("topics", data if isinstance(data, list) else [])
    clean = []
    for t in topics:
        name = (t.get("name") or "").strip()
        if not name:
            continue
        clean.append({
            "name": name,
            "description": (t.get("description") or "").strip(),
            "sub_topics": t.get("sub_topics", [])
        })
    return clean

def get_client():
    api_key = os.getenv("OPENAI_API_KEY")
    if not api_key:
        raise EnvironmentError("未检测到 OPENAI_API_KEY")
    return OpenAI(api_key=api_key)

def llm_summary(client: OpenAI, topic: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    prompt = f"""
You are generating a structured learning summary for a machine learning concept.

Concept name: {topic['name']}
Description: {topic.get('description','')}

Return JSON only:
{{
  "difficulty_level": 1,
  "target_audience": "Undergraduate",
  "estimated_learning_time": "1-2 hours",
  "prerequisites_summary": "...",
  "key_sub_concepts": ["...", "..."],
  "common_misconceptions": ["...", "..."],
  "practical_applications": ["...", "..."]
}}

Constraints:
- difficulty_level must be integer 1-5
- concise and educational
"""
    resp = client.chat.completions.create(
        model=MODEL_NAME,
        messages=[{"role": "user", "content": prompt}],
        response_format={"type": "json_object"},
        temperature=0
    )
    return json.loads(resp.choices[0].message.content)

def fallback_summary(topic: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    name = topic["name"].lower()
    hard_kw = ["markov", "eigen", "reinforcement", "bayesian", "optimization", "backpropagation"]
    diff = 3
    if any(k in name for k in hard_kw):
        diff = 4
    return {
        "difficulty_level": diff,
        "target_audience": "Undergraduate",
        "estimated_learning_time": "1-2 hours",
        "prerequisites_summary": "Basic probability, linear algebra, and introductory ML are recommended.",
        "key_sub_concepts": [s.get("name","") for s in topic.get("sub_topics", [])[:3] if s.get("name")],
        "common_misconceptions": ["Memorizing definitions without understanding assumptions."],
        "practical_applications": ["Course assignments", "Model analysis tasks"]
    }

def main():
    print("=== Stage 3: Node Summary (LLM + Fallback) ===")
    in_path = ask_input_path()
    out_path = ask_output_path()

    topics = load_topics(in_path)
    print(f"[1] 读取Topic数量: {len(topics)}")

    method = "llm"
    try:
        client = get_client()
        for i, t in enumerate(topics, 1):
            print(f"[LLM] {i}/{len(topics)} {t['name']}")
            try:
                t["summary"] = llm_summary(client, t)
            except Exception as e:
                print(f"[Warn] 单节点LLM失败，回退: {t['name']} | {e}")
                t["summary"] = fallback_summary(t)
                method = "hybrid_partial_fallback"
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"[Warn] LLM不可用，全部回退: {e}")
        method = "rules_fallback"
        for t in topics:
            t["summary"] = fallback_summary(t)

    output = {
        "stage": "3",
        "method": method,
        "topics_count": len(topics),
        "topics": topics
    }

    with open(out_path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        json.dump(output, f, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)

    print(f"[Saved] {out_path}")
    print("✅ Stage 3 完成")

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```

运行：
```powershell
python "d:/ic/master project/project_code/KG_construction/stage3_node_summary_hybrid.py"
```
