# ## 1) 可运行的 **Streamlit `app.py` 完整代码**

> 这是“明天可展示版”：  
> - 批量上传 PDF  
> - 调用你已有 pipeline 脚本（按顺序跑）  
> - 汇总显示节点/边数量  
> - 下载最终 `knowledge_graph.gexf/json`  
> - 展示先修图/相似图（如果有 png）

请新建 `app.py`：

```python
import os
import json
import shutil
import subprocess
from pathlib import Path
import streamlit as st

# ========= 你的项目根目录（按需修改） =========
PROJECT_DIR = Path(r"D:\ic\master project\project_code\KG_construction")
PYTHON_EXE = PROJECT_DIR / ".venv" / "Scripts" / "python.exe"

# 你已有脚本（按需改文件名）
STAGE1_SCRIPT = PROJECT_DIR / "stage1_adaptive_chunking.py"
STAGE2A_SCRIPT = PROJECT_DIR / "stage2a_hybrid_keybert_llm.py"
STAGE2B_SCRIPT = PROJECT_DIR / "stage2b_prerequisites_hybrid.py"
STAGE2C_SCRIPT = PROJECT_DIR / "stage2c_similarity.py"
STAGE3_SCRIPT = PROJECT_DIR / "stage3_node_summary_hybrid.py"
STAGE4_SCRIPT = PROJECT_DIR / "stage4_build_and_visualize_kg.py"

DATA_DIR = PROJECT_DIR / "web_data"
UPLOAD_DIR = DATA_DIR / "uploads"
RUN_DIR = DATA_DIR / "runs"
GLOBAL_DIR = DATA_DIR / "global"

for d in [UPLOAD_DIR, RUN_DIR, GLOBAL_DIR]:
    d.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)


def run_script_with_inputs(script_path: Path, inputs: list[str], cwd: Path):
    """
    用stdin把“交互输入”喂给你现有脚本
    """
    if not script_path.exists():
        return 1, f"[Error] Script not found: {script_path}"

    input_text = "\n".join(inputs) + "\n"
    try:
        p = subprocess.run(
            [str(PYTHON_EXE), str(script_path)],
            input=input_text,
            text=True,
            capture_output=True,
            cwd=str(cwd)
        )
        log = f"--- STDOUT ---\n{p.stdout}\n\n--- STDERR ---\n{p.stderr}"
        return p.returncode, log
    except Exception as e:
        return 1, f"Subprocess error: {e}"


def read_json_safe(path: Path):
    if not path.exists():
        return None
    try:
        with open(path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            return json.load(f)
    except Exception:
        return None


def merge_global_kg(global_json_path: Path, doc_kg_json_path: Path):
    """
    简单合并全局图（按节点id去重，边三元组去重）
    """
    g = read_json_safe(global_json_path)
    d = read_json_safe(doc_kg_json_path)

    if d is None:
        return {"nodes": [], "edges": []}

    if g is None:
        with open(global_json_path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            json.dump(d, f, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)
        return d

    node_map = {n["id"]: n for n in g.get("nodes", [])}
    for n in d.get("nodes", []):
        node_map[n["id"]] = n

    edge_set = set()
    edges = []
    for e in g.get("edges", []) + d.get("edges", []):
        key = (e.get("from"), e.get("to"), e.get("relation"))
        if key not in edge_set:
            edge_set.add(key)
            edges.append(e)

    merged = {"nodes": list(node_map.values()), "edges": edges}
    with open(global_json_path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        json.dump(merged, f, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)
    return merged


st.set_page_config(page_title="KG Builder Demo", layout="wide")
st.title("📚 KG Builder Demo (Batch Upload + Multi-Doc Knowledge Base)")

st.markdown(
    """
    这个页面用于：
    1) 批量上传 PDF  
    2) 调用现有 Stage1~Stage4 脚本  
    3) 自动汇总到全局知识库/知识图谱
    """
)

uploaded_files = st.file_uploader(
    "上传一个或多个 PDF",
    type=["pdf"],
    accept_multiple_files=True
)

col1, col2 = st.columns([1, 1])

run_btn = col1.button("🚀 Run Pipeline For Uploaded PDFs")
show_global_btn = col2.button("📊 Show Global Stats")

if run_btn:
    if not uploaded_files:
        st.warning("请先上传至少一个PDF。")
    else:
        for up in uploaded_files:
            doc_name = Path(up.name).stem
            doc_dir = RUN_DIR / doc_name
            doc_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

            pdf_path = doc_dir / up.name
            with open(pdf_path, "wb") as f:
                f.write(up.read())

            st.subheader(f"Processing: {up.name}")
            log_box = st.empty()

            # Stage1
            stage1_out = doc_dir / "stage1_chunks.json"
            code, log = run_script_with_inputs(
                STAGE1_SCRIPT,
                [str(pdf_path), str(stage1_out)],
                cwd=PROJECT_DIR
            )
            log_box.text(log[-3000:])
            if code != 0:
                st.error("Stage1 failed")
                continue

            # Stage2a
            stage2a_out = doc_dir / "stage2a_topics_hybrid.json"
            code, log = run_script_with_inputs(
                STAGE2A_SCRIPT,
                [str(stage1_out), str(stage2a_out)],
                cwd=PROJECT_DIR
            )
            log_box.text(log[-3000:])
            if code != 0:
                st.error("Stage2a failed")
                continue

            # Stage2b
            stage2b_out = doc_dir / "stage2b_prerequisites.json"
            code, log = run_script_with_inputs(
                STAGE2B_SCRIPT,
                [str(stage2a_out), str(stage2b_out)],
                cwd=PROJECT_DIR
            )
            log_box.text(log[-3000:])
            if code != 0:
                st.error("Stage2b failed")
                continue

            # Stage2c
            stage2c_out = doc_dir / "stage2c_similarity_edges.json"
            code, log = run_script_with_inputs(
                STAGE2C_SCRIPT,
                [str(stage2a_out), str(stage2c_out)],
                cwd=PROJECT_DIR
            )
            log_box.text(log[-3000:])
            if code != 0:
                st.error("Stage2c failed")
                continue

            # Stage3
            stage3_out = doc_dir / "stage3_topics_with_summary.json"
            code, log = run_script_with_inputs(
                STAGE3_SCRIPT,
                [str(stage2a_out), str(stage3_out)],
                cwd=PROJECT_DIR
            )
            log_box.text(log[-3000:])
            if code != 0:
                st.error("Stage3 failed")
                continue

            # Stage4（这里让它读stage3）
            # 你需要把stage4脚本改成接收 stage3 topics + stage2b + stage2c
            code, log = run_script_with_inputs(
                STAGE4_SCRIPT,
                [str(stage3_out), str(stage2b_out), str(stage2c_out)],
                cwd=doc_dir
            )
            log_box.text(log[-3000:])
            if code != 0:
                st.error("Stage4 failed")
                continue

            # 把结果汇总到global
            doc_kg_json = doc_dir / "knowledge_graph.json"
            global_kg_json = GLOBAL_DIR / "global_knowledge_graph.json"
            merged = merge_global_kg(global_kg_json, doc_kg_json)

            st.success(f"✅ {up.name} done. Nodes={len(merged['nodes'])}, Edges={len(merged['edges'])}")

st.divider()

if show_global_btn:
    global_kg_json = GLOBAL_DIR / "global_knowledge_graph.json"
    g = read_json_safe(global_kg_json)

    if not g:
        st.info("全局图谱为空，请先运行至少一个PDF。")
    else:
        st.metric("Global Nodes", len(g.get("nodes", [])))
        st.metric("Global Edges", len(g.get("edges", [])))

        st.subheader("Sample Nodes")
        st.json(g.get("nodes", [])[:5])

        st.subheader("Sample Edges")
        st.json(g.get("edges", [])[:5])

        st.download_button(
            "⬇️ Download global_knowledge_graph.json",
            data=json.dumps(g, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2),
            file_name="global_knowledge_graph.json",
            mime="application/json"
        )
```

# ## 3) 有 Stage3 之后怎么合图跑？

关键变化只有一点：  
**Stage4 的 topics 输入从 Stage2a 改成 Stage3 文件。**

也就是你在运行 Stage4 时输入：

1. `stage3_topics_with_summary.json`（不是 stage2a）
2. `stage2b_prerequisites.json`
3. `stage2c_similarity_edges.json`

并且在 Stage4 构图里加上：

```python
summary = t.get("summary", {})
G.add_node(
    t["name"],
    description=t.get("description",""),
    difficulty_level=summary.get("difficulty_level", None),
    estimated_learning_time=summary.get("estimated_learning_time", ""),
    target_audience=summary.get("target_audience", ""),
    prerequisites_summary=summary.get("prerequisites_summary", "")
)
```

> 注意：写 GEXF 时不要存 `list/dict` 原样，先转字符串（你之前报错就是这个点）。

  - `kg_similarity.png`（如果有相似边）

## ## 新建文件：`stage4_build_and_visualize_kg_v2.py`

```python
import os
import json
import networkx as nx
import matplotlib.pyplot as plt


def ask_path(prompt):
    return input(prompt + "\n> ").strip().strip('"').strip("'")


def load_json(path):
    if not os.path.exists(path):
        raise FileNotFoundError(f"文件不存在: {path}")
    with open(path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        return json.load(f)


def extract_topics_with_summary(data):
    """
    兼容:
    - {"topics":[...]}
    - [...]
    每个topic可能包含 summary 字段
    """
    if isinstance(data, dict):
        topics = data.get("topics", [])
    elif isinstance(data, list):
        topics = data
    else:
        topics = []

    out = []
    for t in topics:
        name = (t.get("name") or "").strip()
        if not name:
            continue

        summary = t.get("summary", {})
        if not isinstance(summary, dict):
            summary = {}

        out.append({
            "name": name,
            "description": (t.get("description") or "").strip(),
            "sub_topics": t.get("sub_topics", []),
            "summary": summary
        })
    return out


def extract_prereq(data):
    if isinstance(data, dict):
        rels = data.get("prerequisites", [])
    else:
        rels = data
    return rels if isinstance(rels, list) else []


def extract_similarity(data):
    if isinstance(data, dict):
        rels = data.get("similarity_edges", [])
    else:
        rels = data
    return rels if isinstance(rels, list) else []


def safe_str(v):
    """把复杂对象安全转字符串，防止GEXF报错"""
    if isinstance(v, (str, int, float, bool)) or v is None:
        return v
    return json.dumps(v, ensure_ascii=False)


def build_graph(topics, prereq, sim):
    G = nx.DiGraph()

    # 节点
    for t in topics:
        summary = t.get("summary", {})

        # 常用字段尽量拆成标量字段
        node_attrs = {
            "description": t.get("description", ""),
            "sub_topics": safe_str(t.get("sub_topics", [])),
            "difficulty_level": summary.get("difficulty_level", None),
            "target_audience": summary.get("target_audience", ""),
            "estimated_learning_time": summary.get("estimated_learning_time", ""),
            "prerequisites_summary": summary.get("prerequisites_summary", ""),
            "key_sub_concepts": safe_str(summary.get("key_sub_concepts", [])),
            "common_misconceptions": safe_str(summary.get("common_misconceptions", [])),
            "practical_applications": safe_str(summary.get("practical_applications", []))
        }

        # 全部做一次safe，避免类型问题
        node_attrs = {k: safe_str(v) for k, v in node_attrs.items()}
        G.add_node(t["name"], **node_attrs)

    # 先修边
    for r in prereq:
        a = (r.get("from") or "").strip()
        b = (r.get("to") or "").strip()
        if a in G.nodes and b in G.nodes and a != b:
            G.add_edge(
                a, b,
                relation="prerequisite",
                reason=safe_str(r.get("reason", ""))
            )

    # 相似边
    for e in sim:
        a = (e.get("from") or "").strip()
        b = (e.get("to") or "").strip()
        s = float(e.get("similarity", 0.0))
        if a in G.nodes and b in G.nodes and a != b:
            # 若已有先修边，补充相似度属性；否则新建相似边
            if G.has_edge(a, b):
                G[a][b]["similarity"] = s
            else:
                G.add_edge(a, b, relation="similarity", score=s)

    return G


def sanitize_graph_for_gexf(G):
    """
    NetworkX GEXF安全图：确保所有属性都是标量字符串/数值
    """
    H = nx.DiGraph()

    for n, d in G.nodes(data=True):
        clean_d = {}
        for k, v in d.items():
            if isinstance(v, (str, int, float, bool)) or v is None:
                clean_d[k] = v
            else:
                clean_d[k] = safe_str(v)
        H.add_node(n, **clean_d)

    for u, v, d in G.edges(data=True):
        clean_d = {}
        for k, val in d.items():
            if isinstance(val, (str, int, float, bool)) or val is None:
                clean_d[k] = val
            else:
                clean_d[k] = safe_str(val)
        H.add_edge(u, v, **clean_d)

    return H


def visualize_prerequisite(G, out_png="kg_prerequisite.png"):
    H = nx.DiGraph()
    for u, v, d in G.edges(data=True):
        if d.get("relation") == "prerequisite":
            H.add_edge(u, v)

    if H.number_of_edges() == 0:
        print("[Info] 先修边为0，跳过先修图绘制。")
        return

    plt.figure(figsize=(12, 8))
    pos = nx.spring_layout(H, seed=42, k=1.2)
    nx.draw_networkx_nodes(H, pos, node_size=1800)
    nx.draw_networkx_edges(H, pos, arrows=True)
    nx.draw_networkx_labels(H, pos, font_size=9)
    plt.title("Prerequisite Graph")
    plt.axis("off")
    plt.tight_layout()
    plt.savefig(out_png, dpi=220)
    plt.close()
    print(f"[Saved] {out_png}")


def visualize_similarity(G, out_png="kg_similarity.png"):
    H = nx.Graph()
    for n in G.nodes:
        H.add_node(n)

    for u, v, d in G.edges(data=True):
        if d.get("relation") == "similarity":
            H.add_edge(u, v, weight=d.get("score", 0.0))

    if H.number_of_edges() == 0:
        print("[Info] 相似边为0，跳过相似图绘制。")
        return

    plt.figure(figsize=(12, 8))
    pos = nx.spring_layout(H, seed=42, k=1.2)
    widths = [1 + 4 * H[u][v]["weight"] for u, v in H.edges()]
    nx.draw_networkx_nodes(H, pos, node_size=1800)
    nx.draw_networkx_edges(H, pos, width=widths)
    nx.draw_networkx_labels(H, pos, font_size=9)
    plt.title("Similarity Graph")
    plt.axis("off")
    plt.tight_layout()
    plt.savefig(out_png, dpi=220)
    plt.close()
    print(f"[Saved] {out_png}")


def export_graph_json(G, out_json="knowledge_graph.json"):
    data = {
        "nodes": [],
        "edges": []
    }

    for n, d in G.nodes(data=True):
        node_obj = {"id": n}
        for k, v in d.items():
            node_obj[k] = safe_str(v)
        data["nodes"].append(node_obj)

    for u, v, d in G.edges(data=True):
        edge_obj = {"from": u, "to": v}
        for k, val in d.items():
            edge_obj[k] = safe_str(val)
        data["edges"].append(edge_obj)

    with open(out_json, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        json.dump(data, f, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)
    print(f"[Saved] {out_json}")


def main():
    print("=== Stage 4 v2: 合图与可视化（支持Stage3）===")

    p_topics = ask_path("请输入 stage3_topics_with_summary.json 路径")
    p_prereq = ask_path("请输入 stage2b_prerequisites.json 路径")
    p_sim = ask_path("请输入 stage2c_similarity_edges.json 路径")

    topics = extract_topics_with_summary(load_json(p_topics))
    prereq = extract_prereq(load_json(p_prereq))
    sim = extract_similarity(load_json(p_sim))

    print(f"[1] Topics: {len(topics)}")
    print(f"[2] Prerequisites: {len(prereq)}")
    print(f"[3] Similarity edges: {len(sim)}")

    G = build_graph(topics, prereq, sim)
    print(f"[4] Graph nodes={G.number_of_nodes()}, edges={G.number_of_edges()}")

    # GEXF安全导出
    G_safe = sanitize_graph_for_gexf(G)
    nx.write_gexf(G_safe, "knowledge_graph.gexf")
    print("[Saved] knowledge_graph.gexf")

    # JSON导出
    export_graph_json(G, "knowledge_graph.json")

    # 可视化
    visualize_prerequisite(G, "kg_prerequisite.png")
    visualize_similarity(G, "kg_similarity.png")

    print("\n✅ Stage 4 完成（支持Stage3 + 已修复GEXF导出）")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```

所以这不是算法失败，是“收尾 print 出错”。

AttributeError: 'str' object has no attribute 'get'
Stage2a failed
这又是为什么

Response:
你这个报错定位很清楚了：**LLM 返回的 `topics` 里混入了字符串，不是对象**，所以在 `dedup_topics()` 里 `t.get(...)` 崩掉。

---

## 为什么会这样
`dedup_topics()` 期望 `topics` 长这样：

```json
[
  {"name":"...", "description":"...", "sub_topics":[...]}
]
```

但这次某些元素是字符串，例如：

```json
["Markov Chains", "Reinforcement Learning", ...]
```

所以 `t` 是 `str`，没有 `.get()`，报：
`AttributeError: 'str' object has no attribute 'get'`

---

## 最小修复（直接改代码）
把你 `dedup_topics()` 替换成这个“容错版”：

```python
def dedup_topics(topics):
    out, seen = [], set()

    for t in topics:
        # 1) 如果是字符串，自动转成标准topic对象
        if isinstance(t, str):
            name = t.strip()
            if not name:
                continue
            t = {
                "name": name,
                "description": "",
                "sub_topics": []
            }

        # 2) 如果不是dict也不是str，跳过
        if not isinstance(t, dict):
            continue

        name = (t.get("name") or "").strip()
        if not name:
            continue

        k = name.lower()
        if k in seen:
            continue
        seen.add(k)

        desc = (t.get("description") or "").strip()
        subs = t.get("sub_topics", [])
        if not isinstance(subs, list):
            subs = []

        clean_subs, sub_seen = [], set()
        for s in subs:
            if isinstance(s, str):
                sn = s.strip()
                if not sn:
                    continue
                s = {"name": sn, "description": ""}
            if not isinstance(s, dict):
                continue

            sn = (s.get("name") or "").strip()
            if not sn:
                continue
            sk = sn.lower()
            if sk in sub_seen:
                continue
            sub_seen.add(sk)

            clean_subs.append({
                "name": sn,
                "description": (s.get("description") or "").strip()
            })

        out.append({
            "name": name,
            "description": desc,
            "sub_topics": clean_subs
        })

    return out
```

---

## 再加一道保险（在 LLM 输出后）
在 `llm_refine_topics()` 里拿到 `topics` 后加：

```python
if isinstance(topics, dict):
    topics = topics.get("topics", [])
if not isinstance(topics, list):
    topics = []
```

## 为什么另一些会失败？

你之前失败主要是两类：

Response:
# ## 这个网站展示的含义：你已经在看“最终知识图谱的节点和边”了 ✅

你贴出来的内容其实是 `knowledge_graph.json` 的两部分：

- **Sample Nodes**：图里的节点（知识点）
- **Sample Edges**：图里的关系边（先修/相似）

---

## ## 1) `Sample Nodes` 是什么？

每个节点代表一个知识概念（如 `Neural Networks`），并附带 Stage3 的教学属性：

- `difficulty_level`：难度等级（1-5）
- `estimated_learning_time`：预计学习时长
- `prerequisites_summary`：前置知识说明
- `common_misconceptions`：常见误解
- `practical_applications`：应用场景

这说明你的图不只是“关系图”，还是“可教学知识图”。

---

## ## 2) `Sample Edges` 是什么？

边表示两个知识点之间的关系：

- `relation = "prerequisite"`：先修关系（$$A \rightarrow B$$）
- `relation = "similarity"`：相似关系（语义接近）

比如你贴的：

- `Neural Networks -> Deep Learning`（先修）
- `Learning Models -> Practical Learning`（相似，score=0.7238）

---

## ## 3) 为什么看起来有些边怪怪的？

你贴的边里有：
- `Learning Models -> Learning Challenges`
- `Learning Models -> Practical Learning`

但你前面节点示例里没看到 `Learning Challenges / Practical Learning`。  
这通常是因为你网站展示的是**全局合并图**（多文档累计），不是单个PDF局部图。

---

## ## 4) 一句话总结你这个页面

> 这是“多文档共建知识库”的可视化数据视图：  
> 节点展示知识点及学习属性，边展示先修与相似关系，用于路径规划和补救推荐。

---

- 按文档来源过滤节点/边
- 理论节点和应用节点分色
- 先修边/相似边分开开关显示（导师看起来会更清楚）。

## ## 新建 `app.py`（完整可运行）

```python
import os
import json
from pathlib import Path
import streamlit as st
import pandas as pd
import networkx as nx
import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
from pyvis.network import Network

st.set_page_config(page_title="KG Demo", layout="wide")
st.title("📚 Knowledge Graph Demo (with Visual Graphs)")

# ====== 路径配置（按你本地项目改）======
BASE_DIR = Path(r"D:\ic\master project\project_code\KG_construction")
KG_JSON = BASE_DIR / "knowledge_graph.json"   # 你的stage4输出
PREREQ_PNG = BASE_DIR / "kg_prerequisite.png"
SIM_PNG = BASE_DIR / "kg_similarity.png"

# ====== 工具函数 ======
def load_kg_json(path: Path):
    if not path.exists():
        return {"nodes": [], "edges": []}
    with open(path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        return json.load(f)

def filter_edges(edges, show_prereq, show_sim):
    out = []
    for e in edges:
        r = e.get("relation", "")
        if r == "prerequisite" and show_prereq:
            out.append(e)
        elif r == "similarity" and show_sim:
            out.append(e)
    return out

def build_nx_graph(nodes, edges):
    G = nx.DiGraph()
    for n in nodes:
        nid = n.get("id")
        if nid:
            G.add_node(nid, **n)
    for e in edges:
        u, v = e.get("from"), e.get("to")
        if u and v:
            G.add_edge(u, v, **e)
    return G

def save_pyvis_html(nodes, edges, out_html: Path):
    net = Network(height="700px", width="100%", directed=True, bgcolor="#ffffff", font_color="black")
    net.barnes_hut()

    for n in nodes:
        nid = n.get("id")
        if not nid:
            continue
        diff = n.get("difficulty_level", "")
        title = f"""
        <b>{nid}</b><br>
        difficulty: {diff}<br>
        time: {n.get("estimated_learning_time","")}<br>
        audience: {n.get("target_audience","")}
        """
        net.add_node(
            nid,
            label=nid,
            title=title,
            color="#97C2FC",
            size=20
        )

    for e in edges:
        u, v = e.get("from"), e.get("to")
        if not u or not v:
            continue
        rel = e.get("relation", "")
        if rel == "prerequisite":
            color = "red"
            label = "prereq"
        else:
            color = "green"
            score = e.get("score", e.get("similarity", ""))
            label = f"sim:{score}"
        net.add_edge(u, v, color=color, label=label, arrows="to")

    net.save_graph(str(out_html))

def draw_matplotlib_graph(nodes, edges, title):
    G = build_nx_graph(nodes, edges)
    if G.number_of_nodes() == 0:
        st.info("No nodes to display.")
        return

    plt.figure(figsize=(10, 7))
    pos = nx.spring_layout(G, seed=42, k=1.0)

    nx.draw_networkx_nodes(G, pos, node_size=1300)
    nx.draw_networkx_labels(G, pos, font_size=8)
    nx.draw_networkx_edges(G, pos, arrows=True)
    plt.title(title)
    plt.axis("off")
    st.pyplot(plt.gcf())
    plt.close()

# ====== 读取数据 ======
kg = load_kg_json(KG_JSON)
nodes = kg.get("nodes", [])
edges = kg.get("edges", [])

# ====== 侧边栏筛选 ======
st.sidebar.header("🔧 Filters")
show_prereq = st.sidebar.checkbox("Show prerequisite edges", value=True)
show_sim = st.sidebar.checkbox("Show similarity edges", value=True)

# 可选：文档来源筛选（前提是你节点里有source_doc字段）
all_sources = sorted(list({n.get("source_doc","") for n in nodes if n.get("source_doc","")}))
selected_sources = st.sidebar.multiselect("Source docs (optional)", all_sources, default=all_sources if all_sources else [])

# source筛选
if selected_sources:
    nodes_filtered = [n for n in nodes if n.get("source_doc","") in selected_sources]
else:
    nodes_filtered = nodes

node_ids = set([n.get("id") for n in nodes_filtered if n.get("id")])
edges_filtered = [e for e in edges if e.get("from") in node_ids and e.get("to") in node_ids]
edges_filtered = filter_edges(edges_filtered, show_prereq, show_sim)

# ====== 顶部统计 ======
c1, c2, c3 = st.columns(3)
c1.metric("Nodes", len(nodes_filtered))
c2.metric("Edges (filtered)", len(edges_filtered))
c3.metric("All Edges", len(edges))

# ====== 图像展示（你要的“网页看图片”）======
st.subheader("🖼️ Graph Images")
img_col1, img_col2 = st.columns(2)

with img_col1:
    if PREREQ_PNG.exists():
        st.image(str(PREREQ_PNG), caption="Prerequisite Graph", use_container_width=True)
    else:
        st.warning("kg_prerequisite.png not found. Run stage4 first.")

with img_col2:
    if SIM_PNG.exists():
        st.image(str(SIM_PNG), caption="Similarity Graph", use_container_width=True)
    else:
        st.info("kg_similarity.png not found or no similarity edges.")

# ====== 交互图（PyVis）======
st.subheader("🌐 Interactive Graph")
html_path = BASE_DIR / "kg_interactive.html"
save_pyvis_html(nodes_filtered, edges_filtered, html_path)
with open(html_path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
    html_content = f.read()
st.components.v1.html(html_content, height=750, scrolling=True)

# ====== 备用：Matplotlib快速图 ======
with st.expander("Show quick matplotlib graph"):
    draw_matplotlib_graph(nodes_filtered, edges_filtered, "Filtered Knowledge Graph")

# ====== 表格展示 ======
st.subheader("📌 Nodes Table")
df_nodes = pd.DataFrame(nodes_filtered)
st.dataframe(df_nodes, use_container_width=True)

st.subheader("🔗 Edges Table")
df_edges = pd.DataFrame(edges_filtered)
st.dataframe(df_edges, use_container_width=True)

# ====== 下载 ======
st.subheader("⬇️ Download")
st.download_button(
    "Download filtered edges JSON",
    data=json.dumps(edges_filtered, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2),
    file_name="filtered_edges.json",
    mime="application/json"
)
st.download_button(
    "Download full KG JSON",
    data=json.dumps(kg, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2),
    file_name="knowledge_graph.json",
    mime="application/json"
)
```

## 怎么修（一次修好）

### 1) 找到这段（你报错附近）：
```python

    ...
```
如果它在函数外（顶格或缩进不在函数内），删掉/挪回函数里。

### 2) 用下面这个完整函数替换你当前的 `llm_refine_topics(...)`（最稳）：

```python
def llm_refine_topics(client: OpenAI, candidates: List[Dict[str, Any]]) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
    candidate_lines = []
    for c in candidates:
        candidate_lines.append(f"- {c['name']} (freq={c['frequency']}, score={c['avg_score']})")
    cand_text = "\n".join(candidate_lines)

    prompt = f"""
You are refining candidate concepts for a university Machine Learning course knowledge graph.

Candidate phrases:
{cand_text}

Task:
Convert candidates into a practical Topic/Sub-Topic structure for learning graph construction.

Hard constraints:
1) Output between 8 and 15 topics (unless candidates are truly insufficient).
2) Prefer specific technical concepts over overly broad labels.
3) Do NOT keep only generic topics such as "Machine Learning" unless needed as one parent topic.
4) Remove header/footer/institution noise.
5) Merge obvious duplicates/synonyms, but DO NOT over-merge distinct concepts.
6) Topic names must be short noun phrases (1-4 words preferred).
7) Each topic should have 1-3 sub_topics when possible.

Output JSON only:
{{
  "topics": [
    {{
      "name": "...",
      "description": "1 concise sentence",
      "sub_topics": [
        {{"name": "...", "description": "1 concise sentence"}}
      ]
    }}
  ]
}}
"""

    resp = client.chat.completions.create(
        model=MODEL_NAME,
        messages=[{"role": "user", "content": prompt}],
        response_format={"type": "json_object"},
        temperature=0
    )

    data = json.loads(resp.choices[0].message.content)
    topics = data.get("topics", [])

    # 关键：容错，防LLM返回漂移
    if isinstance(topics, dict):
        topics = topics.get("topics", [])
    if not isinstance(topics, list):
        topics = []

    # 若过少，再补一次
    if len(topics) < 8:
        repair_prompt = f"""
You previously returned too few topics ({len(topics)}).
Please expand to 8-15 topics using the same candidates below.
Keep technical granularity and avoid only broad labels.

Candidates:
{cand_text}

Return JSON with key "topics" only.
"""
        resp2 = client.chat.completions.create(
            model=MODEL_NAME,
            messages=[{"role": "user", "content": repair_prompt}],
            response_format={"type": "json_object"},
            temperature=0
        )
        data2 = json.loads(resp2.choices[0].message.content)
        topics2 = data2.get("topics", [])
        if isinstance(topics2, list) and len(topics2) > len(topics):
            topics = topics2

    return topics
```

## 最小补丁（直接贴到你原 app.py 底部）

```python
st.divider()
st.header("📊 Graph Visualization & Inspection")

from pathlib import Path
import json
import pandas as pd

PROJECT_DIR = Path(r"D:\ic\master project\project_code\KG_construction")
kg_json_path = PROJECT_DIR / "knowledge_graph.json"
pre_png = PROJECT_DIR / "kg_prerequisite.png"
sim_png = PROJECT_DIR / "kg_similarity.png"

# 图片展示
c1, c2 = st.columns(2)
with c1:
    if pre_png.exists():
        st.image(str(pre_png), caption="Prerequisite Graph", use_container_width=True)
    else:
        st.info("kg_prerequisite.png 暂未生成")

with c2:
    if sim_png.exists():
        st.image(str(sim_png), caption="Similarity Graph", use_container_width=True)
    else:
        st.info("kg_similarity.png 暂未生成或相似边为0")

# JSON表格展示
if kg_json_path.exists():
    with open(kg_json_path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        kg = json.load(f)

    nodes = kg.get("nodes", [])
    edges = kg.get("edges", [])

    st.subheader("Nodes")
    st.dataframe(pd.DataFrame(nodes), use_container_width=True)

    st.subheader("Edges")
    rel_filter = st.multiselect(
        "过滤关系类型",
        options=sorted(list(set([e.get("relation", "") for e in edges]))),
        default=sorted(list(set([e.get("relation", "") for e in edges])))
    )
    edges_filtered = [e for e in edges if e.get("relation", "") in rel_filter]
    st.dataframe(pd.DataFrame(edges_filtered), use_container_width=True)

    st.download_button(
        "⬇️ 下载 knowledge_graph.json",
        data=json.dumps(kg, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2),
        file_name="knowledge_graph.json",
        mime="application/json"
    )
else:
    st.warning("knowledge_graph.json 不存在，请先跑完 Stage4")
```

---

## ## 1) 中英双语展示（for supervisor demo）

**可以直接在 Streamlit 做 i18n 开关**，最简单是顶部加语言选择：

- `Language: 中文 / English`
- 所有标题、按钮、说明用字典映射显示

你不用重构，只要加一个 `t()` 函数：

```python
LANG = st.sidebar.selectbox("Language / 语言", ["中文", "English"])

I18N = {
    "中文": {"upload": "上传PDF", "run": "运行流水线", "nodes": "节点数"},
    "English": {"upload": "Upload PDFs", "run": "Run Pipeline", "nodes": "Nodes"}
}
def t(key): return I18N[LANG][key]
```

然后 UI 文案改成 `t("upload")` 这种方式。  
**明天展示够用了。**

---

## ## 2) 图谱是否累计？是否需要重复上传同一 PDF？

你现在应该做成 **累计型全局图谱（incremental KG）**，最佳实践：

- 每个 PDF 跑完产出一个 `doc_kg.json`
- 再 merge 到 `global_knowledge_graph.json`
- 记录 `processed_files.json`（文件名 + hash + 时间 + 节点边数量）

### 回答你问题：
- **不需要重复上传同一个文件**（除非你想重跑）
- 你应该在网页看到“历史上传记录”和“全局累计统计”
- 可以加“Force Rebuild”按钮用于强制重算

---

## ## 3) Stage3 的 difficulty 体现在哪里？跑通后应该看到什么？

你上传的 `stage3_topics_with_summary.json` 说明 Stage3 已跑。  
`difficulty_level` 的体现有 3 层：

1. **节点属性里有 `difficulty_level`（1-5）**  
2. 网页表格能看到每个 topic 的难度、学习时间  
3. 图上可按难度上色（例如 1=绿，5=红）

### 跑通后的“可见效果”应该是：
- 一个 topic 节点不仅有名字，还有：
  - 难度
  - 学习时长
  - 常见误解
  - 实践应用
- 这就是从“结构图”升级为“教学知识图”的标志

---

## ## 4) 后续是否要迁移到前后端分离？

**短期：不用，Streamlit 足够你验证和演示。**

## ## C) 新增双语字典与工具函数（放在工具函数区）

```python
# ===== i18n =====
I18N = {
    "中文": {
        "title": "知识图谱构建平台（批量上传 + 累计知识库）",
        "upload": "上传一个或多个 PDF",
        "run": "运行流水线",
        "stats": "全局统计",
        "history": "上传历史",
        "nodes": "节点数",
        "edges": "边数",
        "lang": "语言 / Language",
        "skip": "已处理，跳过（同名+同哈希）",
        "done": "处理完成",
    },
    "English": {
        "title": "KG Builder (Batch Upload + Incremental Knowledge Base)",
        "upload": "Upload one or more PDFs",
        "run": "Run Pipeline",
        "stats": "Global Stats",
        "history": "Upload History",
        "nodes": "Nodes",
        "edges": "Edges",
        "lang": "Language / 语言",
        "skip": "Already processed, skipped (same name+hash)",
        "done": "Done",
    }
}

def load_json_safe(path, default):
    if not path.exists():
        return default
    try:
        with open(path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            return json.load(f)
    except Exception:
        return default

def save_json_safe(path, data):
    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    with open(path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        json.dump(data, f, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)

def file_sha256(file_bytes: bytes) -> str:
    return hashlib.sha256(file_bytes).hexdigest()
```

## ## E) 修改上传区：加入去重/历史记录/累计逻辑（核心补丁）

把你原来 `if run_btn:` 的处理循环替换为下面逻辑（保留你各 stage 调用函数）：

```python
history = load_json_safe(HISTORY_JSON, [])
processed = load_json_safe(PROCESSED_JSON, {})  # key: filename, value: sha256

if run_btn:
    if not uploaded_files:
        st.warning("请先上传至少一个PDF。" if lang == "中文" else "Please upload at least one PDF.")
    else:
        for up in uploaded_files:
            raw = up.read()
            digest = file_sha256(raw)
            filename = up.name

            # 去重：同名+同hash则跳过
            if processed.get(filename) == digest:
                st.info(f"{filename}: {T['skip']}")
                history.append({
                    "time": datetime.now().strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S"),
                    "file_name": filename,
                    "sha256": digest,
                    "status": "skipped_same_file"
                })
                continue

            doc_name = Path(filename).stem
            doc_dir = RUN_DIR / doc_name
            doc_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

            pdf_path = doc_dir / filename
            with open(pdf_path, "wb") as f:
                f.write(raw)

            st.subheader(f"Processing: {filename}")
            log_box = st.empty()

            # ===== 你原来的 Stage1~4 调用保留 =====
            # 下面是示例变量名，请和你现有脚本输出保持一致
            stage1_out = doc_dir / "stage1_chunks.json"
            code, log = run_script_with_inputs(STAGE1_SCRIPT, [str(pdf_path), str(stage1_out)], cwd=PROJECT_DIR)
            log_box.text(log[-3000:])
            if code != 0:
                history.append({
                    "time": datetime.now().strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S"),
                    "file_name": filename,
                    "sha256": digest,
                    "status": "failed_stage1"
                })
                st.error("Stage1 failed")
                continue

            stage2a_out = doc_dir / "stage2a_topics_hybrid.json"
            code, log = run_script_with_inputs(STAGE2A_SCRIPT, [str(stage1_out), str(stage2a_out)], cwd=PROJECT_DIR)
            log_box.text(log[-3000:])
            if code != 0:
                history.append({
                    "time": datetime.now().strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S"),
                    "file_name": filename,
                    "sha256": digest,
                    "status": "failed_stage2a"
                })
                st.error("Stage2a failed")
                continue

            stage2b_out = doc_dir / "stage2b_prerequisites.json"
            code, log = run_script_with_inputs(STAGE2B_SCRIPT, [str(stage2a_out), str(stage2b_out)], cwd=PROJECT_DIR)
            log_box.text(log[-3000:])
            if code != 0:
                history.append({
                    "time": datetime.now().strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S"),
                    "file_name": filename,
                    "sha256": digest,
                    "status": "failed_stage2b"
                })
                st.error("Stage2b failed")
                continue

            stage2c_out = doc_dir / "stage2c_similarity_edges.json"
            code, log = run_script_with_inputs(STAGE2C_SCRIPT, [str(stage2a_out), str(stage2c_out)], cwd=PROJECT_DIR)
            log_box.text(log[-3000:])
            if code != 0:
                history.append({
                    "time": datetime.now().strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S"),
                    "file_name": filename,
                    "sha256": digest,
                    "status": "failed_stage2c"
                })
                st.error("Stage2c failed")
                continue

            stage3_out = doc_dir / "stage3_topics_with_summary.json"
            code, log = run_script_with_inputs(STAGE3_SCRIPT, [str(stage2a_out), str(stage3_out)], cwd=PROJECT_DIR)
            log_box.text(log[-3000:])
            if code != 0:
                history.append({
                    "time": datetime.now().strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S"),
                    "file_name": filename,
                    "sha256": digest,
                    "status": "failed_stage3"
                })
                st.error("Stage3 failed")
                continue

            code, log = run_script_with_inputs(STAGE4_SCRIPT, [str(stage3_out), str(stage2b_out), str(stage2c_out)], cwd=doc_dir)
            log_box.text(log[-3000:])
            if code != 0:
                history.append({
                    "time": datetime.now().strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S"),
                    "file_name": filename,
                    "sha256": digest,
                    "status": "failed_stage4"
                })
                st.error("Stage4 failed")
                continue

            # merge global
            doc_kg_json = doc_dir / "knowledge_graph.json"
            merged = merge_global_kg(GLOBAL_KG_JSON, doc_kg_json)

            processed[filename] = digest
            history.append({
                "time": datetime.now().strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S"),
                "file_name": filename,
                "sha256": digest,
                "status": "done",
                "nodes_after_merge": len(merged.get("nodes", [])),
                "edges_after_merge": len(merged.get("edges", []))
            })

            st.success(f"{filename}: {T['done']} | Nodes={len(merged.get('nodes', []))}, Edges={len(merged.get('edges', []))}")

        save_json_safe(PROCESSED_JSON, processed)
        save_json_safe(HISTORY_JSON, history)
```

## ## F) 新增“历史记录 + 全局统计 + 难度展示”区（放在页面底部）

```python
st.divider()
st.header(T["stats"])

global_kg = load_json_safe(GLOBAL_KG_JSON, {"nodes": [], "edges": []})
st.metric(T["nodes"], len(global_kg.get("nodes", [])))
st.metric(T["edges"], len(global_kg.get("edges", [])))

# difficulty分布
nodes = global_kg.get("nodes", [])
diff_vals = []
for n in nodes:
    d = n.get("difficulty_level", None)
    if isinstance(d, int):
        diff_vals.append(d)

if diff_vals:
    df_diff = pd.DataFrame({"difficulty_level": diff_vals})
    st.subheader("Difficulty Distribution / 难度分布")
    st.bar_chart(df_diff["difficulty_level"].value_counts().sort_index())

st.divider()
st.header(T["history"])
history = load_json_safe(HISTORY_JSON, [])
if history:
    st.dataframe(pd.DataFrame(history[::-1]), use_container_width=True)
else:
    st.info("暂无历史记录" if lang == "中文" else "No history yet.")
```

## ## G) 你关心的行为说明（关键）

- **累计图谱**：是的，`global_knowledge_graph.json` 会持续追加合并  
- **重复上传同一文件**：同名+同内容哈希会自动跳过  
- **历史记录**：`upload_history.json` 会记录成功/失败/跳过  
- **difficulty体现**：在节点属性中，页面会显示“难度分布柱状图”

def load_json_safe(path, default):

def save_json_safe(path, data):
    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    with open(path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        json.dump(data, f, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)

def file_sha256(file_bytes: bytes) -> str:
    return hashlib.sha256(file_bytes).hexdigest()

def run_script_with_inputs(script_path: Path, inputs: list[str], cwd: Path):
    """
    用stdin把“交互输入”喂给你现有脚本
    """
    if not script_path.exists():
        return 1, f"[Error] Script not found: {script_path}"

    input_text = "\n".join(inputs) + "\n"
    try:
        p = subprocess.run(
            [str(PYTHON_EXE), str(script_path)],
            input=input_text,
            text=True,
            capture_output=True,
            cwd=str(cwd)
        )
        log = f"--- STDOUT ---\n{p.stdout}\n\n--- STDERR ---\n{p.stderr}"
        return p.returncode, log
    except Exception as e:
        return 1, f"Subprocess error: {e}"


def read_json_safe(path: Path):
    if not path.exists():
        return None
    try:
        with open(path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            return json.load(f)
    except Exception:
        return None


def merge_global_kg(global_json_path: Path, doc_kg_json_path: Path):
    """
    简单合并全局图（按节点id去重，边三元组去重）
    """
    g = read_json_safe(global_json_path)
    d = read_json_safe(doc_kg_json_path)

    if d is None:
        return {"nodes": [], "edges": []}

    if g is None:
        with open(global_json_path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            json.dump(d, f, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)
        return d

    node_map = {n["id"]: n for n in g.get("nodes", [])}
    for n in d.get("nodes", []):
        node_map[n["id"]] = n

    edge_set = set()
    edges = []
    for e in g.get("edges", []) + d.get("edges", []):
        key = (e.get("from"), e.get("to"), e.get("relation"))
        if key not in edge_set:
            edge_set.add(key)
            edges.append(e)

    merged = {"nodes": list(node_map.values()), "edges": edges}
    with open(global_json_path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        json.dump(merged, f, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)
    return merged

        st.image(str(sim_png), caption="Similarity Graph", use_container_width=True)
    else:
        st.info("kg_similarity.png 暂未生成或相似边为0")

# JSON表格展示
if kg_json_path.exists():
    with open(kg_json_path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        kg = json.load(f)

    nodes = kg.get("nodes", [])
    edges = kg.get("edges", [])

    st.subheader("Nodes")
    st.dataframe(pd.DataFrame(nodes), use_container_width=True)

    st.subheader("Edges")
    rel_filter = st.multiselect(
        "过滤关系类型",
        options=sorted(list(set([e.get("relation", "") for e in edges]))),
        default=sorted(list(set([e.get("relation", "") for e in edges])))
    )
    edges_filtered = [e for e in edges if e.get("relation", "") in rel_filter]
    st.dataframe(pd.DataFrame(edges_filtered), use_container_width=True)

    st.download_button(
        "⬇️ 下载 knowledge_graph.json",
        data=json.dumps(kg, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2),
        file_name="knowledge_graph.json",
        mime="application/json"
    )
else:
    st.warning("knowledge_graph.json 不存在，请先跑完 Stage4")

st.divider()
st.header(T["stats"])

global_kg = load_json_safe(GLOBAL_KG_JSON, {"nodes": [], "edges": []})
st.metric(T["nodes"], len(global_kg.get("nodes", [])))
st.metric(T["edges"], len(global_kg.get("edges", [])))

# difficulty分布
nodes = global_kg.get("nodes", [])
diff_vals = []
for n in nodes:
    d = n.get("difficulty_level", None)
    if isinstance(d, int):
        diff_vals.append(d)

if diff_vals:
    df_diff = pd.DataFrame({"difficulty_level": diff_vals})
    st.subheader("Difficulty Distribution / 难度分布")
    st.bar_chart(df_diff["difficulty_level"].value_counts().sort_index())

st.divider()
st.header(T["history"])
history = load_json_safe(HISTORY_JSON, [])
if history:
    st.dataframe(pd.DataFrame(history[::-1]), use_container_width=True)
else:
    st.info("暂无历史记录" if lang == "中文" else "No history yet.")

```python
import os
import json
import subprocess
import hashlib
from datetime import datetime
from pathlib import Path

import streamlit as st
import pandas as pd

# 必须是第一个 Streamlit 调用
st.set_page_config(page_title="KG Builder Demo", layout="wide")

# ========= 项目路径 =========
PROJECT_DIR = Path(r"D:\ic\master project\project_code\KG_construction")
PYTHON_EXE = PROJECT_DIR / ".venv" / "Scripts" / "python.exe"

# ========= 脚本路径（按你当前文件名） =========
STAGE1_SCRIPT = PROJECT_DIR / "stage1_adaptive_chunking.py"
STAGE2A_SCRIPT = PROJECT_DIR / "stage2a_hybrid_keybert_llm.py"
STAGE2B_SCRIPT = PROJECT_DIR / "stage2b_prerequisites_hybrid.py"
STAGE2C_SCRIPT = PROJECT_DIR / "stage2c_similarity.py"
STAGE3_SCRIPT = PROJECT_DIR / "stage3_node_summary_hybrid.py"
STAGE4_SCRIPT = PROJECT_DIR / "stage4_build_and_visualize_kg_v2.py"  # 建议用v2

# ========= 数据目录 =========
DATA_DIR = PROJECT_DIR / "web_data"
RUN_DIR = DATA_DIR / "runs"
GLOBAL_DIR = DATA_DIR / "global"

HISTORY_JSON = GLOBAL_DIR / "upload_history.json"
PROCESSED_JSON = GLOBAL_DIR / "processed_files.json"
GLOBAL_KG_JSON = GLOBAL_DIR / "global_knowledge_graph.json"

for d in [RUN_DIR, GLOBAL_DIR]:
    d.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

st.title(T["title"])

# ========= 工具函数 =========
def load_json_safe(path: Path, default):
    if not path.exists():
        return default
    try:
        with open(path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            return json.load(f)
    except Exception:
        return default


def save_json_safe(path: Path, data):
    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    with open(path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        json.dump(data, f, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)


def file_sha256(file_bytes: bytes) -> str:
    return hashlib.sha256(file_bytes).hexdigest()


def run_script_with_inputs(script_path: Path, inputs: list[str], cwd: Path):
    if not script_path.exists():
        return 1, f"[Error] Script not found: {script_path}"

    input_text = "\n".join(inputs) + "\n"
    try:
        p = subprocess.run(
            [str(PYTHON_EXE), str(script_path)],
            input=input_text,
            text=True,
            capture_output=True,
            cwd=str(cwd)
        )
        log = f"--- STDOUT ---\n{p.stdout}\n\n--- STDERR ---\n{p.stderr}"
        return p.returncode, log
    except Exception as e:
        return 1, f"Subprocess error: {e}"


def merge_global_kg(global_json_path: Path, doc_kg_json_path: Path):
    g = load_json_safe(global_json_path, {"nodes": [], "edges": []})
    d = load_json_safe(doc_kg_json_path, None)

    if d is None:
        return g

    node_map = {n["id"]: n for n in g.get("nodes", []) if "id" in n}
    for n in d.get("nodes", []):
        nid = n.get("id")
        if nid:
            node_map[nid] = n

    edge_set = set()
    edges = []
    for e in g.get("edges", []) + d.get("edges", []):
        key = (e.get("from"), e.get("to"), e.get("relation"))
        if key not in edge_set:
            edge_set.add(key)
            edges.append(e)

    merged = {"nodes": list(node_map.values()), "edges": edges}
    save_json_safe(global_json_path, merged)
    return merged


# ========= UI =========
uploaded_files = st.file_uploader(
    T["upload"],
    type=["pdf"],
    accept_multiple_files=True
)

col1, col2 = st.columns([1, 1])
run_btn = col1.button(f"🚀 {T['run']}")
show_global_btn = col2.button(f"📊 {T['show_stats']}")

history = load_json_safe(HISTORY_JSON, [])
processed = load_json_safe(PROCESSED_JSON, {})  # filename -> sha256

if run_btn:
    if not uploaded_files:
        st.warning(T["warn_upload"])
    else:
        for up in uploaded_files:
            raw = up.read()
            digest = file_sha256(raw)
            filename = up.name

            if processed.get(filename) == digest:
                st.info(f"{filename}: {T['skip']}")
                history.append({
                    "time": datetime.now().strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S"),
                    "file_name": filename,
                    "sha256": digest,
                    "status": "skipped_same_file"
                })
                continue

            doc_name = Path(filename).stem
            doc_dir = RUN_DIR / doc_name
            doc_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

            pdf_path = doc_dir / filename
            with open(pdf_path, "wb") as f:
                f.write(raw)

            st.subheader(f"{T['processing']}: {filename}")
            log_box = st.empty()

            # Stage1
            stage1_out = doc_dir / "stage1_chunks.json"
            code, log = run_script_with_inputs(STAGE1_SCRIPT, [str(pdf_path), str(stage1_out)], cwd=PROJECT_DIR)
            log_box.text(log[-4000:])
            if code != 0:
                history.append({
                    "time": datetime.now().strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S"),
                    "file_name": filename,
                    "sha256": digest,
                    "status": "failed_stage1"
                })
                st.error("Stage1 failed")
                continue

            # Stage2a
            stage2a_out = doc_dir / "stage2a_topics_hybrid.json"
            code, log = run_script_with_inputs(STAGE2A_SCRIPT, [str(stage1_out), str(stage2a_out)], cwd=PROJECT_DIR)
            log_box.text(log[-4000:])
            if code != 0:
                history.append({
                    "time": datetime.now().strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S"),
                    "file_name": filename,
                    "sha256": digest,
                    "status": "failed_stage2a"
                })
                st.error("Stage2a failed")
                continue

            # Stage2b
            stage2b_out = doc_dir / "stage2b_prerequisites.json"
            code, log = run_script_with_inputs(STAGE2B_SCRIPT, [str(stage2a_out), str(stage2b_out)], cwd=PROJECT_DIR)
            log_box.text(log[-4000:])
            if code != 0:
                history.append({
                    "time": datetime.now().strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S"),
                    "file_name": filename,
                    "sha256": digest,
                    "status": "failed_stage2b"
                })
                st.error("Stage2b failed")
                continue

            # Stage2c
            stage2c_out = doc_dir / "stage2c_similarity_edges.json"
            code, log = run_script_with_inputs(STAGE2C_SCRIPT, [str(stage2a_out), str(stage2c_out)], cwd=PROJECT_DIR)
            log_box.text(log[-4000:])
            if code != 0:
                history.append({
                    "time": datetime.now().strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S"),
                    "file_name": filename,
                    "sha256": digest,
                    "status": "failed_stage2c"
                })
                st.error("Stage2c failed")
                continue

            # Stage3
            stage3_out = doc_dir / "stage3_topics_with_summary.json"
            code, log = run_script_with_inputs(STAGE3_SCRIPT, [str(stage2a_out), str(stage3_out)], cwd=PROJECT_DIR)
            log_box.text(log[-4000:])
            if code != 0:
                history.append({
                    "time": datetime.now().strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S"),
                    "file_name": filename,
                    "sha256": digest,
                    "status": "failed_stage3"
                })
                st.error("Stage3 failed")
                continue

            # Stage4 (在doc_dir下产出该文档图)
            code, log = run_script_with_inputs(
                STAGE4_SCRIPT,
                [str(stage3_out), str(stage2b_out), str(stage2c_out)],
                cwd=doc_dir
            )
            log_box.text(log[-4000:])
            if code != 0:
                history.append({
                    "time": datetime.now().strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S"),
                    "file_name": filename,
                    "sha256": digest,
                    "status": "failed_stage4"
                })
                st.error("Stage4 failed")
                continue

            # merge global
            doc_kg_json = doc_dir / "knowledge_graph.json"
            merged = merge_global_kg(GLOBAL_KG_JSON, doc_kg_json)

            processed[filename] = digest
            history.append({
                "time": datetime.now().strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S"),
                "file_name": filename,
                "sha256": digest,
                "status": "done",
                "nodes_after_merge": len(merged.get("nodes", [])),
                "edges_after_merge": len(merged.get("edges", [])),
                "doc_dir": str(doc_dir)
            })

            st.success(f"{filename}: {T['done']} | {T['nodes']}={len(merged.get('nodes', []))}, {T['edges']}={len(merged.get('edges', []))}")

        save_json_safe(PROCESSED_JSON, processed)
        save_json_safe(HISTORY_JSON, history)

# ========= 全局统计 =========
if show_global_btn:
    g = load_json_safe(GLOBAL_KG_JSON, {"nodes": [], "edges": []})
    if len(g.get("nodes", [])) == 0 and len(g.get("edges", [])) == 0:
        st.info(T["global_empty"])
    else:
        st.metric(T["nodes"], len(g.get("nodes", [])))
        st.metric(T["edges"], len(g.get("edges", [])))

        st.subheader(T["sample_nodes"])

            data=json.dumps(g, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2),
            file_name="global_knowledge_graph.json",
            mime="application/json"
        )

st.divider()
st.header(T["viz"])

# 显示“最近一次成功文档”的图片

for r in reversed(history):

if latest_done:

    c1, c2 = st.columns(2)

    with c2:
        if sim_png.exists():

        else:
            st.info(T["sim_img_missing"])
else:
    st.info(T["global_empty"])

# 用全局图谱做表格
kg = load_json_safe(GLOBAL_KG_JSON, {"nodes": [], "edges": []})
nodes = kg.get("nodes", [])
edges = kg.get("edges", [])

st.subheader(T["nodes_table"])
st.dataframe(pd.DataFrame(nodes), use_container_width=True)

st.subheader(T["edges_table"])
rel_options = sorted(list(set([e.get("relation", "") for e in edges if e.get("relation", "")])))

st.dataframe(pd.DataFrame(edges_filtered), use_container_width=True)

    f"⬇️ {T['download_filtered']}",
    data=json.dumps(edges_filtered, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2),
    file_name="filtered_edges.json",
    mime="application/json"
)

# 难度分布（Stage3价值可视化）
diff_vals = []
for n in nodes:
    d = n.get("difficulty_level", None)
    if isinstance(d, int):
        diff_vals.append(d)

if diff_vals:
    st.subheader(T["diff_dist"])
    df_diff = pd.DataFrame({"difficulty_level": diff_vals})
    st.bar_chart(df_diff["difficulty_level"].value_counts().sort_index())

st.divider()
st.header(T["history"])
history_df = load_json_safe(HISTORY_JSON, [])
if history_df:
    st.dataframe(pd.DataFrame(history_df[::-1]), use_container_width=True)
else:
    st.info(T["no_history"])
```

[06/17/2026 06:02:43 +00:00] MI, YU:
哈希是什么

Response:
## **哈希（Hash）是什么？**

**哈希**就是把任意文件内容通过一个算法，变成一串固定长度的“指纹字符串”。

比如你代码里用的是 `SHA-256`，同一个文件内容会得到同一个哈希值；  
哪怕文件只改一个字，哈希值都会完全变掉。

---

## **在你这个项目里，哈希的作用**

你现在用它来做**去重**：

- 上传 PDF 时先算哈希
- 如果“文件名 + 哈希”跟以前一样，说明内容没变  
  → 可以直接跳过，不重复跑 pipeline
- 如果哈希变了  
  → 说明内容变了（即使同名），就重新处理

---

## **一句话理解**

哈希就是文件的“内容身份证”。  
用它你就能判断：这是新文件，还是以前处理过的同一个内容。

报错了[Error] Script not found: D:\ic\master project\project_code\KG_construction\stage4_build_and_visualize_kg_v2.py

# ## 这个报错很简单：`app.py` 里配置的 Stage4 文件名不存在 ✅

你现在写的是：

```python
STAGE4_SCRIPT = PROJECT_DIR / "stage4_build_and_visualize_kg_v2.py"
```

```python
STAGE4_SCRIPT = PROJECT_DIR / "stage4_build_and_visualize_kg.py"
```

`stage4_build_and_visualize_kg_v2.py`

```python
for p in [STAGE1_SCRIPT, STAGE2A_SCRIPT, STAGE2B_SCRIPT, STAGE2C_SCRIPT, STAGE3_SCRIPT, STAGE4_SCRIPT]:
```

Response:
你这个问题非常好，分三部分给你结论：

## 1) 为什么 Stage1 会“有时失败，不稳定”？
**不是算法不稳定，主要是 Windows 终端编码问题。**  
你的报错是：

`UnicodeEncodeError: 'gbk' codec can't encode character '\xa9'`

意思是：脚本在打印预览文本时，遇到了 `©` 这类字符，GBK 终端打印不了，导致程序退出。  
而实际上你前面已经 `[Saved] stage1_chunks.json` 了，核心处理已完成。

### 立刻修复（推荐）
在 `stage1_adaptive_chunking.py` 里把预览打印改成安全打印，或直接关掉预览打印：

```python
# 原来
# print(snippet + ("..." if len(chunks[i]) > 220 else ""))

# 改成
safe_snippet = snippet.encode("gbk", errors="ignore").decode("gbk", errors="ignore")
print(safe_snippet + ("..." if len(chunks[i]) > 220 else ""))
```

并把所有 emoji 去掉（如 ✅），改成 `[OK]`。

---

## 2) 为什么节点表里有的没有 `description`？
正常现象，常见原因：

- Stage2a 某些 topic 由短语候选直接转来，LLM没补全描述
- 回退分支（KeyBERT/规则）可能只保留 `name`
- 合并全局图时，不同文档同名节点被覆盖，后写入版本可能 description 为空

### 建议
在合图前做兜底：

```python
description = t.get("description", "").strip()
if not description:
    description = f"Auto-generated topic: {t['name']}"
```

---

## 3) 难度分布一共有几级？
你设计里是 **1 到 5 级**。  
也就是：

- 1：非常基础
- 2：基础
- 3：中等
- 4：较难
- 5：高级

如果页面里某些节点没有难度，通常是：
- Stage3没给到该节点
- 或合并时该节点来自旧数据（无summary）

---

如果你愿意，我下一步可以给你一个**一次性“稳定补丁包”**（Stage1防编码崩溃 + 合图description兜底 + difficulty缺失填默认值），你直接贴进去就能稳定跑批量文档。

# ## 好的，给你一个“一次性稳定补丁包”✅  
目标：解决你现在的 3 个痛点：

1. Stage1 编码报错（GBK/©/emoji）
2. 合图时 `description` 为空
3. `difficulty_level` 缺失导致分布不完整

# ## 补丁 1：`stage1_adaptive_chunking.py` 防编码崩溃

## **A. 在文件顶部加这个函数**

```python
def safe_print_text(s: str):
    """
    Windows GBK 终端安全打印，避免 UnicodeEncodeError
    """
    try:
        print(s)
    except UnicodeEncodeError:
        s2 = s.encode("gbk", errors="ignore").decode("gbk", errors="ignore")
        print(s2)
```

## **B. 把预览输出改成安全打印**

把你现在这段：

```python
print(f"\n[Chunk {i+1}] words={len(chunks[i].split())}")
print(snippet + ("..." if len(chunks[i]) > 220 else ""))
```

替换成：

```python
safe_print_text(f"\n[Chunk {i+1}] words={len(chunks[i].split())}")
safe_print_text(snippet + ("..." if len(chunks[i]) > 220 else ""))
```

## **C. 去掉所有 emoji 输出**
例如：

```python
print("\n✅ Stage1 完成，可进入 Stage2")
```

改成：

```python
print("\n[OK] Stage1 完成，可进入 Stage2")
```

---

# ## 补丁 2：`stage4_build_and_visualize_kg_v2.py` 节点属性兜底

在构图节点处，确保 description 和 difficulty 都有值。

把节点属性构建改成下面模式（核心字段）：

```python
summary = t.get("summary", {})
if not isinstance(summary, dict):
    summary = {}

desc = (t.get("description") or "").strip()
if not desc:
    desc = f"Auto-generated topic: {t['name']}"

difficulty = summary.get("difficulty_level", None)
if not isinstance(difficulty, int) or difficulty < 1 or difficulty > 5:
    difficulty = 3  # 默认中等
```

并写入节点时：

```python
G.add_node(
    t["name"],
    description=desc,
    difficulty_level=difficulty,
    target_audience=summary.get("target_audience", "Undergraduate"),
    estimated_learning_time=summary.get("estimated_learning_time", "1-2 hours"),
    prerequisites_summary=summary.get("prerequisites_summary", ""),
    key_sub_concepts=json.dumps(summary.get("key_sub_concepts", []), ensure_ascii=False),
    common_misconceptions=json.dumps(summary.get("common_misconceptions", []), ensure_ascii=False),
    practical_applications=json.dumps(summary.get("practical_applications", []), ensure_ascii=False),
)
```

---

# ## 补丁 3：Streamlit 页面难度统计稳健化

你现在 difficulty 统计只接受 int，建议容错字符串数字。

把这段：

```python
if isinstance(d, int):
    diff_vals.append(d)
```

替换成：

```python
if isinstance(d, int):
    diff_vals.append(d)
elif isinstance(d, str) and d.isdigit():
    dv = int(d)
    if 1 <= dv <= 5:
        diff_vals.append(dv)
```

在 `run_script_with_inputs(...)` 里让子进程使用 UTF-8 环境，进一步降低编码问题：

```python
env = os.environ.copy()
env["PYTHONIOENCODING"] = "utf-8"

p = subprocess.run(
    [str(PYTHON_EXE), str(script_path)],
    input=input_text,
    text=True,
    capture_output=True,
    cwd=str(cwd),
    env=env
)
```

[06/17/2026 09:33:48 +00:00] MI, YU:
我的文档类型有哪些来着？我上传一个论文咋被识别成lecture note了

Response:
你现在的文档类型（在你 `stage1_adaptive_chunking.py` 里）是这三类：

1. **slides**
2. **lecture_notes**
3. **paper_book**

---

你问“论文为什么被识别成 lecture_notes”——很常见，原因是你现在用的是**启发式规则**，不是分类模型。  
它通常根据这些特征猜：

- 行长短不短（短行多像 slides/notes）
- 是否含教学词（`definition`, `example`, `exercise`, `theorem`）
- 是否有 lecture/week/content 等词

如果论文里也有这些词（尤其是教程型论文、survey、带大量小标题），就可能被误判成 `lecture_notes`。

---

## 怎么修（最实用）

### 方案 A：允许手动覆盖（推荐，最快）
在 Stage1 加一个输入：

- `auto`（自动）
- `slides`
- `lecture_notes`
- `paper_book`

如果用户指定了，就不用自动识别。

### 方案 B：改判定阈值
把 `lecture_notes` 判定条件调严格，比如必须同时出现多个教学词再判为 notes。

### 方案 C：论文优先规则
如果出现论文常见模式（`abstract`, `introduction`, `related work`, `references`），优先判成 `paper_book`。

---

一句话：你现在不是“错”，而是启发式分类天然会有边界误判。  
**工程上最稳的是“自动识别 + 手动覆盖开关”**。如果你要，我可以下一条直接给你这段可粘贴代码。

[06/17/2026 09:35:35 +00:00] MI, YU:
这三种类型策略区别很大吗？识别错误会发生什么

Response:
# ## 区别有，但**不是天差地别**；错分会影响质量，不会让流程必然崩

你现在这三类策略主要差在 **分块粒度和边界方式**：

- **slides**：更偏短块、段落/页面窗口，抗噪声强  
- **lecture_notes**：中等粒度，段落+句子混合  
- **paper_book**：更依赖语义边界（如 TextTiling），块可更长

---

## ## 识别错误会发生什么？

### 1) **最常见：质量下降**
- 论文误判成 notes/slides：块可能过碎，topic更散
- slides 误判成 paper：块可能过大，噪声混进同一块

### 2) **关系边会受影响**
- 2a topic抽取更噪或过泛
- 2b 先修关系可能变少/方向不稳
- 2c 相似边可能变少（或全0）

### 3) **通常不会导致程序直接失败**
真正导致失败的往往是编码、JSON格式漂移、路径问题，不是类型错分本身。

---

## ## 实操建议（你现在最该做）

- 保留自动识别，但加一个**手动覆盖选项**：`auto / slides / lecture_notes / paper_book`
- 如果你知道是论文，就直接强制 `paper_book`
- 这是最稳、最快提升质量的方式

---

一句话：**错分是“效果问题”，不是“可运行性问题”**。你现在先跑通，后面再精调识别器就行。

## **English Version (for supervisor update)**

### **1. Weekly Objective**
The main objective this week was to **run an end-to-end KG construction pipeline from PDF documents** and prepare a demonstrable prototype.

---

### **2. Completed Technical Work**

#### **Stage 1: Document Chunking (Done)**
- Implemented an **adaptive chunking pipeline**:
  - PDF text extraction and cleaning
  - Document-type detection (`slides / lecture_notes / paper_book`)
  - Type-specific chunking strategy
- Output: `stage1_chunks.json`
- Issue fixed:
  - Windows GBK encoding errors during preview printing (special characters)
  - Confirmed as output encoding issue, not algorithmic failure

#### **Stage 2a: Topic Extraction (Done)**
- Upgraded from pure KeyBERT to a **hybrid approach**:
  - KeyBERT for candidate recall
  - GPT-4o-mini for structured refinement (Topic/Sub-topic)
  - Local fallback when API quota is unavailable
- Output: `stage2a_topics_hybrid.json`
- Quality improvement:
  - Reduced over-generalization (e.g., only 2–3 broad topics)
  - Achieved more usable topic granularity (e.g., 9 topics)

#### **Stage 2b: Prerequisite Extraction (Done)**
- Implemented prerequisite relation extraction (LLM-first + rule fallback)
- Output: `stage2b_prerequisites.json`
- Used for learning-path planning (Planning Agent)

#### **Stage 2c: Similarity Edges (Done)**
- Used Sentence-BERT cosine similarity for semantic edges
- Output: `stage2c_similarity_edges.json`
- Supports threshold tuning (high threshold may produce zero edges)

#### **Stage 3: Node Summarization (Done)**
- Added structured learning metadata per topic:
  - difficulty level, estimated learning time, misconceptions, applications
- Output: `stage3_topics_with_summary.json`
- This turns the graph from a structural KG into an instructional KG

#### **Stage 4: Graph Integration & Visualization (Done)**
- Successfully merged:
  - topic nodes (with Stage 3 metadata)
  - prerequisite + similarity edges
- Outputs:
  - `knowledge_graph.gexf`
  - `knowledge_graph.json`
  - `kg_prerequisite.png`
  - `kg_similarity.png` (if similarity edges exist)
- Fixed GEXF export errors caused by complex attribute types

---

### **3. Platform/Demo Interface Progress**
- Built a Streamlit demo interface supporting:
  - batch PDF upload
  - automatic Stage1–Stage4 execution
  - upload history
  - incremental global KG merge
  - bilingual UI (Chinese/English)
  - node/edge table + image display

---

### **4. Major Engineering Issues Solved**
- Interpreter mismatch (`Anaconda` vs `.venv`) → standardized environment
- OpenAI quota/rate-limit issues → fallback mechanism added
- LLM output schema drift (string vs dict) → robust parsing added
- Windows encoding instability → safe output handling implemented

  PDF → chunking → topics → relations → summaries → graph export/visualization
- Supports incremental multi-document knowledge base construction
- Provides a solid base for future Graph-RAG integration

```mermaid
flowchart TD

    C4 --> F[Stage 3: Node Summary]

    F1 --> F2[difficulty/time/misconception/application]

    D3 --> G[Stage 4: Graph Integration & Visualization]
```

Response:
你这个报错非常明确：在 `build_graph()` 里用了 `desc`，但这个变量没有先定义。

---

## 为什么会这样
你大概率是把我给的“兜底逻辑”贴了一半，像这样：

```python
G.add_node(
    t["name"],
    description=desc,   # 这里用了 desc
    ...
)
```

但前面没有：

```python
desc = ...
```

---

## 直接修复（可复制）

在 `build_graph()` 的 `for t in topics:` 循环里，**先定义 `desc` 和 `difficulty`**，再 `add_node`：

```python
for t in topics:
    summary = t.get("summary", {})
    if not isinstance(summary, dict):
        summary = {}

    desc = (t.get("description") or "").strip()
    if not desc:
        desc = f"Auto-generated topic: {t['name']}"

    difficulty = summary.get("difficulty_level", None)
    if not isinstance(difficulty, int) or difficulty < 1 or difficulty > 5:
        difficulty = 3

    G.add_node(
        t["name"],
        description=desc,
        difficulty_level=difficulty,
        target_audience=summary.get("target_audience", "Undergraduate"),
        estimated_learning_time=summary.get("estimated_learning_time", "1-2 hours"),
        prerequisites_summary=summary.get("prerequisites_summary", "")
    )
```

Response:
下面给你一份**Stage4 最终版（可直接运行）**，已处理你遇到的关键问题：  
- 支持 `stage3_topics_with_summary.json`
- 兼容 `stage2b_prerequisites.json` / `stage2c_similarity_edges.json`
- 自动兜底 `description` / `difficulty_level`
- 修复 GEXF 导出类型问题（list/dict 自动转字符串）
- 避免 Windows 控制台编码导致崩溃（不用 emoji）

---

## `stage4_build_and_visualize_kg_v2.py`

```python
import os
import json
import networkx as nx
import matplotlib.pyplot as plt


def safe_print(msg: str):
    """Windows GBK 控制台安全打印"""
    try:
        print(msg)
    except UnicodeEncodeError:
        print(msg.encode("gbk", errors="ignore").decode("gbk", errors="ignore"))


def ask_path(prompt: str) -> str:
    p = input(prompt + "\n> ").strip().strip('"').strip("'")
    return p


def load_json(path: str):
    if not os.path.exists(path):
        raise FileNotFoundError(f"File not found: {path}")
    with open(path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        return json.load(f)


def safe_str(v):
    """GEXF 仅稳定支持标量，复杂对象转JSON字符串"""
    if isinstance(v, (str, int, float, bool)) or v is None:
        return v
    return json.dumps(v, ensure_ascii=False)


def extract_topics(data):
    """
    支持:
    1) {"topics":[...]}
    2) [...]
    """
    if isinstance(data, dict):
        topics = data.get("topics", [])
    elif isinstance(data, list):
        topics = data
    else:
        topics = []

    out = []
    for t in topics:
        if not isinstance(t, dict):
            continue
        name = (t.get("name") or "").strip()
        if not name:
            continue

        summary = t.get("summary", {})
        if not isinstance(summary, dict):
            summary = {}

        # description 兜底
        desc = (t.get("description") or "").strip()
        if not desc:
            desc = f"Auto-generated topic: {name}"

        # difficulty 兜底（1~5）
        difficulty = summary.get("difficulty_level", None)
        if isinstance(difficulty, str) and difficulty.isdigit():
            difficulty = int(difficulty)
        if not isinstance(difficulty, int) or not (1 <= difficulty <= 5):
            difficulty = 3

        out.append({
            "name": name,
            "description": desc,
            "sub_topics": t.get("sub_topics", []),
            "summary": {
                "difficulty_level": difficulty,
                "target_audience": summary.get("target_audience", "Undergraduate"),
                "estimated_learning_time": summary.get("estimated_learning_time", "1-2 hours"),
                "prerequisites_summary": summary.get("prerequisites_summary", ""),
                "key_sub_concepts": summary.get("key_sub_concepts", []),
                "common_misconceptions": summary.get("common_misconceptions", []),
                "practical_applications": summary.get("practical_applications", [])
            }
        })
    return out


def extract_prerequisites(data):
    """
    支持:
    1) {"prerequisites":[...]}
    2) [...]
    """
    if isinstance(data, dict):
        rels = data.get("prerequisites", [])
    elif isinstance(data, list):
        rels = data
    else:
        rels = []
    return rels if isinstance(rels, list) else []


def extract_similarity(data):
    """
    支持:
    1) {"similarity_edges":[...]}
    2) [...]
    """
    if isinstance(data, dict):
        rels = data.get("similarity_edges", [])
    elif isinstance(data, list):
        rels = data
    else:
        rels = []
    return rels if isinstance(rels, list) else []


def build_graph(topics, prereq, sim):
    G = nx.DiGraph()

    # 节点
    for t in topics:
        s = t["summary"]
        G.add_node(
            t["name"],
            description=safe_str(t["description"]),
            sub_topics=safe_str(t.get("sub_topics", [])),
            difficulty_level=safe_str(s.get("difficulty_level")),
            target_audience=safe_str(s.get("target_audience")),
            estimated_learning_time=safe_str(s.get("estimated_learning_time")),
            prerequisites_summary=safe_str(s.get("prerequisites_summary")),
            key_sub_concepts=safe_str(s.get("key_sub_concepts", [])),
            common_misconceptions=safe_str(s.get("common_misconceptions", [])),
            practical_applications=safe_str(s.get("practical_applications", [])),
        )

    # 先修边
    for r in prereq:
        if not isinstance(r, dict):
            continue
        a = (r.get("from") or "").strip()
        b = (r.get("to") or "").strip()
        if a in G.nodes and b in G.nodes and a != b:
            G.add_edge(
                a, b,
                relation="prerequisite",
                reason=safe_str(r.get("reason", ""))
            )

    # 相似边
    for e in sim:
        if not isinstance(e, dict):
            continue
        a = (e.get("from") or "").strip()
        b = (e.get("to") or "").strip()
        if a in G.nodes and b in G.nodes and a != b:
            score = e.get("similarity", e.get("score", 0.0))
            try:
                score = float(score)
            except Exception:
                score = 0.0

            # 若已有边则附加相似度
            if G.has_edge(a, b):
                G[a][b]["similarity"] = score
            else:
                G.add_edge(a, b, relation="similarity", score=score)

    return G


def sanitize_graph_for_gexf(G):
    """确保所有属性都是GEXF可写类型"""
    H = nx.DiGraph()

    for n, d in G.nodes(data=True):
        nd = {}
        for k, v in d.items():
            nd[k] = safe_str(v)
        H.add_node(n, **nd)

    for u, v, d in G.edges(data=True):
        ed = {}
        for k, val in d.items():
            ed[k] = safe_str(val)
        H.add_edge(u, v, **ed)

    return H


def export_json_graph(G, out_json="knowledge_graph.json"):
    data = {"nodes": [], "edges": []}

    for n, d in G.nodes(data=True):
        node_obj = {"id": n}
        for k, v in d.items():
            node_obj[k] = safe_str(v)
        data["nodes"].append(node_obj)

    for u, v, d in G.edges(data=True):
        edge_obj = {"from": u, "to": v}
        for k, val in d.items():
            edge_obj[k] = safe_str(val)
        data["edges"].append(edge_obj)

    with open(out_json, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        json.dump(data, f, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)

    safe_print(f"[Saved] {out_json}")


def visualize_prereq(G, out_png="kg_prerequisite.png"):
    H = nx.DiGraph()
    for u, v, d in G.edges(data=True):
        if d.get("relation") == "prerequisite":
            H.add_edge(u, v)

    if H.number_of_edges() == 0:
        safe_print("[Info] No prerequisite edges. Skip prerequisite plot.")
        return

    plt.figure(figsize=(12, 8))
    pos = nx.spring_layout(H, seed=42, k=1.2)
    nx.draw_networkx_nodes(H, pos, node_size=1800)
    nx.draw_networkx_edges(H, pos, arrows=True)
    nx.draw_networkx_labels(H, pos, font_size=9)
    plt.title("Prerequisite Graph")
    plt.axis("off")
    plt.tight_layout()
    plt.savefig(out_png, dpi=220)
    plt.close()
    safe_print(f"[Saved] {out_png}")


def visualize_similarity(G, out_png="kg_similarity.png"):
    H = nx.Graph()
    for n in G.nodes:
        H.add_node(n)

    for u, v, d in G.edges(data=True):
        if d.get("relation") == "similarity":
            w = d.get("score", d.get("similarity", 0.0))
            try:
                w = float(w)
            except Exception:
                w = 0.0
            H.add_edge(u, v, weight=w)

    if H.number_of_edges() == 0:
        safe_print("[Info] No similarity edges. Skip similarity plot.")
        return

    plt.figure(figsize=(12, 8))
    pos = nx.spring_layout(H, seed=42, k=1.2)
    widths = [1 + 4 * H[u][v]["weight"] for u, v in H.edges()]
    nx.draw_networkx_nodes(H, pos, node_size=1800)
    nx.draw_networkx_edges(H, pos, width=widths)
    nx.draw_networkx_labels(H, pos, font_size=9)
    plt.title("Similarity Graph")
    plt.axis("off")
    plt.tight_layout()
    plt.savefig(out_png, dpi=220)
    plt.close()
    safe_print(f"[Saved] {out_png}")


def main():
    safe_print("=== Stage 4 v2: KG Merge & Visualization ===")

    p_topics = ask_path("Input stage3_topics_with_summary.json path")
    p_prereq = ask_path("Input stage2b_prerequisites.json path")
    p_sim = ask_path("Input stage2c_similarity_edges.json path")

    topics_data = load_json(p_topics)
    prereq_data = load_json(p_prereq)
    sim_data = load_json(p_sim)

    topics = extract_topics(topics_data)
    prereq = extract_prerequisites(prereq_data)
    sim = extract_similarity(sim_data)

    safe_print(f"[1] Topics: {len(topics)}")
    safe_print(f"[2] Prerequisites: {len(prereq)}")
    safe_print(f"[3] Similarity edges: {len(sim)}")

    G = build_graph(topics, prereq, sim)
    safe_print(f"[4] Graph nodes={G.number_of_nodes()}, edges={G.number_of_edges()}")

    # GEXF安全导出
    G_safe = sanitize_graph_for_gexf(G)
    nx.write_gexf(G_safe, "knowledge_graph.gexf")
    safe_print("[Saved] knowledge_graph.gexf")

    # JSON导出
    export_json_graph(G, "knowledge_graph.json")

    # 可视化
    visualize_prereq(G, "kg_prerequisite.png")
    visualize_similarity(G, "kg_similarity.png")

    safe_print("[OK] Stage4 completed.")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```


输入 3 个文件时，用你自己的输出文件（友好名）：

- `stage2b_prerequisites.json`
- `stage2c_similarity_edges.json`

如果你愿意，我下一步可以把你的 `app.py` 里 Stage4 脚本路径也一起改成这个 v2，避免再出现“找不到脚本/变量未定义”的问题。
